\displaystyle\leq 6\left(\left\|\left(\bm{I}_{n_{1}}-\bm{U}_{1}\bm{U}_{1}^{\top}\right)\bm{X}_{1}^{\star}\right\|_{\rm F}^{2}+\left\|\left(\bm{I}_{n_{2}}-\bm{U}_{2}\bm{U}_{2}^{\top}\right)\bm{X}_{2}^{\star}\right\|_{\rm F}^{2}+\left\|\left(\bm{I}_{n_{3}}-\bm{U}_{3}\bm{U}_{3}^{\top}\right)\bm{X}_{3}^{\star}\right\|_{\rm F}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+2\left\|\bm{\mathcal{E}}\times_{1}\mathcal{P}_{\bm{U}_{1}}\times_{2}\mathcal{P}_{\bm{U}_{2}}\times_{3}\mathcal{P}_{\bm{U}_{3}}\right\|_{\rm F}^{2}.

(53)

Recognizing that for any
1\leq\ell\leq k_{i}
, we have

\displaystyle\sigma_{\ell}\left(\mathcal{M}_{i}\left(\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\star}\right)\right)=\sigma_{\ell}\left(\bm{M}_{i}^{\star}\mathcal{M}_{i}\left(\bm{\mathcal{S}}^{\star}\right)\left(\bm{M}_{i+2}^{\star}\otimes\bm{M}_{i+1}^{\star}\right)^{\top}\right)\geq\prod_{i=1}^{3}\sigma_{k_{i}}\left(\bm{M}_{i}^{\star}\right)\sigma_{\ell}\left(\mathcal{M}_{i}\left(\bm{\mathcal{S}}^{\star}\right)\right)\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\eqref{ineq159}}}{{\geq}}\sqrt{\frac{\beta^{3}n_{1}n_{2}n_{3}}{k_{1}k_{2}k_{3}}}\sigma_{\ell}\left(\mathcal{M}_{i}\left(\bm{\mathcal{S}}^{\star}\right)\right)

and

\displaystyle\sigma_{\ell}\left(\mathcal{M}_{i}\left(\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\star}\right)\right)\leq\prod_{i=1}^{3}\left\|\bm{M}_{i}^{\star}\right\|\sigma_{\ell}\left(\mathcal{M}_{i}\left(\bm{\mathcal{S}}^{\star}\right)\right)\leq\sqrt{n_{1}n_{2}n_{3}}\sigma_{\ell}\left(\mathcal{M}_{i}\left(\bm{\mathcal{S}}^{\star}\right)\right).

In view of Theorem
6
, by choosing the numbers of iterations as in (
30a
) and (
30b
),
we have

\displaystyle\left\|\left(\bm{I}_{n_{1}}-\bm{U}_{1}\bm{U}_{1}^{\top}\right)\bm{X}_{1}^{\star}\right\|_{\rm F}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq n_{1}\left\|\left(\bm{I}_{n_{1}}-\bm{U}_{1}\bm{U}_{1}^{\top}\right)\bm{X}_{1}^{\star}\right\|_{2,\infty}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim n_{1}\cdot\frac{\mu_{1}k_{1}^{3}}{n_{1}}\left(k_{1}^{2}\sqrt{n_{1}}\omega_{\sf max}\log n+k_{1}\left(n_{1}n_{2}n_{3}\right)^{1/4}\omega_{\sf max}\log n\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\eqref{ineq:dimension_assumption}~\text{and}~\eqref{ineq157}}}{{\lesssim}}\frac{k^{6}}{\beta^{2}}\left(n_{1}n_{2}n_{3}\right)^{1/2}\omega_{\sf max}^{2}\log^{2}n

(54)

with probability at least
1-O(n^{-10})
.
Similarly, one has, with probability exceeding
1-O(n^{-10})
,

\displaystyle\left\|\left(\bm{I}_{n_{2}}-\bm{U}_{2}\bm{U}_{2}^{\top}\right)\bm{X}_{2}^{\star}\right\|_{\rm F}^{2}\lesssim\frac{k^{6}}{\beta^{2}}\left(n_{1}n_{2}n_{3}\right)^{1/2}\omega_{\sf max}^{2}\log^{2}n,

(55a)

\displaystyle\left\|\left(\bm{I}_{n_{2}}-\bm{U}_{2}\bm{U}_{2}^{\top}\right)\bm{X}_{2}^{\star}\right\|_{\rm F}^{2}\lesssim\frac{k^{6}}{\beta^{2}}\left(n_{1}n_{2}n_{3}\right)^{1/2}\omega_{\sf max}^{2}\log^{2}n.

(55b)

Moreover, we learn from Theorem
6
that with probability at least
1-O(n^{-10})
,

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{U}_{i}\right\|_{2,\infty}=\left\|\bm{U}_{i}\bm{U}_{i}^{\top}\right\|_{2,\infty}\leq\left\|\bm{U}_{i}\bm{U}_{i}^{\top}-\bm{U}^{\star}\bm{U}^{\star}\right\|_{2,\infty}+\left\|\bm{U}^{\star}\right\|_{2,\infty}\leq 2\sqrt{\frac{\mu_{i}k_{i}^{3}}{n_{i}}},~\quad~\forall i\in[3].

(56)

Applying Lemma
7
yields that with probability at least
1-O(n^{-10})
,

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{\mathcal{E}}\times_{1}\mathcal{P}_{\bm{U}_{1}}\times_{2}\mathcal{P}_{\bm{U}_{2}}\times_{3}\mathcal{P}_{\bm{U}_{3}}\right\|_{\rm F}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq k_{1}\left\|\bm{U}_{1}^{\top}\mathcal{M}_{1}\left(\bm{\mathcal{E}}\right)\left(\bm{U}_{3}\otimes\bm{U}_{2}\right)\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim kn\left(\mu_{1}k_{1}^{2}\right)\left(\mu_{2}k_{2}^{2}\right)\left(\mu_{3}k_{3}^{2}\right)k^{3}\omega_{\sf max}^{2}\log n

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\eqref{ineq157}}}{{\lesssim}}\frac{k^{13}}{\beta^{6}}n\omega_{\sf max}^{2}\log n.

(57)

Putting (
B.2
) - (
B.2
) together, we obtain

\displaystyle\big\|\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}-\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\star}\big\|_{\rm F}^{2}\lesssim\frac{k^{6}}{\beta^{2}}\left(n_{1}n_{2}n_{3}\right)^{1/2}\omega_{\sf max}^{2}\log^{2}n+\frac{k^{13}}{\beta^{6}}n\omega_{\sf max}^{2}\log n

(58)

with probability exceeding
1-O(n^{-10})
.

Step 3: deriving estimation accuracy of the center estimates.

We let

\displaystyle\widehat{\bm{\theta}}_{\ell}^{(i)}=\big(\bm{U}_{i+2}\otimes\bm{U}_{i+1}\big)\widehat{\bm{b}}_{\ell}^{(i)}\in\mathbb{R}^{n_{-i}},~\quad~\forall i\in[3],\ell\in[k_{i}],

(59)

and also define

\displaystyle\bm{\theta}_{\ell}^{(i)\star}=\left(\mathcal{M}_{i}\left(\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\star}\right)\right)_{j,:}^{\top}\in\mathbb{R}^{n_{-i}},~\quad~\forall i\in[3],\ell\in[k_{i}].

(60)

Here,
j\in[n_{i}]
is any index satisfying
z_{i,j}^{\star}=\ell
and the
\widehat{\bm{b}}_{\ell}^{(i)}
’s are the center estimates satisfying (
18a
). Recalling that
\widehat{\bm{B}}_{i}=\bm{U}_{i}\bm{U}_{i}^{\top}\mathcal{M}_{i}({\bm{\mathcal{Y}}})(\bm{U}_{i+2}\otimes\bm{U}_{i+1})
, we have

\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{i}\big(\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}\big)=\widehat{\bm{B}}_{i}\big(\bm{U}_{i+2}^{\top}\otimes\bm{U}_{i+1}^{\top}\big),~\quad~\forall i\in[3].

This allows one to show that

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{n_{i}}\left\|\big(\mathcal{M}_{i}\big(\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}\big)\big)_{j:}^{\top}-\widehat{\bm{\theta}}_{\widehat{z}_{i,j}}^{(i)}\right\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{j=1}^{n_{i}}\left\|\big(\bm{U}_{i+2}\otimes\bm{U}_{i+1}\big)\left(\big(\widehat{\bm{B}}_{i}\big)_{j:}^{\top}-\widehat{\bm{b}}^{(i)}_{\widehat{z}_{i,j}}\right)\right\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{j=1}^{n_{i}}\left\|\big(\widehat{\bm{B}}_{i}\big)_{j:}^{\top}-\widehat{\bm{b}}^{(i)}_{\widehat{z}_{i,j}}\right\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\eqref{ineq:k_means_approximate}}}{{\leq}}M\min_{\bm{b}_{1},\dots,\bm{b}_{k_{i}}\in\mathbb{R}^{r_{1}r_{2}r_{3}/r_{i}}\atop\bm{z}_{i}\in[k_{i}]^{n_{i}}}\sum_{j=1}^{n_{i}}\left\|\big(\widehat{\bm{B}}_{i}\big)_{j:}^{\top}-\bm{b}_{z_{i,j}}\right\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=M\min_{\bm{\theta}_{1},\dots,\bm{\theta}_{k_{i}}\in\mathbb{R}^{n_{-i}}\atop\bm{z}_{i}\in[k_{i}]^{n_{i}}}\sum_{j=1}^{n_{i}}\left\|\big(\mathcal{M}_{i}\big(\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}\big)\big)_{j:}^{\top}-\bm{\theta}_{z_{i,j}}\right\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq M\sum_{j=1}^{n_{i}}\left\|\big(\mathcal{M}_{i}\big(\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}\big)\big)_{j:}^{\top}-\bm{\theta}^{(i)\star}_{z_{i,j}^{\star}}\right\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=M\sum_{j=1}^{n_{i}}\left\|\big(\mathcal{M}_{i}\big(\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}\big)\big)_{j:}^{\top}-\left(\mathcal{M}_{i}\left(\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\star}\right)\right)_{j:}^{\top}\right\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=M\big\|\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}-\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\star}\big\|_{\rm F}^{2}.

(61)

Here, the fourth line makes use of
Han et al., 2022a
. As a result, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{n_{i}}\left\|\widehat{\bm{\theta}}_{\widehat{z}_{i,j}}^{(i)}-\bm{\theta}^{(i)\star}_{z_{i,j}^{\star}}\right\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2\left(\sum_{j=1}^{n_{i}}\left\|\big(\mathcal{M}_{i}\big(\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}\big)\big)_{j:}^{\top}-\widehat{\bm{\theta}}_{\widehat{z}_{i,j}}^{(i)}\right\|_{2}^{2}+\sum_{j=1}^{n_{i}}\left\|\big(\mathcal{M}_{i}\big(\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}\big)\big)_{j:}^{\top}-\bm{\theta}^{(i)\star}_{z_{i,j}^{\star}}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq 2\left(M\big\|\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}-\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\star}\big\|_{\rm F}^{2}+\big\|\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}-\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\star}\big\|_{\rm F}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq 4M\big\|\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}-\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\star}\big\|_{\rm F}^{2}.

(62)

Noting that
\bm{M}_{i}^{\star\top}\bm{M}_{i}^{\star}
is a diagonal matrix and the diagonal entries

\displaystyle\left(\bm{M}_{i}^{\star\top}\bm{M}_{i}^{\star}\right)_{\ell,\ell}=\sum_{j=1}^{n_{i}}\left(\bm{M}_{i}^{\star}\right)_{j,\ell}^{2}=\left\{j\in[n_{i}]:z_{i,j}^{\star}=\ell\right\}\geq\beta n_{i}/k_{i},

we have

\displaystyle\sigma_{k_{i}}\left(\bm{M}_{i}^{\star}\right)\geq\sqrt{\beta n_{i}/k_{i}},~\quad~\forall i\in[3].

(63)

As a consequence, for all
\ell_{1}\neq\ell_{2}\in[k_{i}]
, we can derive

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{\theta}_{\ell_{1}}^{(i)\star}-\bm{\theta}_{\ell_{2}}^{(i)\star}\right\|

\displaystyle=\big\|\left(\mathcal{M}_{i}\left(\bm{\mathcal{X}}\right)\right)_{j_{1},:}-\left(\mathcal{M}_{i}\left(\bm{\mathcal{X}}\right)\right)_{j_{2},:}\big\|

\displaystyle=\left\|\big(\left(\mathcal{M}_{i}\left(\bm{\mathcal{S}}\right)\right)_{\ell_{1},:}-\left(\mathcal{M}_{i}\left(\bm{\mathcal{S}}\right)\right)_{\ell_{2},:}\big)\left(\bm{M}_{i+2}^{\star}\otimes\bm{M}_{i+1}^{\star}\right)\right\|

\displaystyle\geq\big\|\left(\mathcal{M}_{i}\left(\bm{\mathcal{S}}\right)\right)_{\ell_{1},:}-\left(\mathcal{M}_{i}\left(\bm{\mathcal{S}}\right)\right)_{\ell_{2},:}\big\|\sigma_{k_{i+1}}\left(\bm{M}_{i+1}^{\star}\right)\sigma_{k_{i+2}}\left(\bm{M}_{i+2}^{\star}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\beta\sqrt{\frac{n_{-i}}{k_{-i}}}\Delta_{i},

(64)

where
j_{1}
(resp.
j_{2}
) is any index such that
z_{i,j_{1}}^{\star}=\ell_{1}
(resp.
z_{i,j_{2}}^{\star}=\ell_{2}
). We define

\displaystyle\mathcal{S}_{i}:=\left\{j\in[n_{i}]:\left\|\widehat{\bm{\theta}}_{\widehat{z}_{i,j}}^{(i)}-\bm{\theta}^{(i)\star}_{z_{i,j}^{\star}}\right\|_{2}\geq\frac{\beta}{2}\sqrt{\frac{n_{-i}}{k_{-i}}}\Delta_{i}\right\}.

(65)

In view of (
58
) and (
B.2
), with probability exceeding
1-O(n^{-10})
,

\displaystyle\left|\mathcal{S}_{i}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\sum_{j=1}^{n_{i}}\left\|\widehat{\bm{\theta}}_{\widehat{z}_{i,j}}^{(i)}-\bm{\theta}^{(i)\star}_{z_{i,j}^{\star}}\right\|_{2}^{2}}{\left(\frac{\beta}{2}\sqrt{\frac{n_{-i}}{k_{-i}}}\Delta_{i}\right)^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4M\cdot C_{6}\left(\frac{k^{6}}{\beta^{2}}\left(n_{1}n_{2}n_{3}\right)^{1/2}\omega_{\sf max}^{2}\log^{2}n+\frac{k^{13}}{\beta^{6}}n\omega_{\sf max}^{2}\log n\right)}{\left(\frac{\beta}{2}\sqrt{\frac{n_{-i}}{k_{-i}}}\Delta_{i}\right)^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\beta}{2}\frac{n_{i}}{k_{i}},

(66)

provided that

\Delta_{i}\geq C_{1}\sqrt{M}\left(\frac{k^{9/2}}{\beta^{5/2}}\left(n_{1}n_{2}n_{3}\right)^{-1/4}\omega_{\sf max}\log n+\frac{k^{8}}{\beta^{9/2}}\left(n_{1}n_{2}n_{3}/n\right)^{-1/2}\omega_{\sf max}\sqrt{\log n}\right).

For each
1\leq i\leq 3
and
\ell\in[k_{i}]
, denote by
\mathcal{N}_{i,\ell}
the following set:

\displaystyle\mathcal{N}_{i,\ell}:=\left\{j\in[n_{i}]:z_{i,j}^{\star}=\ell,j\in\mathcal{S}^{c}\right\}.

(67)

Then we can verify that with probability exceeding
1-O(n^{-10})
, the following two important properties of the
\mathcal{N}_{i,\ell}
’s hold:

1

. The
\mathcal{N}_{i,\ell}
’s are nonempty:

\displaystyle\left|\mathcal{N}_{i,\ell}\right|\geq\big|\big\{j\in[n_{i}]:z_{i,j}^{\star}=\ell\big\}\big|-\left|\mathcal{S}_{i}\right|\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\eqref{def:beta}~\text{and}~\eqref{ineq177}}}{{\geq}}\beta\frac{n_{i}}{k_{i}}-\frac{\beta}{2}\frac{n_{i}}{k_{i}}=\frac{\beta}{2}\frac{n_{i}}{k_{i}}>0.

(68)

2.

For any
i\in[3]
, the sets
\{\widehat{z}_{i,j}:j\in\mathcal{N}_{i,\ell}\},\ell\in[k_{i}]
are disjoint: for all
\ell_{1}\neq\ell_{2}\in[k_{i}],j_{1}\in\mathcal{N}_{i,\ell_{1}},j_{2}\in\mathcal{N}_{i,\ell_{2}}
,

\displaystyle\left\|\widehat{\bm{\theta}}^{(i)}_{\widehat{z}_{i,j_{1}}}-\widehat{\bm{\theta}}^{(i)}_{\widehat{z}_{i,j_{2}}}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\geq\left\|{\bm{\theta}}^{(i)\star}_{z_{i,j_{1}}^{\star}}-{\bm{\theta}}^{(i)\star}_{z_{i,j_{2}}^{\star}}\right\|_{2}-\left\|\widehat{\bm{\theta}}^{(i)}_{\widehat{z}_{i,j_{1}}}-{\bm{\theta}}^{(i)\star}_{z_{i,j_{1}}^{\star}}\right\|_{2}-\left\|\widehat{\bm{\theta}}^{(i)}_{\widehat{z}_{i,j_{2}}}-{\bm{\theta}}^{(i)\star}_{z_{i,j_{2}}^{\star}}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle=\left\|{\bm{\theta}}^{(i)\star}_{\ell_{1}}-{\bm{\theta}}^{(i)\star}_{\ell_{2}}\right\|_{2}-\left\|\widehat{\bm{\theta}}^{(i)}_{\widehat{z}_{i,j_{1}}}-{\bm{\theta}}^{(i)\star}_{z_{i,j_{1}}^{\star}}\right\|_{2}-\left\|\widehat{\bm{\theta}}^{(i)}_{\widehat{z}_{i,j_{2}}}-{\bm{\theta}}^{(i)\star}_{z_{i,j_{2}}^{\star}}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\eqref{ineq175}}}{{>}}\beta\sqrt{\frac{n_{-i}}{k_{-i}}}\Delta_{i}-\frac{\beta}{2}\sqrt{\frac{n_{-i}}{k_{-i}}}\Delta_{i}-\frac{\beta}{2}\sqrt{\frac{n_{-i}}{k_{-i}}}\Delta_{i}=0,

which implies
\widehat{z}_{i,j_{1}}\neq\widehat{z}_{i,j_{2}}
and further tells us
\{\widehat{z}_{i,j}:j\in\mathcal{N}_{i,\ell_{1}}\}\cap\{\widehat{z}_{i,j}:j\in\mathcal{N}_{i,\ell_{2}}\}=\emptyset
.

Therefore, with probability at least
1-O(n^{-10})
, for any
i\in[3]
, there exists a permutation
\phi_{i}:[k_{i}]\to[k_{i}]
such that

\displaystyle\widehat{z}_{i,j}=\phi_{i}\left(z_{i,j}^{\star}\right),~\quad~\forall j\in\mathcal{S}_{i}^{c},i\in[3].

(69)

In view of (
B.2
), with probability at least
1-O(n^{-10})
, one has

\displaystyle{\sf MCR}\left(\widehat{\bm{z}}_{i},\bm{z}_{i}^{\star}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{n_{i}}\left|\left\{j\in[n_{i}]:\widehat{z}_{i,j}\neq\phi_{i}\left(z_{i,j}^{\star}\right)\right\}\right|\leq\frac{1}{n_{i}}\left|\mathcal{S}_{i}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4M\cdot C_{6}\left(\frac{k^{6}}{\beta^{2}}\left(n_{1}n_{2}n_{3}\right)^{1/2}\omega_{\sf max}^{2}\log^{2}n+\frac{k^{13}}{\beta^{6}}n\omega_{\sf max}^{2}\log n\right)}{n_{i}\left(\frac{\beta}{2}\sqrt{\frac{n_{-i}}{k_{-i}}}\Delta_{i}\right)^{2}}

(70)

for all
i\in[3]
, and

\displaystyle\left\|\widehat{\bm{\theta}}_{\phi_{i}\left(\ell\right)}^{(i)}-\bm{\theta}^{(i)\star}_{\ell}\right\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\sum_{j=1}^{n_{i}}\left\|\widehat{\bm{\theta}}_{\widehat{z}_{i,j}}^{(i)}-\bm{\theta}^{(i)\star}_{z_{i,j}^{\star}}\right\|_{2}^{2}}{\left|\left\{j\in[n_{i}]:\widehat{z}_{i,j}=\phi_{i}\left(\ell\right),z_{i,j}^{\star}=\ell\right\}\right|}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\sum_{j=1}^{n_{i}}\left\|\widehat{\bm{\theta}}_{\widehat{z}_{i,j}}^{(i)}-\bm{\theta}^{(i)\star}_{z_{i,j}^{\star}}\right\|_{2}^{2}}{\left|\left\{j\in[n_{i}]:z_{i,j}^{\star}=\ell,j\in\mathcal{S}^{c}\right\}\right|}

\displaystyle=\frac{\sum_{j=1}^{n_{i}}\left\|\widehat{\bm{\theta}}_{\widehat{z}_{i,j}}^{(i)}-\bm{\theta}^{(i)\star}_{z_{i,j}^{\star}}\right\|_{2}^{2}}{\left|\mathcal{N}_{i,\ell}\right|}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4M\cdot C\left(\frac{k^{6}}{\beta^{2}}\left(n_{1}n_{2}n_{3}\right)^{1/2}\omega_{\sf max}^{2}\log^{2}n+\frac{k^{13}}{\beta^{6}}n\omega_{\sf max}^{2}\log n\right)}{\frac{\beta n_{i}}{2k_{i}}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C_{7}M\left(\frac{k^{7}}{\beta^{3}}\left(n_{1}n_{2}n_{3}\right)^{1/2}\omega_{\sf max}^{2}\log^{2}n+\frac{k^{14}}{\beta^{7}}n\omega_{\sf max}^{2}\log n\right)}{n_{i}}

(71)

for all
\ell\in[k_{i}]
,
provided that

\Delta_{i}\geq C_{1}\sqrt{M}\left(\frac{k^{9/2}}{\beta^{5/2}}\left(n_{1}n_{2}n_{3}\right)^{-1/4}\omega_{\sf max}\log n+\frac{k^{8}}{\beta^{9/2}}\left(n_{1}n_{2}n_{3}/n\right)^{-1/2}\omega_{\sf max}\sqrt{\log n}\right).

Here, the fourth line of (
B.2
) makes use of (
58
), (
B.2
) and (
68
).

Step 4: proving
\mathsf{MCR}\big(\widehat{\bm{z}}_{i},\bm{z}_{i}^{\star}\big)=0
.

Finally, we would like to show that with probability exceeding
1-O(n^{-10})
,
\widehat{\bm{z}}_{i}=\phi_{i}(\bm{z}_{i}^{\star})
for all
i\in[3]
. In view of Theorem
6
, Lemma
7
, (
48
) and (
56
), with probability at least
1-O(n^{-10})
,

\displaystyle\left\|\left(\bm{I}_{n_{i}}-\bm{U}_{i}\bm{U}_{i}^{\top}\right)\bm{X}_{i}^{\star}\right\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{\mu_{i}k_{i}^{3}}{n_{i}}}\left(k_{i}^{2}\sqrt{n_{1}}\omega_{\sf max}\log n+k_{i}\left(n_{1}n_{2}n_{3}\right)^{1/4}\omega_{\sf max}\log n\right)

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\eqref{ineq157}}}{{\lesssim}}\frac{k^{3}}{\beta}\frac{\left(n_{1}n_{2}n_{3}\right)^{1/4}}{n_{i}^{1/2}}\omega_{\sf max}\log n

(72)

and

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{U}_{i}\bm{U}_{i}^{\top}\bm{E}_{i}\left(\bm{U}_{i+2}\bm{U}_{i+2}^{\top}\otimes\bm{U}_{i+1}\bm{U}_{i+1}^{\top}\right)\right\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\bm{U}_{i}\right\|_{2,\infty}\left\|\bm{U}_{i}^{\top}\bm{E}_{i}\left(\bm{U}_{i+2}\otimes\bm{U}_{i+1}\right)\right\|

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\eqref{ineq:incoherence_estimate}~\text{and}~\text{Lemma}~\ref{lm:noise}}}{{\lesssim}}\sqrt{\frac{\mu_{i}k_{i}^{3}}{n_{i}}}\omega_{\sf max}\sqrt{n\left(\mu_{1}k_{1}^{2}\right)\left(\mu_{2}k_{2}^{2}\right)\left(\mu_{3}k_{3}^{2}\right)k^{3}\log n}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\eqref{ineq157}}}{{\lesssim}}\sqrt{\frac{k^{4}}{\beta^{2}n_{i}}}\frac{k^{6}}{\beta^{3}}\omega_{\sf max}\sqrt{n\log n}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{k^{8}}{\beta^{4}}\omega_{\sf max}\sqrt{\frac{n\log n}{n_{i}}}

(73)

holds for all
i\in[3]
.
Here,
\bm{X}_{i}^{\star}=\mathcal{M}_{i}(\bm{\mathcal{X}}^{\star})
and
\bm{E}_{i}=\mathcal{M}_{i}(\bm{\mathcal{E}})
. By virtue of (
18b
), we know that for any
i\in[3]
,

\displaystyle\left\{j\in[n_{1}]:\widehat{z}_{i,j}\neq\phi_{i}\left(z_{i,j}^{\star}\right)\right\}\subseteq\left\{j\in[n_{1}]:\exists\ell\in[k_{i}]~\text{s.t.}~\left\|\big(\widehat{\bm{B}}_{i}\big)_{j:}^{\top}-\widehat{\bm{b}}^{(i)}_{\ell}\right\|_{2}\leq\left\|\big(\widehat{\bm{B}}_{i}\big)_{j:}^{\top}-\widehat{\bm{b}}^{(i)}_{\phi_{i}(z_{i,j}^{\star})}\right\|_{2}\right\}.

(74)

For any fixed
\ell\neq\phi_{i}(z_{i,j}^{\star})\in[k_{i}]
, recalling that
\widehat{\bm{\theta}}_{\ell}^{(i)}=\big(\bm{U}_{i+2}\otimes\bm{U}_{i+1}\big)\widehat{\bm{b}}_{\ell}^{(i)}
, one has

\displaystyle\mathbbm{1}\left\{\left\|\big(\widehat{\bm{B}}_{i}\big)_{j:}^{\top}-\widehat{\bm{b}}^{(i)}_{\ell}\right\|_{2}\leq\left\|\big(\widehat{\bm{B}}_{i}\big)_{j:}^{\top}-\widehat{\bm{b}}^{(i)}_{\phi_{i}(z_{i,j}^{\star})}\right\|_{2}\right\}

\displaystyle\quad=\mathbbm{1}\left\{\left\|\big(\widehat{\bm{B}}_{i}\big)_{j:}^{\top}-\widehat{\bm{b}}^{(i)}_{\ell}\right\|_{2}+\left\|\big(\widehat{\bm{B}}_{i}\big)_{j:}^{\top}-\widehat{\bm{b}}^{(i)}_{\phi_{i}(z_{i,j}^{\star})}\right\|_{2}\leq 2\left\|\big(\widehat{\bm{B}}_{i}\big)_{j:}^{\top}-\widehat{\bm{b}}^{(i)}_{\phi_{i}(z_{i,j}^{\star})}\right\|_{2}\right\}

\displaystyle\quad\leq\mathbbm{1}\left\{\left\|\widehat{\bm{b}}^{(i)}_{\ell}-\widehat{\bm{b}}^{(i)}_{\phi_{i}(z_{i,j}^{\star})}\right\|_{2}\leq 2\left\|\big(\widehat{\bm{B}}_{i}\big)_{j:}^{\top}-\widehat{\bm{b}}^{(i)}_{\phi_{i}(z_{i,j}^{\star})}\right\|_{2}\right\}

\displaystyle\quad\leq\mathbbm{1}\left\{\left\|\widehat{\bm{\theta}}^{(i)}_{\ell}-\widehat{\bm{\theta}}^{(i)}_{\phi_{i}(z_{i,j}^{\star})}\right\|_{2}\leq 2\left\|\big(\widehat{\bm{B}}_{i}\big)_{j:}^{\top}-\widehat{\bm{b}}^{(i)}_{\phi_{i}(z_{i,j}^{\star})}\right\|_{2}\right\}

\displaystyle\quad=\mathbbm{1}\left\{\left\|\widehat{\bm{\theta}}^{(i)}_{\ell}-\widehat{\bm{\theta}}^{(i)}_{\phi_{i}(z_{i,j}^{\star})}\right\|_{2}\leq 2\left\|\left(\bm{U}_{i}\right)_{j,:}\bm{U}_{i}^{\top}\bm{Y}_{i}\left(\bm{U}_{i+2}\otimes\bm{U}_{i+1}\right)-\widehat{\bm{b}}^{(i)\top}_{\phi_{i}(z_{i,j}^{\star})}\right\|_{2}\right\},

(75)

where
\bm{Y}_{i}=\mathcal{M}_{i}(\bm{\mathcal{Y}})=\bm{X}_{i}^{\star}+\bm{E}_{i}
. Note that

\displaystyle\left\|\left(\bm{U}_{i}\right)_{j,:}\bm{U}_{i}^{\top}\bm{Y}_{i}\left(\bm{U}_{i+2}\otimes\bm{U}_{i+1}\right)-\widehat{\bm{b}}^{(i)\top}_{\phi_{i}(z_{i,j}^{\star})}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\quad\leq\left\|\left(\bm{U}_{i}\right)_{j,:}\bm{U}_{i}^{\top}\bm{E}_{i}\left(\bm{U}_{i+2}\otimes\bm{U}_{i+1}\right)\right\|_{2}+\left\|\left(\bm{U}_{i}\right)_{j,:}\bm{U}_{i}^{\top}\bm{X}_{i}^{\star}\left(\bm{U}_{i+2}\otimes\bm{U}_{i+1}\right)-\widehat{\bm{b}}^{(i)\top}_{\phi_{i}(z_{i,j}^{\star})}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\quad\leq\left\|\bm{U}_{i}\bm{U}_{i}^{\top}\bm{E}_{i}\left(\bm{U}_{i+2}\otimes\bm{U}_{i+1}\right)\right\|_{2,\infty}+\left\|\left(\bm{U}_{i}\right)_{j,:}\bm{U}_{i}^{\top}\bm{X}_{i}^{\star}\left(\bm{U}_{i+2}\otimes\bm{U}_{i+1}\right)-\widehat{\bm{\theta}}^{(i)\top}_{\phi_{i}(z_{i,j}^{\star})}\left(\bm{U}_{i+2}\otimes\bm{U}_{i+1}\right)\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\quad\leq\left\|\bm{U}_{i}\bm{U}_{i}^{\top}\bm{E}_{i}\left(\bm{U}_{i+2}\otimes\bm{U}_{i+1}\right)\right\|_{2,\infty}+\left\|\left(\bm{U}_{i}\right)_{j,:}\bm{U}_{i}^{\top}\bm{X}_{i}^{\star}-\widehat{\bm{\theta}}^{(i)\top}_{\phi_{i}(z_{i,j}^{\star})}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\quad\leq\left\|\bm{U}_{i}\bm{U}_{i}^{\top}\bm{E}_{i}\left(\bm{U}_{i+2}\otimes\bm{U}_{i+1}\right)\right\|_{2,\infty}+\left\|\left(\bm{U}_{i}\right)_{j,:}\bm{U}_{i}^{\top}\bm{X}_{i}^{\star}-\left(\bm{X}_{i}^{\star}\right)_{j,:}\right\|_{2}+\left\|\left(\bm{X}_{i}^{\star}\right)_{j,:}-\widehat{\bm{\theta}}^{(i)\top}_{\phi_{i}(z_{i,j}^{\star})}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\quad\leq\left\|\bm{U}_{i}\bm{U}_{i}^{\top}\bm{E}_{i}\left(\bm{U}_{i+2}\otimes\bm{U}_{i+1}\right)\right\|_{2,\infty}+\left\|\left(\bm{I}_{n_{i}}-\bm{U}_{i}\bm{U}_{i}^{\top}\right)\bm{X}_{i}^{\star}\right\|_{2,\infty}+\left\|\left(\bm{X}_{i}^{\star}\right)_{j,:}-\widehat{\bm{\theta}}^{(i)\top}_{\phi_{i}(z_{i,j}^{\star})}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\quad=\left\|\bm{U}_{i}\bm{U}_{i}^{\top}\bm{E}_{i}\left(\bm{U}_{i+2}\otimes\bm{U}_{i+1}\right)\right\|_{2,\infty}+\left\|\left(\bm{I}_{n_{i}}-\bm{U}_{i}\bm{U}_{i}^{\top}\right)\bm{X}_{i}^{\star}\right\|_{2,\infty}+\left\|\widehat{\bm{\theta}}^{(i)}_{\phi_{i}(z_{i,j}^{\star})}-\bm{\theta}^{(i)\star}_{z_{i,j}^{\star}}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\quad\leq\left\|\bm{U}_{i}\bm{U}_{i}^{\top}\bm{E}_{i}\left(\bm{U}_{i+2}\otimes\bm{U}_{i+1}\right)\right\|_{2,\infty}+\left\|\left(\bm{I}_{n_{i}}-\bm{U}_{i}\bm{U}_{i}^{\top}\right)\bm{X}_{i}^{\star}\right\|_{2,\infty}+\sup_{a\in[k_{i}]}\left\|\widehat{\bm{\theta}}^{(i)}_{\phi_{i}(a)}-\bm{\theta}^{(i)\star}_{a}\right\|_{2}

and

\displaystyle\left\|\widehat{\bm{\theta}}^{(i)}_{\ell}-\widehat{\bm{\theta}}^{(i)}_{\phi_{i}(z_{i,j}^{\star})}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\geq\left\|\bm{\theta}^{(i)\star}_{\phi_{i}^{-1}(\ell)}-\bm{\theta}^{(i)\star}_{z_{i,j}^{\star}}\right\|_{2}-\left\|\widehat{\bm{\theta}}^{(i)}_{\ell}-\bm{\theta}^{(i)\star}_{\phi_{i}^{-1}(\ell)}\right\|_{2}-\left\|\widehat{\bm{\theta}}^{(i)}_{\phi_{i}(z_{i,j}^{\star})}-\bm{\theta}^{(i)\star}_{z_{i,j}^{\star}}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\eqref{ineq175}}}{{\geq}}\beta\sqrt{\frac{n_{-i}}{k_{-i}}}\Delta_{i}-2\sup_{a\in[k_{i}]}\left\|\widehat{\bm{\theta}}^{(i)}_{\phi_{i}(a)}-\bm{\theta}^{(i)\star}_{a}\right\|_{2}.

Putting the previous two inequalities, (
B.2
), (
B.2
) and (
B.2
) together yields: with probability at least
1-O(n^{-10})
,

\displaystyle\left\|\widehat{\bm{\theta}}^{(i)}_{\ell}-\widehat{\bm{\theta}}^{(i)}_{\phi_{i}(z_{i,j}^{\star})}\right\|_{2}-2\left\|\left(\bm{U}_{i}\right)_{j,:}\bm{U}_{i}^{\top}\bm{Y}_{i}\left(\bm{U}_{i+2}\otimes\bm{U}_{i+1}\right)-\widehat{\bm{b}}^{(i)\top}_{\phi_{i}(z_{i,j}^{\star})}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\quad\geq\beta\sqrt{\frac{n_{-i}}{k_{-i}}}\Delta_{i}-2\sup_{a\in[k_{i}]}\left\|\widehat{\bm{\theta}}^{(i)}_{\phi_{i}(a)}-\bm{\theta}^{(i)\star}_{a}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\qquad-2\left(\left\|\bm{U}_{i}\bm{U}_{i}^{\top}\bm{E}_{i}\left(\bm{U}_{i+2}\otimes\bm{U}_{i+1}\right)\right\|_{2,\infty}+\left\|\left(\bm{I}_{n_{i}}-\bm{U}_{i}\bm{U}_{i}^{\top}\right)\bm{X}_{i}^{\star}\right\|_{2,\infty}+\sup_{a\in[k_{i}]}\left\|\widehat{\bm{\theta}}^{(i)}_{\phi_{i}(a)}-\bm{\theta}^{(i)\star}_{a}\right\|_{2}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\geq\beta\sqrt{\frac{n_{-i}}{k_{-i}}}\Delta_{i}-4\sqrt{\frac{C_{7}M\left(\frac{k^{7}}{\beta^{3}}\left(n_{1}n_{2}n_{3}\right)^{1/2}\omega_{\sf max}^{2}\log^{2}n+\frac{k^{14}}{\beta^{7}}n\omega_{\sf max}^{2}\log n\right)}{n_{i}}}

\displaystyle\qquad-C_{8}\frac{k^{3}}{\beta}\frac{\left(n_{1}n_{2}n_{3}\right)^{1/4}}{n_{i}^{1/2}}\omega_{\sf max}\log n-C_{8}\frac{k^{8}}{\beta^{4}}\omega_{\sf max}\sqrt{\frac{n\log n}{n_{i}}}

\displaystyle\quad>0

(76)

holds for all
\ell\in[k_{i}]
,
provided that

\Delta_{i}/\omega_{\sf max}\geq C_{1}\sqrt{M}\left(\frac{k^{9/2}}{\beta^{5/2}}\left(n_{1}n_{2}n_{3}\right)^{-1/4}\log n+\frac{k^{9}}{\beta^{5}}\left(n_{1}n_{2}n_{3}/n\right)^{-1/2}\sqrt{\log n}\right).

Combining (
74
), (
B.2
) and (
B.2
), we arrive at

\displaystyle\mathsf{MCR}\big(\widehat{\bm{z}}_{i},\bm{z}_{i}^{\star}\big)=0,~\quad~\forall i\in[3]

with probability exceeding
1-O(n^{-10})
.

Appendix C
Proof of Theorem
5

Part (a): proving
\mathcal{A}\neq\emptyset
.

Let

\displaystyle\overline{r}=\begin{cases}\max\mathcal{A},\quad&\text{if }\mathcal{A}\neq\emptyset;\\
0,\quad&\text{otherwise}.\end{cases},

(77)

We claim that

\displaystyle\sigma_{\overline{r}+1}^{\star}\leq 2C_{0}r\big[(m_{1}m_{2})^{1/4}+rm_{1}^{1/2}\big]\omega_{\sf max}\log m.

(78)

In fact, if
\sigma_{\overline{r}+1}^{\star}=0
, then (
78
) clearly holds. If
\sigma_{\overline{r}+1}^{\star}>0
, then we must have
\overline{r}<r
. Let

\displaystyle i=\min\left\{j:\overline{r}+1\leq j\leq r,\sigma_{j}^{\star}\geq\frac{4r}{4r-1}\sigma_{j+1}^{\star}\right\}.

Note that such
i
does exist as the largest
j\leq r
satisfying
\sigma_{j}^{\star}>0
must obey
\sigma_{j}^{\star}\geq\frac{4r}{4r-1}\sigma_{j+1}^{\star}
. The definition of
\overline{r}
immediately tells us that
\sigma_{i}^{\star}\leq C_{0}r[(m_{1}m_{2})^{1/4}+rm_{1}^{1/2}]\log m
and consequently one has

\displaystyle\sigma_{\overline{r}+1}^{\star}=\sigma_{i}^{\star}\prod_{j=\overline{r}+1}^{i-1}\frac{\sigma_{j}^{\star}}{\sigma_{j+1}^{\star}}\leq\sigma_{i}^{\star}\left(\frac{4r}{4r-1}\right)^{i-\overline{r}-1}\leq\sigma_{i}^{\star}\cdot\left(1+\frac{1}{3r}\right)^{r}\leq 2C_{0}r\big[(m_{1}m_{2})^{1/4}+rm_{1}^{1/2}\big]\omega_{\sf max}\log m.

The first inequality holds due to the definition of
i
. This combined with the assumption on
\sigma_{1}^{\star}
reveals that
\overline{r}\neq 0
, i.e.,
\mathcal{A}\neq\emptyset
and

\displaystyle\overline{r}=\max\mathcal{A}.

(79)

Part (b): proving (
39b
).

The rest of the proof is devoted to proving (
39b
). Letting

\displaystyle\bm{U}^{\star(1)}

\displaystyle=\left[\bm{u}_{1}^{\star},\dots,\bm{u}_{\overline{r}}^{\star}\right],~\qquad~\bm{\Sigma}^{\star(1)}={\sf diag}\left(\sigma_{1}^{\star},\dots,\sigma_{\overline{r}}^{\star}\right),~\qquad~\bm{V}^{\star(1)}=\left[\bm{v}_{1}^{\star},\dots,\bm{v}_{\overline{r}}^{\star}\right],

(80a)

\displaystyle\bm{U}^{\star(2)}

\displaystyle=\left[\bm{u}_{\overline{r}+1}^{\star},\dots,\bm{u}_{r}^{\star}\right],~\qquad~\bm{\Sigma}^{\star(2)}={\sf diag}\left(\sigma_{\overline{r}+1}^{\star},\dots,\sigma_{r}^{\star}\right),~\qquad~\bm{V}^{\star(2)}=\left[\bm{v}_{\overline{r}+1}^{\star},\dots,\bm{v}_{r}^{\star}\right],

(80b)

we can derive

\displaystyle\bm{U}^{\star}=\big[\bm{U}^{\star(1)}\ \bm{U}^{\star(2)}\big],~\qquad~\bm{V}^{\star}=\big[\bm{V}^{\star(1)}\ \bm{V}^{\star(2)}\big],~\qquad~\bm{\Sigma}^{\star}=\begin{bmatrix}\bm{\Sigma}^{\star(1)}&\bm{0}\\
\bm{0}&\bm{\Sigma}^{\star(2)}\end{bmatrix}.

(81)

Let the SVD of
\bm{U}^{\star(1)}\bm{\Sigma}^{\star(1)}+\bm{E}\bm{V}^{\star(1)}
be denoted by

\displaystyle\widetilde{\bm{U}}^{(1)}\widetilde{\bm{\Sigma}}^{(1)}\widetilde{\bm{W}}^{(1)\top}=\bm{U}^{\star(1)}\bm{\Sigma}^{\star(1)}+\bm{E}\bm{V}^{\star(1)}.

(82)

Here,
\widetilde{\bm{U}}^{(1)}\in\mathcal{O}^{n_{1},\overline{r}}
,
\widetilde{\bm{\Sigma}}^{(1)}={\sf diag}(\widetilde{\sigma}_{1},\dots,\widetilde{\sigma}_{\overline{r}})
where
\widetilde{\sigma}_{1}\geq\cdots\geq\widetilde{\sigma}_{\overline{r}}\geq 0
,
\widetilde{\bm{W}}^{(1)}\in\mathcal{O}^{\overline{r},\overline{r}}
. Then one has

\displaystyle\big(\bm{U}^{\star(1)}\bm{\Sigma}^{\star(1)}+\bm{E}\bm{V}^{\star(1)}\big)\big(\bm{U}^{\star(1)}\bm{\Sigma}^{\star(1)}+\bm{E}\bm{V}^{\star(1)}\big)^{\top}=\widetilde{\bm{U}}^{(1)}\big(\widetilde{\bm{\Sigma}}^{(1)}\big)^{2}\widetilde{\bm{U}}^{(1)\top}.

(83)

We can then write
\bm{M}^{\sf oracle}
as follows:

\displaystyle\bm{M}^{\sf oracle}

\displaystyle=\left(\bm{X}^{\star}+\bm{E}\right)\bm{V}^{\star}\bm{V}^{\star\top}\left(\bm{X}^{\star}+\bm{E}\right)^{\top}+\mathcal{P}_{\sf off\text{-}diag}\left(\bm{E}\bm{E}^{\top}-\bm{E}\bm{V}^{\star}\bm{V}^{\star\top}\bm{E}^{\top}\right)

\displaystyle=\left(\bm{X}^{\star}+\bm{E}\right)\bm{V}^{\star(1)}\bm{V}^{\star(1)\top}\left(\bm{X}^{\star}+\bm{E}\right)^{\top}+\left(\bm{X}^{\star}+\bm{E}\right)\bm{V}^{\star(2)}\bm{V}^{\star(2)\top}\left(\bm{X}^{\star}+\bm{E}\right)^{\top}

\displaystyle\quad+\mathcal{P}_{\sf off\text{-}diag}\left(\bm{E}\bm{E}^{\top}-\bm{E}\bm{V}^{\star}\bm{V}^{\star\top}\bm{E}^{\top}\right)

\displaystyle=\underbrace{\big(\bm{U}^{\star(1)}\bm{\Sigma}^{\star(1)}+\bm{E}\bm{V}^{\star(1)}\big)\big(\bm{U}^{\star(1)}\bm{\Sigma}^{\star(1)}+\bm{E}\bm{V}^{\star(1)}\big)^{\top}+\mathcal{P}_{(\widetilde{\bm{U}}^{(1)})_{\perp}}\bm{U}^{\star(2)}\big(\bm{\Sigma}^{\star(2)}\big)^{2}\bm{U}^{\star(2)\top}\mathcal{P}_{(\widetilde{\bm{U}}^{(1)})_{\perp}}}_{=:\widetilde{\bm{M}}}

\displaystyle\quad+\underbrace{\bm{U}^{\star(2)}\bm{\Sigma}^{\star(2)}\bm{V}^{\star(2)\top}\bm{E}^{\top}+\bm{E}\bm{V}^{\star(2)}\bm{\Sigma}^{\star(2)}\bm{U}^{\star(2)\top}+\bm{E}\bm{V}^{\star(2)}\bm{V}^{\star(2)\top}\bm{E}^{\top}}_{=:\bm{Z}_{1}}

\displaystyle\quad+\underbrace{\mathcal{P}_{\widetilde{\bm{U}}^{(1)}}\bm{U}^{\star(2)}\big(\bm{\Sigma}^{\star(2)}\big)^{2}\bm{U}^{\star(2)\top}\mathcal{P}_{\big(\widetilde{\bm{U}}^{(1)}\big)_{\perp}}+\bm{U}^{\star(2)}\big(\bm{\Sigma}^{\star(2)}\big)^{2}\bm{U}^{\star(2)\top}\mathcal{P}_{\widetilde{\bm{U}}^{(1)}}}_{=:\bm{Z}_{2}}

\displaystyle\quad+\underbrace{\mathcal{P}_{\sf off\text{-}diag}\left(\bm{E}\bm{E}^{\top}-\bm{E}\bm{V}^{\star}\bm{V}^{\star\top}\bm{E}^{\top}\right)}_{=:\bm{Z}_{3}}.

(84)

For convenience, we shall also let

\displaystyle\bm{Z}=\bm{Z}_{1}+\bm{Z}_{2}+\bm{Z}_{3}.

(85)

C.1
Several key lemmas

Before proceeding, we first introduce the following lemma, which allows us to bound an infinite sum of
\ell_{2,\infty}
norms of perturbation matrix polynomials instead of bounding
\left\|\bm{U}_{:,1:r^{\prime}}^{\sf oracle}\bm{U}_{:,1:r^{\prime}}^{\sf oracle\top}-\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{:,1:r^{\prime}}\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{:,1:r^{\prime}}^{\top}\right\|_{2,\infty}
directly.

Lemma 1
.

Suppose that
\bm{M}=\overline{\bm{M}}+\bm{Z}\in\mathbb{R}^{n\times n}
, where
\overline{\bm{M}}
and
\bm{Z}
are both symmetric matrices.
Assume that
\overline{\bm{M}}
is a matrix with rank not exceeding
r
and has eigenvalues
\overline{\lambda}_{1}\geq\dots\geq\overline{\lambda}_{r}\geq 0

and rank-
r
leading eigenspace
\overline{\bm{U}}=[\overline{\bm{u}}_{1},\ \dots,\ \overline{\bm{u}}_{r}]
(so that
\overline{\bm{u}}_{i}
is the eigenvector associated with
\overline{\lambda}_{i}
).
If there exists some
r_{1}
obeying
1\leq r_{1}\leq r
and

\displaystyle\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}>2\|\bm{Z}\|,

(86)

then it holds that

\displaystyle\big\|\overline{\bm{U}}_{1}\overline{\bm{U}}_{1}^{\top}-\bm{U}_{1}\bm{U}_{1}^{\top}\big\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{8}{\pi}\sum_{k\geq 1}\left(\frac{2}{\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}}\right)^{k}\sum_{0\leq j_{1},\dots,j_{k+1}\leq r\atop\left(j_{1},...,j_{k+1}\right)\neq\bm{0}}\left\|\overline{\bm{P}}_{j_{1}}\bm{Z}\overline{\bm{P}}_{j_{2}}\bm{Z}\cdots\bm{Z}\overline{\bm{P}}_{j_{k+1}}\right\|_{2,\infty},

(87a)

\displaystyle\big\|\big(\overline{\bm{U}}_{1}\overline{\bm{U}}_{1}^{\top}-\bm{U}_{1}\bm{U}_{1}^{\top}\big)\overline{\bm{M}}\big\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{40}{\pi}\sum_{k\geq 1}\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}}\left(\frac{2}{\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}}\right)^{k}\sum_{0\leq j_{1},\dots,j_{k+1}\leq r\atop\left(j_{1},...,j_{k+1}\right)\neq\bm{0}}\left\|\overline{\bm{P}}_{j_{1}}\bm{Z}\overline{\bm{P}}_{j_{2}}\bm{Z}\cdots\bm{Z}\overline{\bm{P}}_{j_{k+1}}\right\|_{2,\infty}.

(87b)

Here,
\overline{\bm{U}}_{1}
and
\bm{U}_{1}
denote the rank-
r_{1}
leading eigen-subspace of
\overline{\bm{M}}
and
\bm{M}
, respectively;
and we denote
\overline{\bm{P}}_{j}=\overline{\bm{u}}_{j}\overline{\bm{u}}_{j}^{\top}
for any
1\leq j\leq r

and
\overline{\bm{P}}_{0}=\overline{\bm{U}}_{\perp}\overline{\bm{U}}_{\perp}^{\top}
.

The proof of Lemma
1
can be found in Section
C.3
. In addition, the following lemmas deliver sharp
\ell_{2,\infty}
guarantees for some polynomials of the noise matrix.

Lemma 2

(
Zhou and Chen, 2023
, Lemma 2)
.

Suppose that Assumption
2
holds. Then we have, with probability at least
1-O(m^{-10})
,

\displaystyle\left\|\left[\mathcal{P}_{\sf off\text{-}diag}\left(\bm{E}\bm{E}^{\top}\right)\right]^{k}\bm{E}\bm{V}^{\star}\right\|_{2,\infty}\leq C_{3}\sqrt{\mu r}\left(C_{3}\left(\sqrt{m_{1}m_{2}}+m_{1}\right)\omega_{\sf max}^{2}\log^{2}m\right)^{k}\omega_{\sf max}\log m

(88)

for all
0\leq k\leq\log n
, where
C_{3}
is some sufficiently large constant.

Lemma 3

(
Zhou and Chen, 2023
, Lemma 3)
.

Suppose that Assumption
2
holds. Then we have, with probability at least
1-O(m^{-10})
,

\displaystyle\left\|\left[\mathcal{P}_{\sf off\text{-}diag}\left(\bm{E}\bm{E}^{\top}\right)\right]^{k}\bm{U}^{\star}\right\|_{2,\infty}\leq C_{3}\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{n_{1}}}\left(C_{3}\left(\sqrt{m_{1}m_{2}}+m_{1}\right)\omega_{\sf max}^{2}\log^{2}m\right)^{k}

(89)

for all
0\leq k\leq\log n
, where
C_{3}
is some sufficiently large constant.

The following lemma, which was also established in
Zhou and Chen, 2023
, provides some helpful consequences on the eigenvalue perturbation, the size of some perturbation matrix, and some incoherence properties of
\widetilde{\bm{U}}^{(1)}
.

Lemma 4

(
Zhou and Chen, 2023
, Lemma 4)
.

Instate the assumptions in Theorem
5
. Then there exist some large enough constant
C_{5}>0
such that with probability exceeding
1-O(m^{-10})
,

\displaystyle\big|\widetilde{\sigma}_{i}-\sigma_{i}^{\star}\big|

\displaystyle\leq\big\|\bm{E}\bm{V}^{\star(1)}\big\|\leq\left\|\bm{E}\bm{V}^{\star}\right\|\leq\sqrt{C}_{5}\sqrt{m_{1}}\omega_{\sf max}\log m,~\qquad~\forall i\in[\overline{r}],

(90a)

\displaystyle\left\|\mathcal{P}_{\sf off\text{-}diag}\left(\bm{E}\bm{E}^{\top}-\bm{E}\bm{V}^{\star}\bm{V}^{\star\top}\bm{E}^{\top}\right)\right\|

\displaystyle\leq 3C_{5}\left(\sqrt{m_{1}m_{2}}+m_{1}\right)\omega_{\sf max}^{2}\log^{2}m

(90b)

\displaystyle\big\|\bm{U}^{\star(1)}\bm{U}^{\star(1)\top}\widetilde{\bm{U}}^{(1)}-\widetilde{\bm{U}}^{(1)}\big\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4C_{5}\sqrt{\mu r}\omega_{\sf max}\log m}{\sigma_{r}^{\star}}\leq\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{m_{1}}},

(90c)

\displaystyle\big\|\widetilde{\bm{U}}^{(1)}\big\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq 2\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{m_{1}}}.

(90d)

Finally, the following lemma develops
\ell_{2,\infty}
bounds on the polynomials of the perturbation matrix
\bm{Z}_{3}
, which will play a key role in the subsequent proof.

Lemma 5
.

Suppose that Assumption
2
holds. Let

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}=\{\eqref{ineq:power_V}\text{ and }\eqref{ineq:power_U}\text{ hold for }0\leq k\leq\log n\}\cap\{\eqref{ineq2a},\eqref{ineq2b},\eqref{ineq2c}\text{ and }\eqref{ineq2d}\text{ hold}\}.

(91)

Then there exists some large enough constant
C_{2},C_{3}>0
(independent of
C_{0}
) such that under
\mathcal{E}
, for any
0\leq i\leq\log n
, one has

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{Z}_{3}^{i}\bm{U}^{\star}\right\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq 3C_{3}\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{m_{1}}}\left(C_{3}\left(\sqrt{m_{1}m_{2}}+m_{1}\right)\omega_{\sf max}^{2}\log^{2}m\right)^{i},

(92a)

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{Z}_{3}^{i}\bm{E}\bm{V}^{\star}\right\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq 3C_{3}\sqrt{\mu r}\left(C_{3}\left(\sqrt{m_{1}m_{2}}+m_{1}\right)\omega_{\sf max}^{2}\log^{2}m\right)^{i}\omega_{\sf max}\log m,

(92b)

\displaystyle\big\|\bm{Z}_{3}^{i}\widetilde{\bm{U}}^{(1)}\big\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq 4C_{3}\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{m_{1}}}\left(C_{3}\left(\sqrt{m_{1}m_{2}}+m_{1}\right)\omega_{\sf max}^{2}\log^{2}m\right)^{i},

(92c)

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{Z}_{3}^{i}\bm{Z}_{1}\right\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq C_{2}\sqrt{\mu r}\left(\sigma_{\overline{r}+1}^{\star}+\sqrt{m}_{1}\omega_{\sf max}\log m\right)\left(C_{3}\left(\sqrt{m_{1}m_{2}}+m_{1}\right)\omega_{\sf max}^{2}\log^{2}m\right)^{i}\omega_{\sf max}\log m,

(92d)

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{Z}_{3}^{i}\bm{Z}_{2}\right\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq C_{2}\sqrt{\mu r}\left(C_{3}\left(\sqrt{m_{1}m_{2}}+m_{1}\right)\omega_{\sf max}^{2}\log^{2}m\right)^{i}\omega_{\sf max}\sigma_{\overline{r}+1}^{\star}\log m.

(92e)

The proof of Lemma
5
is postponed to Section
C.4
. The union bound taken together with Lemma
2
, Lemma
3
and Lemma
4
shows that

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\mathcal{E}\right)\geq 1-O\left(n^{-10}\right).

(93)

In the rest of the proof, we assume that
\mathcal{E}
occurs unless otherwise noted.

C.2
Main steps for proving (
39b
)

Step 1: bounding
\|\widetilde{\bm{U}}^{(2)}\|_{2}
.

We start with controlling
\|\widetilde{\bm{U}}^{(2)}\|_{2}
.
Combining (
90a
),
(
Chen et al., 2021a
, Lemma 2.5)
and Wedin’s
\sin\bm{\Theta}
theorem, one has

\displaystyle\big\|\widetilde{\bm{U}}^{(1)\top}\bm{U}^{\star(2)}\big\|

\displaystyle\leq\big\|\widetilde{\bm{U}}^{(1)\top}\big(\bm{U}^{\star(1)}\big)_{\perp}\big\|=\big\|\widetilde{\bm{U}}^{(1)}\widetilde{\bm{U}}^{(1)\top}-\bm{U}^{\star(1)}\bm{U}^{\star(1)\top}\big\|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\big\|\bm{E}\bm{V}^{\star(1)}\big\|}{\sigma_{\overline{r}}^{\star}}\leq\frac{2\sqrt{C_{5}}\sqrt{m}_{1}\omega_{\sf max}\log m}{\sigma_{\overline{r}}^{\star}}\ll\frac{1}{2},

(94)

where the first inequality makes use of the fact that
\bm{U}_{1}^{\star\top}\bm{U}_{2}^{\star}=\bm{0}
. Note that the rank of
\widetilde{\bm{M}}
is at most
r
. We also denote the eigendecomposition of
\mathcal{P}_{(\widetilde{\bm{U}}^{(1)})_{\perp}}\bm{U}^{\star(2)}\big(\bm{\Sigma}^{\star(2)}\big)^{2}\bm{U}^{\star(2)\top}\mathcal{P}_{(\widetilde{\bm{U}}^{(1)})_{\perp}}
by

\displaystyle\widetilde{\bm{U}}^{(2)}\big(\widetilde{\bm{\Sigma}}^{(2)}\big)^{2}\widetilde{\bm{U}}^{(2)}=\mathcal{P}_{(\widetilde{\bm{U}}^{(1)})_{\perp}}\bm{U}^{\star(2)}\big(\bm{\Sigma}^{\star(2)}\big)^{2}\bm{U}^{\star(2)\top}\mathcal{P}_{(\widetilde{\bm{U}}^{(1)})_{\perp}},

(95)

where
\widetilde{\bm{U}}^{(2)}\in\mathcal{O}^{m_{1},r-\overline{r}}
and
\widetilde{\bm{\Sigma}}^{(1)}={\sf diag}(\widetilde{\sigma}_{\overline{r}+1},\dots,\widetilde{\sigma}_{r})
with
\widetilde{\sigma}_{\overline{r}+1}\geq\cdots\geq\widetilde{\sigma}_{r}\geq 0
. Recognizing that
\widetilde{\bm{U}}^{(1)\top}\widetilde{\bm{U}}^{(2)}=\bm{0}
, we see that the eigendecomposition of
\widetilde{\bm{M}}
can be written as

\displaystyle\widetilde{\bm{M}}=\widetilde{\bm{U}}\widetilde{\bm{\Lambda}}\widetilde{\bm{U}}^{\top},

(96)

where

\displaystyle\widetilde{\bm{U}}=\big[\widetilde{\bm{U}}^{(1)}\ \widetilde{\bm{U}}^{(2)}\big],~\qquad~and~\qquad~\widetilde{\bm{\Lambda}}={\sf diag}\big(\widetilde{\sigma}_{1}^{2},\dots,\widetilde{\sigma}_{r}^{2}\big)=\begin{bmatrix}\big(\widetilde{\bm{\Sigma}}^{(1)}\big)^{2}&\bm{0}\\
\bm{0}&\big(\widetilde{\bm{\Sigma}}^{(2)}\big)^{2}\end{bmatrix}.

(97)

In addition, one observes that

\displaystyle\sigma_{r-\overline{r}}\big(\mathcal{P}_{(\widetilde{\bm{U}}^{(1)})_{\perp}}\bm{U}^{\star(2)}\big)

\displaystyle=\sigma_{r-\overline{r}}\left(\big(\widetilde{\bm{U}}^{(1)}\big)_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{U}^{\star(2)}\right)=\min_{\bm{a}\in\mathbb{R}^{r-\overline{r}}:\left\|\bm{a}\right\|_{2}=1}\left\|\big(\widetilde{\bm{U}}^{(1)}\big)_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{U}^{\star(2)}\bm{a}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\min_{\bm{a}\in\mathbb{R}^{r-\overline{r}}:\left\|\bm{a}\right\|_{2}=1}\left(\big\|\bm{U}^{\star(2)}\bm{a}\big\|_{2}^{2}-\big\|\widetilde{\bm{U}}^{(1)\top}\bm{U}^{\star(2)}\bm{a}\big\|_{2}^{2}\right)}

\displaystyle=\sqrt{1-\max_{\bm{a}\in\mathbb{R}^{r-\overline{r}}:\left\|\bm{a}\right\|_{2}=1}\big\|\widetilde{\bm{U}}^{(1)\top}\bm{U}^{\star(2)}\bm{a}\big\|_{2}^{2}}

\displaystyle=\sqrt{1-\big\|\widetilde{\bm{U}}^{(1)\top}\bm{U}^{\star(2)}\big\|^{2}}

\displaystyle\geq\sqrt{1-\frac{1}{Cr^{2}}}

\displaystyle\geq\sqrt{1-\left(\frac{1}{2}\right)^{2}}=\frac{\sqrt{3}}{2}.

(98)

The last line holds due to (
C.2
). Noting that
\widetilde{\bm{U}}^{(2)}
is also the column subspace of
\mathcal{P}_{(\widetilde{\bm{U}}^{(1)})_{\perp}}\bm{U}^{\star(2)}\in\mathbb{R}^{m_{1},r-\overline{r}}
and combining (
90d
), (
C.2
) and (
C.2
), one reaches

\displaystyle\big\|\widetilde{\bm{U}}^{(2)}\big\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\big\|\mathcal{P}_{(\widetilde{\bm{U}}^{(1)})_{\perp}}\bm{U}^{\star(2)}\big\|_{2,\infty}\sigma_{r-\overline{r}}^{-1}\big(\mathcal{P}_{(\widetilde{\bm{U}}^{(1)})_{\perp}}\bm{U}^{\star(2)}\big)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2}{\sqrt{3}}\left(\big\|\bm{U}^{\star(2)}\big\|_{2,\infty}+\big\|\mathcal{P}_{\widetilde{\bm{U}}^{(1)}}\bm{U}^{\star(2)}\big\|_{2,\infty}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2}{\sqrt{3}}\left(\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{m_{1}}}+\big\|\widetilde{\bm{U}}^{(1)}\big\|_{2,\infty}\big\|\widetilde{\bm{U}}^{(1)\top}\bm{U}^{\star(2)}\big\|\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2}{\sqrt{3}}\left(\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{m_{1}}}+2\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{m_{1}}}\cdot\frac{1}{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq 2\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{m_{1}}}.

(99)

Step 2: bounding
\widetilde{\sigma}_{r^{\prime}}^{2}-\widetilde{\sigma}_{r^{\prime}+1}^{2}
and
\|\bm{Z}\|
.

Recall that
\lambda_{i}(\widetilde{M})=\widetilde{\sigma}_{i}^{2}
for
i\in[r]
. To apply Lemma
1
, one needs to check the condition

\displaystyle\widetilde{\sigma}_{r^{\prime}}^{2}-\widetilde{\sigma}_{r^{\prime}+1}^{2}>2\|\bm{Z}\|,~\qquad~\forall r^{\prime}\in\mathcal{A}.

(100)

It is seen from the definition of
\widetilde{\sigma}_{\overline{r}+1}
that

\displaystyle\widetilde{\sigma}_{\overline{r}+1}\leq\big\|\bm{\Sigma}^{\star(2)}\big\|=\sigma_{\overline{r}+1}^{\star}.

(101)

Further, (
95
) and (
C.2
) taken together imply that

\displaystyle\widetilde{\sigma}_{\overline{r}+1}^{2}

\displaystyle\geq\sigma_{r-\overline{r}}^{2}\big(\mathcal{P}_{(\widetilde{\bm{U}}^{(1)})_{\perp}}\bm{U}^{\star(2)}\big)\sigma_{\overline{r}+1}^{\star 2}\geq\left(1-\frac{2\sqrt{C_{5}}\sqrt{m}_{1}\omega_{\sf max}\log m}{\sigma_{\overline{r}}^{\star}}\right)^{2}\sigma_{\overline{r}+1}^{\star 2}\geq\left(1-\frac{1}{Cr^{2}}\right)^{2}\sigma_{\overline{r}+1}^{\star 2}

(102)

for some large constant
C>0
.
By virtue of (
101
) and (
102
), one has

\displaystyle\max\left\{\left(1-\frac{1}{Cr^{2}}\right)\sigma_{\overline{r}+1}^{\star},\sigma_{\overline{r}+1}^{\star}-2\sqrt{C_{5}}\sqrt{m}_{1}\omega_{\sf max}\log m\right\}\leq\widetilde{\sigma}_{\overline{r}+1}\leq\sigma_{\overline{r}+1}^{\star}.

(103)

Putting (
90a
), (
101
) and the fact
\sigma_{r^{\prime}}^{\star}\geq\sigma_{\overline{r}}^{\star}\geq C_{0}r[(m_{1}m_{2})^{1/4}+m_{1}^{1/2}]\log m
together yields

\displaystyle\widetilde{\sigma}_{r^{\prime}}-\widetilde{\sigma}_{r^{\prime}+1}

\displaystyle\geq\sigma_{r^{\prime}}^{\star}-\sigma_{r^{\prime}+1}^{\star}-2\left\|\bm{E}\bm{V}^{\star}\right\|

\displaystyle\geq\sigma_{r^{\prime}}^{\star}-\sigma_{r^{\prime}+1}^{\star}-2\sqrt{C_{5}}\sqrt{m_{1}}\omega_{\sf max}\log m

\displaystyle\geq\sigma_{r^{\prime}}^{\star}-\sigma_{r^{\prime}+1}^{\star}-\frac{\sigma_{r^{\prime}}^{\star}}{Cr}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{2}\left(\sigma_{r^{\prime}}^{\star}-\sigma_{r^{\prime}+1}^{\star}\right)+\frac{1}{2}\left(\sigma_{r^{\prime}}^{\star}-\frac{4r-1}{4r}\sigma_{r^{\prime}}^{\star}\right)-\frac{\sigma_{r^{\prime}}^{\star}}{Cr}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{2}\left(\sigma_{r^{\prime}}^{\star}-\sigma_{r^{\prime}+1}^{\star}\right)\geq\frac{\sigma_{r^{\prime}}^{\star}}{8r}.

Here, the penultimate and the last lines hold due to the fact
r^{\prime}\in\mathcal{A}
. In addition, we observe that

\displaystyle\widetilde{\sigma}_{r^{\prime}}+\widetilde{\sigma}_{r^{\prime}+1}\geq\sigma_{r^{\prime}}^{\star}+\sigma_{r^{\prime}+1}^{\star}-2\left\|\bm{E}\bm{V}^{\star}\right\|\geq\sigma_{r^{\prime}}^{\star}+\sigma_{r^{\prime}+1}^{\star}-\frac{\sigma_{r^{\prime}}^{\star}}{Cr}\geq\frac{1}{2}\left(\sigma_{r^{\prime}}^{\star}+\sigma_{r^{\prime}+1}^{\star}\right).

Combining the previous two inequalities leads to

\displaystyle\widetilde{\sigma}_{r^{\prime}}^{2}-\widetilde{\sigma}_{r^{\prime}+1}^{2}\geq\frac{1}{4}\left(\sigma_{r^{\prime}}^{\star 2}-\sigma_{r^{\prime}+1}^{\star 2}\right)\geq\frac{\sigma_{r^{\prime}}^{\star 2}}{16r}.

(104)

Now, we move on to control
\|\bm{Z}\|
. In view of (
90a
) and (
C.2
), we have

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{Z}_{1}\right\|\leq 2\big\|\bm{\Sigma}^{\star(2)}\big\|\left\|\bm{E}\bm{V}^{\star}\right\|+\left\|\bm{E}\bm{V}^{\star}\right\|^{2}\leq 2\sqrt{C_{5}}\sqrt{m_{1}}\omega_{\sf max}\log m\cdot\sigma_{\overline{r}+1}^{\star}+C_{5}m_{1}\omega_{\sf max}^{2}\log^{2}m

(105)

and

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{Z}_{2}\right\|\leq 2\big\|\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{1}^{\top}\bm{U}_{2}^{\star(2)}\big\|\big\|\bm{\Sigma}^{\star(2)}\big\|^{2}\lesssim\frac{\sqrt{m}_{1}\omega_{\sf max}\log m}{\sigma_{\overline{r}}^{\star}}\sigma_{\overline{r}+1}^{\star 2}\leq\sqrt{m_{1}}\omega_{\sf max}\log m\cdot\sigma_{\overline{r}+1}^{\star}.

(106)

Combining (
105
), (
106
) and (
90b
), we arrive at

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{Z}\right\|\lesssim\sqrt{m_{1}}\omega_{\sf max}\log m\cdot\sigma_{\overline{r}+1}^{\star}+\left(\sqrt{m_{1}m_{2}}+m_{1}\right)\omega_{\sf max}^{2}\log^{2}m\ll\frac{\sigma_{r^{\prime}}^{\star 2}}{16r}\leq\widetilde{\sigma}_{r^{\prime}}^{2}-\widetilde{\sigma}_{r^{\prime}+1}^{2},

(107)

which validates (
100
). Here, the second inequality uses the facts
\sigma_{r^{\prime}}^{\star}\geq\sigma_{\overline{r}}^{\star}\geq C_{0}r[(m_{1}m_{2})^{1/4}+rm_{1}^{1/2}]\log m
.
By virtue of Lemma
1
and (
104
), we have

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{U}_{:,1:r^{\prime}}^{\sf oracle}\bm{U}_{:,1:r^{\prime}}^{\sf oracle\top}-\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{:,1:r^{\prime}}\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{:,1:r^{\prime}}^{\top}\right\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\quad\leq\frac{8}{\pi}\sum_{k\geq 1}\frac{2^{k}}{\left(\widetilde{\sigma}_{r^{\prime}}^{2}-\widetilde{\sigma}_{r^{\prime}+1}^{2}\right)^{k}}\sum_{0\leq j_{1},\dots,j_{k+1}\leq r\atop\left(j_{1},...,j_{k+1}\right)^{\top}\neq\bm{0}_{k+1}}\big\|\widetilde{\bm{P}}_{j_{1}}\bm{Z}\widetilde{\bm{P}}_{j_{2}}\bm{Z}\cdots\bm{Z}\widetilde{\bm{P}}_{j_{k+1}}\big\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\quad\leq\frac{8}{\pi}\sum_{k\geq 1}\left(\frac{8}{\sigma_{r^{\prime}}^{\star 2}-\sigma_{r^{\prime}+1}^{\star 2}}\right)^{k}\sum_{0\leq j_{1},\dots,j_{k+1}\leq r\atop\left(j_{1},...,j_{k+1}\right)^{\top}\neq\bm{0}_{k+1}}\big\|\widetilde{\bm{P}}_{j_{1}}\bm{Z}\widetilde{\bm{P}}_{j_{2}}\bm{Z}\cdots\bm{Z}\widetilde{\bm{P}}_{j_{k+1}}\big\|_{2,\infty},

(108)

Here, for any
1\leq j\leq r
,
\widetilde{\bm{P}}_{j}=\widetilde{\bm{u}}_{j}\widetilde{\bm{u}}_{j}^{\top}
and
\widetilde{\bm{P}}_{0}=\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{\perp}\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{\perp}^{\top}
.

To bound
\|\bm{U}_{:,1:r^{\prime}}^{\sf oracle}\bm{U}_{:,1:r^{\prime}}^{\sf oracle\top}-\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{:,1:r^{\prime}}\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{:,1:r^{\prime}}^{\top}\|_{2,\infty}
, we will bound each single term
\|\widetilde{\bm{P}}_{j_{1}}\bm{Z}\widetilde{\bm{P}}_{j_{2}}\bm{Z}\cdots\bm{Z}\widetilde{\bm{P}}_{j_{k+1}}\|_{2,\infty}
for
1\leq k\leq\log n
, and show that the total contribution of the remaining terms is small.

Step 3: bounding
\|\widetilde{\bm{P}}_{j_{1}}\bm{Z}\widetilde{\bm{P}}_{j_{2}}\bm{Z}\cdots\bm{Z}\widetilde{\bm{P}}_{j_{k+1}}\|_{2,\infty}
for small
k
.

For any
1\leq k\leq\log n
and
(j_{1},\dots,j_{k+1})\in\{0,1,\dots,r\}^{k+1}\backslash\bm{0}
, let
\ell
denote the the smallest
i
such that
j_{i}\neq 0
.

Step 3.1: bounding
\|\widetilde{\bm{P}}_{j_{1}}\bm{Z}\widetilde{\bm{P}}_{j_{2}}\bm{Z}\cdots\bm{Z}\widetilde{\bm{P}}_{j_{k+1}}\|_{2,\infty}
when
\ell=1
.

If
\ell=1
, then (
90d
) and (
C.2
) taken collectively show that

\displaystyle\left\|\widetilde{\bm{u}}_{j_{1}}\right\|_{\infty}\leq\max\big\{\big\|\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{1}\big\|_{2,\infty},\big\|\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{2}\big\|_{2,\infty}\big\}\leq 2\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{m_{1}}}.

(109)

Inequality (
107
) taken together with (
109
) leads to

\displaystyle\big\|\widetilde{\bm{P}}_{j_{1}}\bm{Z}\widetilde{\bm{P}}_{j_{2}}\bm{Z}\cdots\bm{Z}\widetilde{\bm{P}}_{j_{k+1}}\big\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle=\big\|\widetilde{\bm{u}}_{j_{1}}\widetilde{\bm{u}}_{j_{1}}^{\top}\bm{Z}\widetilde{\bm{P}}_{j_{2}}\bm{Z}\cdots\bm{Z}\widetilde{\bm{P}}_{j_{k+1}}\big\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\widetilde{\bm{u}}_{j_{1}}\right\|_{\infty}\big\|\widetilde{\bm{u}}_{j_{1}}^{\top}\bm{Z}\widetilde{\bm{P}}_{j_{2}}\bm{Z}\cdots\bm{Z}\widetilde{\bm{P}}_{j_{k+1}}\big\|

\displaystyle\leq 2\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{m_{1}}}\left\|\bm{Z}\right\|^{k}

\displaystyle\leq 2\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{m_{1}}}\left(C_{2}\left(\sqrt{m_{1}}\omega_{\sf max}\log m\cdot\sigma_{\overline{r}+1}^{\star}+\left(\sqrt{m_{1}m_{2}}+m_{1}\right)\omega_{\sf max}^{2}\log^{2}m\right)\right)^{k}.

(110)

Step 3.2: bounding
\|\bm{Z}^{i}\widetilde{\bm{U}}\|_{2,\infty}
.

Turning to
\ell\geq 2
, we see from the triangle inequality that

\displaystyle\big\|\widetilde{\bm{P}}_{j_{1}}\bm{Z}\widetilde{\bm{P}}_{j_{2}}\bm{Z}\cdots\bm{Z}\widetilde{\bm{P}}_{j_{k+1}}\big\|_{2,\infty}\leq\big\|\bm{Z}^{\ell-1}\widetilde{\bm{P}}_{j_{\ell}}\bm{Z}\cdots\bm{Z}\widetilde{\bm{P}}_{j_{k+1}}\big\|_{2,\infty}+\sum_{i=1}^{\ell-1}\left\|\bm{Z}^{i-1}\bm{P}_{\widetilde{\bm{U}}}\bm{Z}\widetilde{\bm{P}}_{j_{i+1}}\bm{Z}\cdots\bm{Z}\widetilde{\bm{P}}_{j_{k+1}}\right\|_{2,\infty}.

(111)

To bound the right-hand side of (
111
), it is helpful to bound
\|\bm{Z}^{i}\widetilde{\bm{U}}\|_{2,\infty}
first.

Step 3.2.1: bounding
\|\bm{Z}^{i}\widetilde{\bm{U}}^{(1)}\|_{2,\infty}
.

Recognizing that for any matrices
\bm{A},\bm{B}\in\mathbb{R}^{m_{1}\times m_{1}}
, we see that the following equation holds:

\displaystyle\left(\bm{A}+\bm{B}\right)^{i}=\bm{B}^{i}+\sum_{j=0}^{i-1}\bm{B}^{j}\bm{A}\left(\bm{A}+\bm{B}\right)^{i-j-1}.

This allows one to derive

\displaystyle\bm{Z}^{i}\widetilde{\bm{U}}^{(1)}

\displaystyle=\left(\bm{Z}_{1}+\bm{Z}_{2}+\bm{Z}_{3}\right)^{i}\widetilde{\bm{U}}^{(1)}

\displaystyle=\bm{Z}_{3}^{i}\widetilde{\bm{U}}^{(1)}+\sum_{j=0}^{i-1}\bm{Z}_{3}^{j}\bm{Z}_{1}\bm{Z}^{i-j-1}\widetilde{\bm{U}}^{(1)}+\sum_{j=0}^{i-1}\bm{Z}_{3}^{j}\bm{Z}_{2}\bm{Z}^{i-j-1}\widetilde{\bm{U}}^{(1)}.

(112)

By virtue of Lemma
5
and (
107
), one has

\displaystyle\big\|\bm{Z}^{i}\widetilde{\bm{U}}^{(1)}\big\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\big\|\bm{Z}_{3}^{i}\widetilde{\bm{U}}^{(1)}\big\|_{2,\infty}+\sum_{j=0}^{i-1}\big\|\bm{Z}_{3}^{j}\left(\bm{Z}_{1}+\bm{Z}_{2}\right)\bm{Z}^{i-j-1}\widetilde{\bm{U}}^{(1)}\big\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\big\|\bm{Z}_{3}^{i}\widetilde{\bm{U}}^{(1)}\big\|_{2,\infty}+\sum_{j=0}^{i-1}\big(\big\|\bm{Z}_{3}^{j}\bm{Z}_{1}\big\|_{2,\infty}+\big\|\bm{Z}_{3}^{j}\bm{Z}_{2}\big\|_{2,\infty}\big)\big\|\bm{Z}^{i-j-1}\widetilde{\bm{U}}^{(1)}\big\|

\displaystyle\leq\big\|\bm{Z}_{3}^{i}\widetilde{\bm{U}}^{(1)}\big\|_{2,\infty}+\sum_{j=0}^{i-1}\big(\big\|\bm{Z}_{3}^{j}\bm{Z}_{1}\big\|_{2,\infty}+\big\|\bm{Z}_{3}^{j}\bm{Z}_{2}\big\|_{2,\infty}\big)\big\|\bm{Z}\big\|^{i-j-1}

\displaystyle\leq 4C_{3}\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{m_{1}}}\left(C_{3}\left(\sqrt{m_{1}m_{2}}+m_{1}\right)\omega_{\sf max}^{2}\log^{2}m\right)^{i}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{j=0}^{i-1}C_{2}\sqrt{\mu r}\left(\sigma_{\overline{r}+1}^{\star}+\sqrt{m}_{1}\omega_{\sf max}\log m\right)\left(C_{3}\left(\sqrt{m_{1}m_{2}}+m_{1}\right)\omega_{\sf max}^{2}\log^{2}m\right)^{j}\omega_{\sf max}\log m\cdot

\displaystyle\hskip 34.14322pt\left(C_{2}\left(\sqrt{m_{1}}\omega_{\sf max}\log m\cdot\sigma_{\overline{r}+1}^{\star}+\left(\sqrt{m_{1}m_{2}}+m_{1}\right)\omega_{\sf max}^{2}\log^{2}m\right)\right)^{i-j-1}

\displaystyle\leq 4C_{3}\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{m_{1}}}\left(C_{2}\left(\sqrt{m_{1}}\omega_{\sf max}\log m\cdot\sigma_{\overline{r}+1}^{\star}+\left(\sqrt{m_{1}m_{2}}+m_{1}\right)\omega_{\sf max}^{2}\log^{2}m\right)\right)^{i}

\displaystyle\quad+\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{m_{1}}}\left(C_{2}\left(\sqrt{m_{1}}\omega_{\sf max}\log m\cdot\sigma_{\overline{r}+1}^{\star}+\left(\sqrt{m_{1}m_{2}}+m_{1}\right)\omega_{\sf max}^{2}\log^{2}m\right)\right)^{i}\sum_{j=0}^{i-1}\frac{1}{2^{j}}

\displaystyle\leq C_{2}\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{m_{1}}}\left(C_{2}\left(\sqrt{m_{1}}\omega_{\sf max}\log m\cdot\sigma_{\overline{r}+1}^{\star}+\left(\sqrt{m_{1}m_{2}}+m_{1}\right)\omega_{\sf max}^{2}\log^{2}m\right)\right)^{i},

(113)

provided that
C_{2}\geq 4C_{3}+1
.

Step 3.2.2: bounding
\|\bm{Z}^{i}\widetilde{\bm{U}}^{(2)}\|_{2,\infty}
.

Note that
\widetilde{\bm{U}}^{(2)}
is also the column subspace of
\mathcal{P}_{(\widetilde{\bm{U}}^{(1)})_{\perp}}\bm{U}^{\star(2)}\in\mathbb{R}^{m_{1},r-\overline{r}}
. In view of Lemma
5
, (
C.2
), (
C.2
) and (
C.2
), we arrive at

\displaystyle\big\|\bm{Z}^{i}\widetilde{\bm{U}}^{(2)}\big\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\big\|\bm{Z}^{i}\mathcal{P}_{(\widetilde{\bm{U}}^{(1)})_{\perp}}\bm{U}^{\star(2)}\big\|_{2,\infty}\sigma_{r-\overline{r}}^{-1}\big(\mathcal{P}_{(\widetilde{\bm{U}}^{(1)})_{\perp}}\bm{U}^{\star(2)}\big)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2}{\sqrt{3}}\left(\big\|\bm{Z}^{i}\bm{U}^{\star(2)}\big\|_{2,\infty}+\big\|\bm{Z}^{i}\mathcal{P}_{\widetilde{\bm{U}}^{(1)}}\bm{U}^{\star(2)}\big\|_{2,\infty}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2}{\sqrt{3}}\left(\big\|\bm{Z}^{i}\bm{U}^{\star(2)}\big\|_{2,\infty}+\big\|\bm{Z}^{i}\widetilde{\bm{U}}^{(1)}\big\|_{2,\infty}\big\|\widetilde{\bm{U}}^{(1)\top}\bm{U}^{\star(2)}\big\|\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2}{\sqrt{3}}\bigg(3C_{3}\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{m_{1}}}\left(C_{3}\left(\sqrt{m_{1}m_{2}}+m_{1}\right)\omega_{\sf max}^{2}\log^{2}m\right)^{i}

\displaystyle\hskip 34.14322pt+C_{2}\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{m_{1}}}\left(C_{2}\left(\sqrt{m_{1}}\omega_{\sf max}\log m\cdot\sigma_{\overline{r}+1}^{\star}+\left(\sqrt{m_{1}m_{2}}+m_{1}\right)\omega_{\sf max}^{2}\log^{2}m\right)\right)^{i}\cdot\frac{1}{2}\bigg)

\displaystyle\leq C_{2}\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{m_{1}}}\left(C_{2}\left(\sqrt{m_{1}}\omega_{\sf max}\log m\cdot\sigma_{\overline{r}+1}^{\star}+\left(\sqrt{m_{1}m_{2}}+m_{1}\right)\omega_{\sf max}^{2}\log^{2}m\right)\right)^{i}.

(114)

Putting (
C.2
) and (
C.2
) together and recognizing that
\widetilde{\bm{U}}=[\widetilde{\bm{U}}^{(1)}\ \widetilde{\bm{U}}^{(2)}]
,
we conclude that

\displaystyle\big\|\bm{Z}^{i}\widetilde{\bm{U}}\big\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\big\|\bm{Z}^{i}\widetilde{\bm{U}}^{(1)}\big\|_{2,\infty}+\big\|\bm{Z}^{i}\widetilde{\bm{U}}^{(2)}\big\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq 2C_{2}\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{m_{1}}}\left(C_{2}\left(\sqrt{m_{1}}\omega_{\sf max}\log m\cdot\sigma_{\overline{r}+1}^{\star}+\left(\sqrt{m_{1}m_{2}}+m_{1}\right)\omega_{\sf max}^{2}\log^{2}m\right)\right)^{i}.

(115)

Step 4: bounding
\|\widetilde{\bm{P}}_{j_{1}}\bm{Z}\widetilde{\bm{P}}_{j_{2}}\bm{Z}\cdots\bm{Z}\widetilde{\bm{P}}_{j_{k+1}}\|_{2,\infty}
when
\ell>1
.

Plugging (
107
) and (
C.2
) into (
111
) yields that, for
\ell\geq 2
,

\displaystyle\big\|\widetilde{\bm{P}}_{j_{1}}\bm{Z}\widetilde{\bm{P}}_{j_{2}}\bm{Z}\cdots\bm{Z}\widetilde{\bm{P}}_{j_{k+1}}\big\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\quad\leq\left\|\bm{Z}^{\ell-1}\widetilde{\bm{u}}_{j_{\ell}}\right\|_{2,\infty}\big\|\widetilde{\bm{u}}_{j_{\ell}}^{\top}\bm{Z}\cdots\bm{Z}\widetilde{\bm{P}}_{j_{k+1}}\big\|_{2,\infty}+\sum_{i=1}^{\ell-1}\big\|\bm{Z}^{i-1}\widetilde{\bm{U}}\big\|_{2,\infty}\big\|\widetilde{\bm{U}}^{\top}\bm{Z}\widetilde{\bm{P}}_{j_{i+1}}\bm{Z}\cdots\bm{Z}\widetilde{\bm{P}}_{j_{k+1}}\big\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\quad\leq\big\|\bm{Z}^{\ell-1}\widetilde{\bm{U}}\big\|_{2,\infty}\left\|\bm{Z}\right\|^{k-\ell+1}+\sum_{i=1}^{\ell-1}\big\|\bm{Z}^{i-1}\widetilde{\bm{U}}\big\|_{2,\infty}\left\|\bm{Z}\right\|^{k-i+1}

\displaystyle\quad\leq 2C_{2}\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{m_{1}}}\left(C_{2}\left(\sqrt{m_{1}}\omega_{\sf max}\log m\cdot\sigma_{\overline{r}+1}^{\star}+\left(\sqrt{m_{1}m_{2}}+m_{1}\right)\omega_{\sf max}^{2}\log^{2}m\right)\right)^{\ell-1}\cdot

\displaystyle\qquad\left(C_{2}\left(\sqrt{m_{1}}\omega_{\sf max}\log m\cdot\sigma_{\overline{r}+1}^{\star}+\left(\sqrt{m_{1}m_{2}}+m_{1}\right)\omega_{\sf max}^{2}\log^{2}m\right)\right)^{k-\ell+1}

\displaystyle\qquad+\sum_{i=1}^{\ell-1}2C_{2}\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{m_{1}}}\left(C_{2}\left(\sqrt{m_{1}}\omega_{\sf max}\log m\cdot\sigma_{\overline{r}+1}^{\star}+\left(\sqrt{m_{1}m_{2}}+m_{1}\right)\omega_{\sf max}^{2}\log^{2}m\right)\right)^{i-1}\cdot

\displaystyle\hskip 45.52458pt\left(C_{2}\left(\sqrt{m_{1}}\omega_{\sf max}\log m\cdot\sigma_{\overline{r}+1}^{\star}+\left(\sqrt{m_{1}m_{2}}+m_{1}\right)\omega_{\sf max}^{2}\log^{2}m\right)\right)^{k-i+1}

\displaystyle\quad=2C_{2}\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{m_{1}}}\left(C_{2}\left(\sqrt{m_{1}}\omega_{\sf max}\log m\cdot\sigma_{\overline{r}+1}^{\star}+\left(\sqrt{m_{1}m_{2}}+m_{1}\right)\omega_{\sf max}^{2}\log^{2}m\right)\right)^{k}\cdot\ell

\displaystyle\quad\leq 2C_{2}\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{m_{1}}}\left(2C_{2}\left(\sqrt{m_{1}}\omega_{\sf max}\log m\cdot\sigma_{\overline{r}+1}^{\star}+\left(\sqrt{m_{1}m_{2}}+m_{1}\right)\omega_{\sf max}^{2}\log^{2}m\right)\right)^{k}.

The last inequality comes from
\ell\leq k+1\leq 2^{k}
.
Combining the previous inequality and (
C.2
) reveals that: for any
1\leq k\leq\log n
and
(j_{1},\dots,j_{k+1})\in\{0,1,\dots,r\}^{k+1}\backslash\bm{0}
, it holds that

\displaystyle\big\|\widetilde{\bm{P}}_{j_{1}}\bm{Z}\widetilde{\bm{P}}_{j_{2}}\bm{Z}\cdots\bm{Z}\widetilde{\bm{P}}_{j_{k+1}}\big\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\leq 2C_{2}\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{m_{1}}}\left(2C_{2}\left(\sqrt{m_{1}}\omega_{\sf max}\log m\cdot\sigma_{\overline{r}+1}^{\star}+\left(\sqrt{m_{1}m_{2}}+m_{1}\right)\omega_{\sf max}^{2}\log^{2}m\right)\right)^{k}.

(116)

Step 5: bounding
\|\bm{U}_{:,1:r^{\prime}}^{\sf oracle}\bm{U}_{:,1:r^{\prime}}^{\sf oracle\top}-\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{:,1:r^{\prime}}\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{:,1:r^{\prime}}^{\top}\|_{2,\infty}
.

Now, we are ready to bound
\|\bm{U}_{:,1:r^{\prime}}^{\sf oracle}\bm{U}_{:,1:r^{\prime}}^{\sf oracle\top}-\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{:,1:r^{\prime}}\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{:,1:r^{\prime}}^{\top}\|_{2,\infty}
. As a consequence of (
C.2
), for any
1\leq k\leq\log n
, one has

\displaystyle\left(\frac{8}{\sigma_{r^{\prime}}^{\star 2}-\sigma_{r^{\prime}+1}^{\star 2}}\right)^{k}\sum_{0\leq j_{1},\dots,j_{k+1}\leq r\atop\left(j_{1},...,j_{k+1}\right)^{\top}\neq\bm{0}_{k+1}}\big\|\widetilde{\bm{P}}_{j_{1}}\bm{Z}\widetilde{\bm{P}}_{j_{2}}\bm{Z}\cdots\bm{Z}\widetilde{\bm{P}}_{j_{k+1}}\big\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\quad\leq\left(\frac{8}{\sigma_{r^{\prime}}^{\star 2}-\sigma_{r^{\prime}+1}^{\star 2}}\right)^{k}\cdot(r+1)^{k+1}\cdot 2C_{2}\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{m_{1}}}\left(2C_{2}\left(\sqrt{m_{1}}\omega_{\sf max}\log m\cdot\sigma_{\overline{r}+1}^{\star}+\left(\sqrt{m_{1}m_{2}}+m_{1}\right)\omega_{\sf max}^{2}\log^{2}m\right)\right)^{k}

\displaystyle\quad\leq 4C_{2}\sqrt{\frac{\mu r^{3}}{m_{1}}}\left(\frac{32C_{2}r\left(\sqrt{m_{1}}\omega_{\sf max}\log m\cdot\sigma_{\overline{r}+1}^{\star}+\left(\sqrt{m_{1}m_{2}}+m_{1}\right)\omega_{\sf max}^{2}\log^{2}m\right)}{\sigma_{r^{\prime}}^{\star 2}-\sigma_{r^{\prime}+1}^{\star 2}}\right)^{k}.

(117)

Recalling that
\sigma_{r^{\prime}}^{\star}\geq\sigma_{\overline{r}}^{\star}\geq C_{0}r[(m_{1}m_{2})^{1/4}+rm_{1}^{1/2}]\log m
, we know from (
104
) that there exists some large constant
C>0
such that

\displaystyle\left(\frac{32C_{2}r\left(\sqrt{m_{1}}\omega_{\sf max}\log m\cdot\sigma_{\overline{r}+1}^{\star}+\left(\sqrt{m_{1}m_{2}}+m_{1}\right)\omega_{\sf max}^{2}\log^{2}m\right)}{\sigma_{r^{\prime}}^{\star 2}-\sigma_{r^{\prime}+1}^{\star 2}}\right)^{k-1}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\leq\left(\frac{32C_{2}r\left(\sqrt{m_{1}}\omega_{\sf max}\log m\cdot\sigma_{\overline{r}+1}^{\star}+\left(\sqrt{m_{1}m_{2}}+m_{1}\right)\omega_{\sf max}^{2}\log^{2}m\right)}{\sigma_{r^{\prime}}^{\star 2}/(4r)}\right)^{k-1}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\leq\left(\frac{1}{C^{2}}\right)^{k-1}\leq\frac{1}{C^{k}}.

(118)

For any
k\geq\lfloor\log m\rfloor+1
, in view of (
107
) and the the previous inequality, we have

\displaystyle\left(\frac{8}{\sigma_{r^{\prime}}^{\star 2}-\sigma_{r^{\prime}+1}^{\star 2}}\right)^{k}\sum_{0\leq j_{1},\dots,j_{k+1}\leq r\atop\left(j_{1},...,j_{k+1}\right)^{\top}\neq\bm{0}_{k+1}}\big\|\widetilde{\bm{P}}_{j_{1}}\bm{Z}\widetilde{\bm{P}}_{j_{2}}\bm{Z}\cdots\bm{Z}\widetilde{\bm{P}}_{j_{k+1}}\big\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\quad\leq\left(\frac{8}{\sigma_{r^{\prime}}^{\star 2}-\sigma_{r^{\prime}+1}^{\star 2}}\right)^{k}\cdot(r+1)^{k+1}\left\|\bm{Z}\right\|^{k}

\displaystyle\quad\leq 2r\cdot\left(\frac{16rC_{2}\left(\sqrt{m_{1}}\omega_{\sf max}\log m\cdot\sigma_{\overline{r}+1}^{\star}+\left(\sqrt{m_{1}m_{2}}+m_{1}\right)\omega_{\sf max}^{2}\log^{2}m\right)}{\sigma_{r^{\prime}}^{\star 2}-\sigma_{r^{\prime}+1}^{\star 2}}\right)^{k}

\displaystyle\quad\leq\frac{2r}{C^{k}}\cdot\frac{16rC_{2}\left(\sqrt{m_{1}}\omega_{\sf max}\log m\cdot\sigma_{\overline{r}+1}^{\star}+\left(\sqrt{m_{1}m_{2}}+m_{1}\right)\omega_{\sf max}^{2}\log^{2}m\right)}{\sigma_{r^{\prime}}^{\star 2}-\sigma_{r^{\prime}+1}^{\star 2}}.

(119)

Combining (
C.2
), (
C.2
), (
C.2
) and (
C.2
), one can obtain

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{U}_{:,1:r^{\prime}}^{\sf oracle}\bm{U}_{:,1:r^{\prime}}^{\sf oracle\top}-\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{:,1:r^{\prime}}\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{:,1:r^{\prime}}^{\top}\right\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\leq\sum_{1\leq k\leq\log m}4C_{2}\sqrt{\frac{\mu r^{3}}{m_{1}}}\left(\frac{32C_{2}r\left(\sqrt{m_{1}}\omega_{\sf max}\log m\cdot\sigma_{\overline{r}+1}^{\star}+\left(\sqrt{m_{1}m_{2}}+m_{1}\right)\omega_{\sf max}^{2}\log^{2}m\right)}{\sigma_{r^{\prime}}^{\star 2}-\sigma_{r^{\prime}+1}^{\star 2}}\right)^{k}

\displaystyle\hskip 36.98866pt+\sum_{k\geq\lfloor\log m\rfloor+1}\frac{2r}{C^{k}}\cdot\frac{16C_{2}r\left(\sqrt{m_{1}}\omega_{\sf max}\log m\cdot\sigma_{\overline{r}+1}^{\star}+\left(\sqrt{m_{1}m_{2}}+m_{1}\right)\omega_{\sf max}^{2}\log^{2}m\right)}{\sigma_{r^{\prime}}^{\star 2}-\sigma_{r^{\prime}+1}^{\star 2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{\mu r^{3}}{m_{1}}}\frac{r\left(\sqrt{m_{1}}\omega_{\sf max}\log m\cdot\sigma_{\overline{r}+1}^{\star}+\left(\sqrt{m_{1}m_{2}}+m_{1}\right)\omega_{\sf max}^{2}\log^{2}m\right)}{\sigma_{r^{\prime}}^{\star 2}-\sigma_{r^{\prime}+1}^{\star 2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{\mu r^{3}}{m_{1}}}\left(\frac{r^{2}\sqrt{m_{1}}\omega_{\sf max}\log m}{\sigma_{r^{\prime}}^{\star}}+\frac{r^{2}\left(\sqrt{m_{1}m_{2}}+m_{1}\right)\omega_{\sf max}^{2}\log^{2}m}{\sigma_{r^{\prime}}^{\star 2}}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\asymp\sqrt{\frac{\mu r^{3}}{m_{1}}}\left(\frac{r^{2}\sqrt{m_{1}}\omega_{\sf max}\log m}{\sigma_{r^{\prime}}^{\star}}+\frac{r^{2}\sqrt{m_{1}m_{2}}\omega_{\sf max}^{2}\log^{2}m}{\sigma_{r^{\prime}}^{\star 2}}\right).

(120)

Here, the second last line holds due to (
104
) and the the last line makes use of the inequality

\displaystyle\frac{r^{2}m_{1}\omega_{\sf max}^{2}\log^{2}m}{\sigma_{r^{\prime}}^{\star 2}}=r^{2}\left(\frac{\sqrt{m_{1}}\omega_{\sf max}\log m}{\sigma_{r^{\prime}}^{\star}}\right)^{2}\lesssim r^{2}\frac{\sqrt{m_{1}}\omega_{\sf max}\log m}{\sigma_{r^{\prime}}^{\star}},

provided that
\sigma_{r^{\prime}}^{\star}\gtrsim\sqrt{m_{1}}\omega_{\sf max}\log m
.

Step 6: bounding
\|\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{:,1:r^{\prime}}\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{:,1:r^{\prime}}^{\top}-\bm{U}_{:,1:r^{\prime}}^{\star}\bm{U}_{:,1:r^{\prime}}^{\star\top}\|_{2,\infty}
.

To control
\|\bm{U}_{:,1:r^{\prime}}^{\sf oracle}\bm{U}_{:,1:r^{\prime}}^{\sf oracle\top}-\bm{U}_{:,1:r^{\prime}}^{\star}\bm{U}_{:,1:r^{\prime}}^{\star\top}\|_{2,\infty}
, one still needs to bound
\|\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{:,1:r^{\prime}}\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{:,1:r^{\prime}}^{\top}-\bm{U}_{:,1:r^{\prime}}^{\star}\bm{U}_{:,1:r^{\prime}}^{\star\top}\|_{2,\infty}
. Recall that
\widetilde{\bm{U}}^{(1)}\widetilde{\bm{\Sigma}}^{(1)}\widetilde{\bm{W}}^{(1)\top}
is the SVD of
\bm{U}^{\star(1)}\bm{\Sigma}^{\star(1)}+\bm{E}\bm{V}^{\star(1)}=\bm{U}_{:,1:\overline{r}}^{\star}\bm{\Sigma}^{\star}_{1:\overline{r},1:\overline{r}}+\bm{E}\bm{V}_{:,1:\overline{r}}
and
\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{:,1:r^{\prime}}
(resp.
\bm{U}_{:,1:r^{\prime}}^{\star}
) is the matrix containing the first
r^{\prime}
columns of
\widetilde{\bm{U}}^{(1)}
(resp.
\bm{U}^{\star(1)}
). We make the observation that

\displaystyle\mathcal{P}_{\left(\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{:,1:r^{\prime}}\right)_{\perp}}\bm{U}_{:,1:r^{\prime}}^{\star}

\displaystyle\quad=\mathcal{P}_{\left(\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{:,1:r^{\prime}}\right)_{\perp}}\left(\bm{U}_{:,1:r^{\prime}}^{\star}\bm{\Sigma}^{\star}_{1:r^{\prime},1:\overline{r}}\right)\left(\bm{I}_{r^{\prime}}\ \bm{0}_{r^{\prime}\times(\overline{r}-r^{\prime})}\right)^{\top}\left(\bm{\Sigma}^{\star}_{1:r^{\prime},1:r^{\prime}}\right)^{-1}

\displaystyle\quad=\mathcal{P}_{\left(\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{:,1:r^{\prime}}\right)_{\perp}}\left(\widetilde{\bm{U}}^{(1)}\widetilde{\bm{\Sigma}}^{(1)}\widetilde{\bm{W}}^{(1)\top}-\bm{E}\bm{V}^{\star(1)}-\bm{U}_{:,r^{\prime}+1:\overline{r}}^{\star}\bm{\Sigma}^{\star}_{r^{\prime}+1:\overline{r},1:\overline{r}}\right)\left(\bm{I}_{r^{\prime}}\ \bm{0}_{r^{\prime}\times(\overline{r}-r^{\prime})}\right)^{\top}\left(\bm{\Sigma}^{\star}_{1:r^{\prime},1:r^{\prime}}\right)^{-1}

\displaystyle\quad=\mathcal{P}_{\left(\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{:,1:r^{\prime}}\right)_{\perp}}\left(\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{:,1:r^{\prime}}^{(1)}\widetilde{\bm{\Sigma}}_{1:r^{\prime},1:r^{\prime}}^{(1)}\widetilde{\bm{W}}_{:,1:r^{\prime}}^{(1)\top}+\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{:,r^{\prime}+1:\overline{r}}^{(1)}\widetilde{\bm{\Sigma}}_{r^{\prime}+1:\overline{r},r^{\prime}+1:\overline{r}}^{(1)}\widetilde{\bm{W}}_{:,r^{\prime}+1:\overline{r}}^{(1)\top}-\bm{E}\bm{V}^{\star(1)}\right)\left(\bm{I}_{r^{\prime}}\ \bm{0}_{r^{\prime}\times(\overline{r}-r^{\prime})}\right)^{\top}

\displaystyle\qquad\cdot\left(\bm{\Sigma}^{\star}_{1:r^{\prime},1:r^{\prime}}\right)^{-1}

\displaystyle\quad=\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{:,r^{\prime}+1:\overline{r}}^{(1)}\widetilde{\bm{\Sigma}}_{r^{\prime}+1:\overline{r},r^{\prime}+1:\overline{r}}^{(1)}\widetilde{\bm{W}}_{:,r^{\prime}+1:\overline{r}}^{(1)\top}\left(\bm{I}_{r^{\prime}}\ \bm{0}_{r^{\prime}\times(\overline{r}-r^{\prime})}\right)^{\top}\left(\bm{\Sigma}^{\star}_{1:r^{\prime},1:r^{\prime}}\right)^{-1}

\displaystyle\qquad-\mathcal{P}_{\left(\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{:,1:r^{\prime}}\right)_{\perp}}\bm{E}\bm{V}^{\star(1)}\left(\bm{I}_{r^{\prime}}\ \bm{0}_{r^{\prime}\times(\overline{r}-r^{\prime})}\right)^{\top}\left(\bm{\Sigma}^{\star}_{1:r^{\prime},1:r^{\prime}}\right)^{-1},

(121)

where the second identity is valid since

\bm{\Sigma}^{\star}_{r^{\prime}+1:\overline{r},1:\overline{r}}\left(\bm{I}_{r^{\prime}}\ \bm{0}_{r^{\prime}\times(\overline{r}-r^{\prime})}\right)^{\top}=\left(\bm{0}_{(\overline{r}-r^{\prime})\times r^{\prime}}\ \bm{\Sigma}^{\star}_{r^{\prime}+1:\overline{r},r^{\prime}+1:\overline{r}}\right)\left(\bm{I}_{r^{\prime}}\ \bm{0}_{r^{\prime}\times(\overline{r}-r^{\prime})}\right)^{\top}=\bm{0}_{(\overline{r}-r^{\prime})\times r^{\prime}}

and the last line comes from
\mathcal{P}_{(\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{:,1:r^{\prime}})_{\perp}}\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{:,1:r^{\prime}}^{(1)}=\bm{0}
and
\mathcal{P}_{(\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{:,1:r^{\prime}})_{\perp}}\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{:,r^{\prime}+1:\overline{r}}^{(1)}=\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{:,r^{\prime}+1:\overline{r}}^{(1)}
. Note that
\widetilde{\bm{W}}_{:,1:r^{\prime}}^{(1)}
(resp.
\left(\bm{I}_{r^{\prime}}\ \bm{0}\right)^{\top}
) is the leading
r^{\prime}
right singular space of
\bm{U}^{\star(1)}\bm{\Sigma}^{\star(1)}+\bm{E}\bm{V}^{\star(1)}
(resp.
\bm{U}^{\star(1)}\bm{\Sigma}^{\star(1)}
) and

\displaystyle\sigma_{r^{\prime}}^{\star}-\sigma_{r^{\prime}+1}^{\star}\geq\frac{1}{4r}\sigma_{r^{\prime}}^{\star}\geq\frac{C_{0}}{4}\big[(m_{1}m_{2})^{1/4}+rm_{1}^{1/2}\big]\omega_{\sf max}\log m\gg r\sqrt{m}_{1}\omega_{\sf max}\log m.

Chen et al., 2021a
and Lemma
4
taken together imply that

\displaystyle\left\|\widetilde{\bm{W}}_{:,r^{\prime}+1:\overline{r}}^{(1)\top}\left(\bm{I}_{r^{\prime}}\ \bm{0}_{r^{\prime}\times(\overline{r}-r^{\prime})}\right)^{\top}\right\|

\displaystyle=\left\|\big(\widetilde{\bm{W}}_{:,1:r^{\prime}}\big)_{\perp}^{(1)\top}\left(\bm{I}_{r^{\prime}}\ \bm{0}_{r^{\prime}\times(\overline{r}-r^{\prime})}\right)^{\top}\right\|\lesssim\frac{\left\|\bm{E}\bm{V}^{\star(1)}\right\|}{\sigma_{r^{\prime}}^{\star}-\sigma_{r^{\prime}+1}^{\star}}\lesssim\frac{r\sqrt{m}_{1}\omega_{\sf max}\log m}{\sigma_{r^{\prime}}^{\star}},

(122a)

\displaystyle\left\|\big(\bm{U}_{:,1:r^{\prime}}^{\star}\big)_{\perp}^{\top}\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{:,1:r^{\prime}}\right\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\left\|\bm{E}\bm{V}^{\star(1)}\right\|}{\sigma_{r^{\prime}}^{\star}-\sigma_{r^{\prime}+1}^{\star}}\lesssim\frac{r\sqrt{m}_{1}\omega_{\sf max}\log m}{\sigma_{r^{\prime}}^{\star}}.

(122b)

Moreover, combining (
90a
) and the assumption
\sigma_{\overline{r}}^{\star}\geq C_{0}r[(m_{1}m_{2})^{1/4}+rm_{1}^{1/2}]\omega_{\sf max}\log m
gives

\displaystyle\widetilde{\sigma}_{i}\leq\sigma_{i}^{\star}+\left\|\bm{E}\bm{V}^{\star}\right\|\leq 2\sigma_{i}^{\star},~\qquad~\forall i\in\left[\overline{r}\right].

(123)

Inequality (
90d
) combined with (
122a
) and (
123
) gives

\displaystyle\left\|\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{:,r^{\prime}+1:\overline{r}}^{(1)}\widetilde{\bm{\Sigma}}_{r^{\prime}+1:\overline{r},r^{\prime}+1:\overline{r}}^{(1)}\widetilde{\bm{W}}_{:,r^{\prime}+1:\overline{r}}^{(1)\top}\left(\bm{I}_{r^{\prime}}\ \bm{0}_{r^{\prime}\times(\overline{r}-r^{\prime})}\right)^{\top}\left(\bm{\Sigma}^{\star}_{1:r^{\prime},1:r^{\prime}}\right)^{-1}\right\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\quad\leq\big\|\widetilde{\bm{U}}^{(1)}\big\|_{2,\infty}\big\|\widetilde{\bm{\Sigma}}_{r^{\prime}+1:\overline{r},r^{\prime}+1:\overline{r}}^{(1)}\big\|\left\|\widetilde{\bm{W}}_{:,r^{\prime}+1:\overline{r}}^{(1)\top}\left(\bm{I}_{r^{\prime}}\ \bm{0}_{r^{\prime}\times(\overline{r}-r^{\prime})}\right)^{\top}\right\|\left\|\left(\bm{\Sigma}^{\star}_{1:r^{\prime},1:r^{\prime}}\right)^{-1}\right\|

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim 2\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{m_{1}}}\cdot\widetilde{\sigma}_{r^{\prime}+1}\cdot\frac{r\sqrt{m}_{1}\omega_{\sf max}\log m}{\sigma_{r^{\prime}}^{\star}}\cdot\frac{1}{\sigma_{r^{\prime}}^{\star}}

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim 2\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{m_{1}}}\cdot 2\sigma_{r^{\prime}+1}^{\star}\cdot\frac{r\sqrt{m}_{1}\omega_{\sf max}\log m}{\sigma_{r^{\prime}}^{\star}}\cdot\frac{1}{\sigma_{r^{\prime}}^{\star}}

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{m_{1}}}\frac{r\sqrt{m}_{1}\omega_{\sf max}\log m}{\sigma_{r^{\prime}}^{\star}}.

(124)

In addition, Lemma
2
and (
90d
) taken together imply that

\displaystyle\left\|\mathcal{P}_{\left(\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{:,1:r^{\prime}}\right)_{\perp}}\bm{E}\bm{V}^{\star(1)}\left(\bm{I}_{r^{\prime}}\ \bm{0}_{r^{\prime}\times(\overline{r}-r^{\prime})}\right)^{\top}\left(\bm{\Sigma}^{\star}_{1:r^{\prime},1:r^{\prime}}\right)^{-1}\right\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\quad\leq\frac{1}{\sigma_{r^{\prime}}^{\star}}\left\|\mathcal{P}_{\left(\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{:,1:r^{\prime}}\right)_{\perp}}\bm{E}\bm{V}^{\star(1)}\right\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\quad\leq\frac{1}{\sigma_{r^{\prime}}^{\star}}\left(\big\|\bm{E}\bm{V}^{\star(1)}\big\|_{2,\infty}+\left\|\mathcal{P}_{\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{:,1:r^{\prime}}}\bm{E}\bm{V}^{\star(1)}\right\|_{2,\infty}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\leq\frac{1}{\sigma_{r^{\prime}}^{\star}}\left(\big\|\bm{E}\bm{V}^{\star(1)}\big\|_{2,\infty}+\big\|\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{:,1:r^{\prime}}\big\|_{2,\infty}\big\|\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{:,1:r^{\prime}}\big\|\big\|\bm{E}\bm{V}^{\star(1)}\big\|\right)

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim\frac{1}{\sigma_{r^{\prime}}^{\star}}\left(\sqrt{\mu r}\omega_{\sf max}\log n+2\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{m_{1}}}\cdot\sqrt{m_{1}}\omega_{\sf max}\log n\right)

\displaystyle\quad\asymp\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{m_{1}}}\frac{\sqrt{m}_{1}\omega_{\sf max}\log m}{\sigma_{r^{\prime}}^{\star}}.

(125)

Taking (
C.2
), (
C.2
) and (
C.2
) together gives

\displaystyle\left\|\mathcal{P}_{\left(\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{:,1:r^{\prime}}\right)_{\perp}}\bm{U}_{:,1:r^{\prime}}^{\star}\right\|_{2,\infty}\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{m_{1}}}\frac{r\sqrt{m}_{1}\omega_{\sf max}\log m}{\sigma_{r^{\prime}}^{\star}}.

The previous inequality taken together with (
122b
) and (
90d
) reveals that

\displaystyle\big\|\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{:,1:r^{\prime}}\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{:,1:r^{\prime}}^{\top}-\bm{U}_{:,1:r^{\prime}}^{\star}\bm{U}_{:,1:r^{\prime}}^{\star\top}\big\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\leq\big\|\big(\bm{U}_{:,1:r^{\prime}}^{\star}-\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{:,1:r^{\prime}}\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{:,1:r^{\prime}}^{\top}\bm{U}_{:,1:r^{\prime}}^{\star}\big)\bm{U}_{:,1:r^{\prime}}^{\star\top}\big\|_{2,\infty}+\big\|\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{:,1:r^{\prime}}\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{:,1:r^{\prime}}^{\top}\bm{U}_{:,1:r^{\prime}}^{\star}\bm{U}_{:,1:r^{\prime}}^{\star\top}-\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{:,1:r^{\prime}}\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{:,1:r^{\prime}}^{\top}\big\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\leq\left\|\left(\mathcal{P}_{\left(\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{:,1:r^{\prime}}\right)_{\perp}}\bm{U}_{:,1:r^{\prime}}^{\star}\right)\bm{U}_{:,1:r^{\prime}}^{\star\top}\right\|_{2,\infty}+\big\|\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{:,1:r^{\prime}}\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{:,1:r^{\prime}}^{\top}\left(\bm{U}_{:,1:r^{\prime}}^{\star}\right)_{\perp}\left(\bm{U}_{:,1:r^{\prime}}^{\star}\right)_{\perp}^{\top}\big\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\leq\left\|\mathcal{P}_{\left(\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{:,1:r^{\prime}}\right)_{\perp}}\bm{U}_{:,1:r^{\prime}}^{\star}\right\|_{2,\infty}+\big\|\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{:,1:r^{\prime}}\big\|_{2,\infty}\left\|\big(\bm{U}_{:,1:r^{\prime}}^{\star}\big)_{\perp}^{\top}\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{:,1:r^{\prime}}\right\|

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{m_{1}}}\frac{r\sqrt{m}_{1}\omega_{\sf max}\log m}{\sigma_{r^{\prime}}^{\star}}+2\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{m_{1}}}\frac{r\sqrt{m}_{1}\omega_{\sf max}\log m}{\sigma_{r^{\prime}}^{\star}}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\asymp\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{m_{1}}}\frac{r\sqrt{m}_{1}\omega_{\sf max}\log m}{\sigma_{r^{\prime}}^{\star}}.

(126)

By virtue of (
C.2
) and (
C.2
), we arrive at

\displaystyle\big\|\bm{U}_{:,1:r^{\prime}}^{\sf oracle}\bm{U}_{:,1:r^{\prime}}^{\sf oracle\top}-\bm{U}_{:,1:r^{\prime}}^{\star}\bm{U}_{:,1:r^{\prime}}^{\star\top}\big\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\quad\leq\big\|\bm{U}_{:,1:r^{\prime}}^{\sf oracle}\bm{U}_{:,1:r^{\prime}}^{\sf oracle\top}-\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{:,1:r^{\prime}}\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{:,1:r^{\prime}}^{\top}\big\|_{2,\infty}+\big\|\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{:,1:r^{\prime}}\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{:,1:r^{\prime}}^{\top}-\bm{U}_{:,1:r^{\prime}}^{\star}\bm{U}_{:,1:r^{\prime}}^{\star\top}\big\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{\mu r^{3}}{m_{1}}}\left(\frac{r^{2}\sqrt{m_{1}}\omega_{\sf max}\log m}{\sigma_{r^{\prime}}^{\star}}+\frac{r^{2}\sqrt{m_{1}m_{2}}\omega_{\sf max}^{2}\log^{2}m}{\sigma_{r^{\prime}}^{\star 2}}\right).

(127)

∎

C.3
Proof of Lemma
1

Denote by
\gamma_{1}
the following counterclockwise contour on the complex plane:

\displaystyle\gamma_{1}=\bigg\{x+y\rm{i}

\displaystyle:x=\frac{\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}}+\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}}{2},-\frac{\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}}{2}\leq y\leq\frac{\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}}+\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}}{2},

\displaystyle\quad\text{or }x=\overline{\lambda}_{1}+\frac{\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}}{2},-\frac{\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}}{2}\leq y\leq\frac{\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}}+\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}}{2},

\displaystyle\quad\text{or }y=\pm\frac{\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}}{2},\frac{\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}}+\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}}{2}\leq x\leq\overline{\lambda}_{1}+\frac{\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}}{2}\bigg\}.

Then
\{\overline{\lambda}_{i}\}_{i=1}^{r_{1}}
lie inside the contour
\gamma_{1}
and
\{\overline{\lambda}_{i}\}_{i=r_{1}+1}^{n}
(where
\overline{\lambda}_{i}=0
for
i\geq r+1
) reside outside
\gamma_{1}
. Moreover, for any
\eta\in\gamma_{1}
and
1\leq i\leq n
,
one has

\displaystyle\left|\eta-\overline{\lambda}_{i}\right|\leq\frac{\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}}{2}.

(128)

Step 1: decompose
\bm{U}_{1}\bm{U}_{1}^{\top}-\overline{\bm{U}}_{1}\overline{\bm{U}}_{1}^{\top}
.

First, we invoke a similar argument as in
Xia, 2021
to express
\bm{U}_{1}\bm{U}_{1}^{\top}-\overline{\bm{U}}_{1}\overline{\bm{U}}_{1}^{\top}
as an infinite sum. Denote by
\lambda_{1}\geq\cdots\geq\lambda_{n}
the eigenvalues of
\bm{M}
. Apply Weyl’s inequality to obtain

\displaystyle\max_{1\leq i\leq r}\left|\lambda_{i}-\overline{\lambda}_{i}\right|\leq\left\|\bm{Z}\right\|<\frac{\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}}{2}.

As a result, we know that
\left\{\lambda_{i}\right\}_{i=1}^{r_{1}}
are inside the contour
\gamma_{1}
, and
\left\{\lambda_{i}\right\}_{i=r_{1}+1}^{n}
are outside the contour. Similar to Eqn. (10) in
Xia, 2021
, one has

\displaystyle\bm{U}_{1}\bm{U}_{1}^{\top}=\frac{1}{2\pi\rm{i}}\oint_{\gamma_{1}}\left(\eta\bm{I}-\bm{M}\right)\rm{d}\eta.

(129)

We define

\mathcal{R}_{\overline{\bm{M}}}(\eta):=\left(\eta\bm{I}-\overline{\bm{M}}\right)^{-1}=\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{1}{\eta-\overline{\lambda}_{j}}\overline{\bm{u}}_{j}\overline{\bm{u}}_{j}^{\top}.

In view of (
128
), for any
\eta\in\gamma_{1}
, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\mathcal{R}_{\overline{\bm{M}}}(\eta)\right\|\leq\frac{2}{\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}},

and consequently,

\displaystyle\left\|\mathcal{R}_{\overline{\bm{M}}}(\eta)\bm{Z}\right\|\leq\left\|\mathcal{R}_{\overline{\bm{M}}}(\eta)\right\|\left\|\bm{Z}\right\|\leq\frac{2\left\|\bm{Z}\right\|}{\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}}<1.

A similar argument in
Xia, 2021
yields

\displaystyle\bm{U}_{1}\bm{U}_{1}^{\top}-\overline{\bm{U}}_{1}\overline{\bm{U}}_{1}^{\top}

\displaystyle=\sum_{k\geq 1}\frac{1}{2\pi\rm{i}}\oint_{\gamma_{1}}\left[\mathcal{R}_{\overline{\bm{M}}}(\eta)\bm{Z}\right]^{k}\mathcal{R}_{\overline{\bm{M}}}(\eta)\rm{d}\eta

\displaystyle=\sum_{k\geq 1}\sum_{1\leq j_{1},\dots,j_{k+1}\leq n}\frac{1}{2\pi\rm{i}}\oint_{\gamma_{1}}\frac{\rm{d}\eta}{\left(\eta-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{1}}\right)\cdots\left(\eta-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{k+1}}\right)}\bm{P}_{\overline{\bm{u}}_{j_{1}}}\bm{Z}\bm{P}_{\overline{\bm{u}}_{j_{2}}}\bm{Z}\cdots\bm{P}_{\overline{\bm{u}}_{j_{k}}}\bm{Z}\bm{P}_{\overline{\bm{u}}_{j_{k+1}}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{k\geq 1}\sum_{0\leq j_{1},\dots,j_{k+1}\leq r}\frac{1}{2\pi\rm{i}}\oint_{\gamma_{1}}\frac{\rm{d}\eta}{\left(\eta-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{1}}\right)\cdots\left(\eta-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{k+1}}\right)}\overline{\bm{P}}_{j_{1}}\bm{Z}\overline{\bm{P}}_{j_{2}}\bm{Z}\cdots\overline{\bm{P}}_{j_{k}}\bm{Z}\overline{\bm{P}}_{j_{k+1}}.

(130)

Here, we define
\overline{\lambda}_{0}=0
and the last line holds since
\overline{\lambda}_{i}=0
for all
i\geq r+1
and
\overline{\bm{P}}_{0}=\overline{\bm{U}}_{\perp}\overline{\bm{U}}_{\perp}^{\top}=\sum_{i=r+1}^{n}\overline{\bm{u}}_{i}\overline{\bm{u}}_{i}^{\top}
.

Step 2: bounding
\|\bm{U}_{1}\bm{U}_{1}^{\top}-\overline{\bm{U}}_{1}\overline{\bm{U}}_{1}^{\top}\|_{2,\infty}
.

By virtue of (
C.3
) and the triangle inequality, we see that: to prove (
87a
), it suffices to bound
|\frac{1}{2\pi\rm{i}}\oint_{\gamma_{1}}\frac{\rm{d}\eta}{(\eta-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{1}})\cdots(\eta-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{k+1}})}|
. We consider two scenarios: all of
j_{1},\dots,j_{k+1}
lie in the set
\{0\}\cup\{r_{1}+1,\dots,r_{1}\}
, and at least one of
j_{1},\dots,j_{k+1}
is in the set
\{1,\dots,r_{1}\}
.

Case 1: all of
j_{1},\dots,j_{k+1}
are either
0
or larger than
r_{1}
.

In this case, none of
\overline{\lambda}_{j_{1}},\dots,\overline{\lambda}_{j_{k+1}}
is inside
\gamma_{1}
and as a result,
f(\eta)=\frac{1}{(\eta-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{1}})\cdots(\eta-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{k+1}})}
is analytic within and on
\gamma_{1}
. Cauchy’s integral theorem tells us that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2\pi\rm{i}}\oint_{\gamma_{1}}\frac{\rm{d}\eta}{(\eta-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{1}})\cdots(\eta-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{k+1}})}=0,

(131)

and thus we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2\pi\rm{i}}\oint_{\gamma_{1}}\frac{\rm{d}\eta}{\left(\eta-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{1}}\right)\cdots\left(\eta-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{k+1}}\right)}\overline{\bm{P}}_{j_{1}}\bm{Z}\overline{\bm{P}}_{j_{2}}\bm{Z}\cdots\overline{\bm{P}}_{j_{k}}\bm{Z}\overline{\bm{P}}_{j_{k+1}}=\bm{0}.

(132)

Case 2: at least one of
j_{1},\dots,j_{k+1}
is between
1
and
r_{1}
.

Let

\displaystyle\mathcal{J}=\{j:1\leq j\leq r_{1},\exists 1\leq\ell\leq k+1\text{ s.t. }j_{\ell}=j\},

(133)

and let
j_{\sf max}
and
j_{\sf min}
denote the largest and smallest elements in
\mathcal{J}
, respectively. We define the following counterclockwise rectangular contour:

\displaystyle\gamma_{2}=\bigg\{x+y\rm{i}
