\displaystyle:x=\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf max}}-\frac{\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}}{2},-\frac{\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}}{2}\leq y\leq\frac{\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}}{2},

\displaystyle\quad\text{or }x=\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf min}}+\frac{\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}}{2},-\frac{\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}}{2}\leq y\leq\frac{\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}}{2},

\displaystyle\quad\text{or }y=\pm\frac{\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}}{2},\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf max}}-\frac{\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}}{2}\leq x\leq\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf min}}+\frac{\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}}{2}\bigg\}.

It is easy to verify that

\displaystyle\left|\eta-\lambda_{j_{\ell}}\right|\geq\frac{\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}}{2},~\qquad~\forall 1\leq\ell\leq k+1,\eta\in\gamma_{2}

(134)

and

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2\pi\rm{i}}\oint_{\gamma_{1}}\frac{\rm{d}\eta}{\left(\eta-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{1}}\right)\cdots\left(\eta-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{k+1}}\right)}=\frac{1}{2\pi\rm{i}}\oint_{\gamma_{2}}\frac{\rm{d}\eta}{\left(\eta-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{1}}\right)\cdots\left(\eta-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{k+1}}\right)}.

(135)

Moreover, the length of
\gamma_{2}
is

L(\gamma_{2})=2\left(\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf min}}-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf max}}\right)+4\left(\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}\right).

If
j_{\sf max}=j_{\sf min}
, i.e., there is only one element in
\mathcal{I}
, then applying the triangle inequality for contour integrals yields

\displaystyle\left|\frac{1}{2\pi\rm{i}}\oint_{\gamma_{2}}\frac{\rm{d}\eta}{\left(\eta-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{1}}\right)\cdots\left(\eta-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{k+1}}\right)}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2\pi}\sup_{\eta\in\gamma_{2}}\left|\frac{1}{\left(\eta-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{1}}\right)\cdots\left(\eta-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{k+1}}\right)}\right|\cdot L(\gamma_{2})

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\eqref{ineq48}}}{{\leq}}\frac{1}{2\pi}\left(\frac{2}{\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}}\right)^{k+1}\cdot 4\left(\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4}{\pi}\left(\frac{2}{\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}}\right)^{k}.

If
j_{\sf max}\neq j_{\sf min}
, then the triangle inequality tells us that for any
\eta\in\gamma_{2}
,

\displaystyle\max\left\{\left|\eta-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf max}}\right|,\left|\eta-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf min}}\right|\right\}\geq\frac{\left|\left(\eta-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf max}}\right)-\left(\eta-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf min}}\right)\right|}{2}=\frac{\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf min}}-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf max}}}{2}.

(136)

In view of (
134
), (
136
) and the basic inequality
\min\{\frac{a}{b},\frac{c}{d}\}\leq\frac{a+c}{b+d}
for
a,b,c,d>0
, one has

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\left|\left(\eta-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf max}}\right)\left(\eta-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf min}}\right)\right|}

\displaystyle\leq\min\left\{\frac{4}{\left(\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf min}}-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf max}}\right)\left(\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}\right)},\left(\frac{2}{\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}}\right)^{2}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{2}{\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}}\right)\cdot\left(\frac{4}{\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}+\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf max}}-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf min}}}\right)

(137)

for all
\eta\in\gamma_{2}
, and consequently, one has

\displaystyle\left|\frac{1}{2\pi\rm{i}}\oint_{\gamma_{2}}\frac{\rm{d}\eta}{\left(\eta-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{1}}\right)\cdots\left(\eta-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{k+1}}\right)}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2\pi}\sup_{\eta\in\gamma_{2}}\left|\frac{1}{\left(\eta-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{1}}\right)\cdots\left(\eta-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{k+1}}\right)}\right|\cdot L(\gamma_{2})

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2\pi}\left(\frac{2}{\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}}\right)\cdot\left(\frac{4}{\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}+\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf min}}-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf min}}}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\cdot\left(\frac{2}{\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}}\right)^{k-1}\cdot\left(2\left(\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf min}}-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf max}}\right)+4\left(\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{8}{\pi}\left(\frac{2}{\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}}\right)^{k}.

Therefore, it is guaranteed that

\displaystyle\left|\frac{1}{2\pi\rm{i}}\oint_{\gamma_{2}}\frac{\rm{d}\eta}{\left(\eta-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{1}}\right)\cdots\left(\eta-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{k+1}}\right)}\right|\leq\frac{8}{\pi}\left(\frac{2}{\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}}\right)^{k}.

(138)

Combining (
C.3
), (
132
), (
138
) and the triangle inequality finishes the proof of (
87a
).

Step 3: bounding
\|(\overline{\bm{U}}_{1}\overline{\bm{U}}_{1}^{\top}-\bm{U}_{1}\bm{U}_{1}^{\top})\overline{\bm{M}}\|_{2,\infty}
.

Next, we move on to control
\|(\overline{\bm{U}}_{1}\overline{\bm{U}}_{1}^{\top}-\bm{U}_{1}\bm{U}_{1}^{\top})\overline{\bm{M}}\|_{2,\infty}
. By virtue of (
C.3
), we have

\displaystyle\big(\bm{U}_{1}\bm{U}_{1}^{\top}-\overline{\bm{U}}_{1}\overline{\bm{U}}_{1}^{\top}\big)\overline{\bm{M}}

\displaystyle\quad=\sum_{k\geq 1}\sum_{0\leq j_{1},\dots,j_{k}\leq r}\sum_{j_{k+1}=1}^{r}\frac{1}{2\pi\rm{i}}\oint_{\gamma_{1}}\frac{\rm{d}\eta}{\left(\eta-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{1}}\right)\cdots\left(\eta-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{k+1}}\right)}\overline{\bm{P}}_{j_{1}}\bm{Z}\overline{\bm{P}}_{j_{2}}\bm{Z}\cdots\overline{\bm{P}}_{j_{k}}\bm{Z}\overline{\bm{P}}_{j_{k+1}}\overline{\bm{M}}

\displaystyle\quad=\sum_{k\geq 1}\sum_{0\leq j_{1},\dots,j_{k}\leq r}\sum_{j_{k+1}=1}^{r}\frac{1}{2\pi\rm{i}}\oint_{\gamma_{1}}\frac{\overline{\lambda}_{j_{k+1}}\rm{d}\eta}{\left(\eta-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{1}}\right)\cdots\left(\eta-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{k+1}}\right)}\overline{\bm{P}}_{j_{1}}\bm{Z}\overline{\bm{P}}_{j_{2}}\bm{Z}\cdots\overline{\bm{P}}_{j_{k}}\bm{Z}\overline{\bm{P}}_{j_{k+1}}.

(139)

The second line and the third line make use of
\overline{\bm{U}}_{\perp}\overline{\bm{M}}=0
and
\overline{\bm{P}}_{j}\,\overline{\bm{M}}=\overline{\bm{u}}_{j}\,\overline{\bm{u}}_{j}^{\top}(\sum_{i=1}^{r}\overline{\lambda}_{i}\overline{\bm{u}}_{i}\,\overline{\bm{u}}_{i}^{\top})=\overline{\lambda}_{j}
, respectively. In the rest of the proof, we will establish upper bounds for
|\frac{1}{2\pi\rm{i}}\oint_{\gamma_{1}}\frac{\overline{\lambda}_{j_{k+1}}\rm{d}\eta}{(\eta-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{1}})\cdots(\eta-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{k+1}})}|
and justify the validity of (
87b
).

Step 3.1: bounding
|\frac{1}{2\pi\rm{i}}\oint_{\gamma_{1}}\frac{\overline{\lambda}_{j_{k+1}}\rm{d}\eta}{(\eta-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{1}})\cdots(\eta-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{k+1}})}|
for
k=1
.

We consider two scenarios: (1)
1\leq j_{1},j_{2}\leq r_{1}
or
j_{1},j_{2}\in\{r_{1}+1,\dots,r\}\cup\{0\}
and (2) only one of
j_{1}
and
j_{2}
falls into the set
\{1,\dots,r_{1}\}
.

Case 1:
1\leq j_{1},j_{2}\leq r_{1}
or
j_{1},j_{2}\in\{r_{1}+1,\dots,r\}\cup\{0\}
.

In this case, Cauchy’s integral formula asserts that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2\pi\rm{i}}\oint_{\gamma_{1}}\frac{\rm{d}\eta}{\left(\eta-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{1}}\right)\left(\eta-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{2}}\right)}=0,

and consequently,

\displaystyle\left|\frac{1}{2\pi\rm{i}}\oint_{\gamma_{1}}\frac{\overline{\lambda}_{j_{2}}\rm{d}\eta}{(\eta-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{1}})(\eta-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{2}})}\right|=0.

(140)

Case 2: exactly one of
j_{i}\in\{1,\dots,r_{1}\}
.

Apply Cauchy’s integral formula to yield

\displaystyle\left|\frac{1}{2\pi\rm{i}}\oint_{\gamma_{1}}\frac{\rm{d}\eta}{\left(\eta-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{1}}\right)\left(\eta-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{2}}\right)}\right|=\left|\frac{1}{2\pi\rm{i}}\oint_{\gamma_{1}}\frac{\frac{1}{\eta-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{1}}}}{\eta-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{2}}}\rm{d}\eta\right|=\frac{1}{\left|\overline{\lambda}_{j_{1}}-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{2}}\right|}.

Observing that the function
f(x)=\frac{1}{|x-1|}
is increasing on
(-\infty,1)
and decreasing on
(1,\infty)
, one has

\displaystyle\left|\frac{1}{2\pi\rm{i}}\oint_{\gamma_{1}}\frac{\overline{\lambda}_{j_{k+1}}\rm{d}\eta}{(\eta-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{1}})(\eta-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{2}})}\right|=\frac{\overline{\lambda}_{j_{2}}}{\left|\overline{\lambda}_{j_{1}}-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{2}}\right|}=\frac{1}{\left|\frac{\overline{\lambda}_{j_{1}}}{\overline{\lambda}_{j_{2}}}-1\right|}\leq\frac{1}{\max\left\{\frac{\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}}}{\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}}-1,1-\frac{\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}}{\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}}}\right\}}=\frac{\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}}}{\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}}.

(141)

Step 3.2: bounding
|\frac{1}{2\pi\rm{i}}\oint_{\gamma_{1}}\frac{\overline{\lambda}_{j_{k+1}}\rm{d}\eta}{(\eta-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{1}})\cdots(\eta-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{k+1}})}|
for
k>1
.

If (1) all
j_{1},\dots,j_{k+1}
lie in the set
\{1,\dots,r_{1}\}
or (2) all
j_{1},\dots,j_{k+1}
are all in the set
\{0\}\cup\{r_{1}+1,\dots,r\}
, then the function

g(x)=\frac{\overline{\lambda}_{j_{k+1}}}{\left(\eta-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{1}}\right)\cdots\left(\eta-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{k+1}}\right)}

is analytic in and on
\gamma_{1}
or outside on on
\gamma_{1}
. As a result, Cauchy’s integral formula tells us that

\displaystyle\left|\frac{1}{2\pi\rm{i}}\oint_{\gamma_{1}}\frac{\overline{\lambda}_{j_{k+1}}\rm{d}\eta}{\left(\eta-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{1}}\right)\cdots\left(\eta-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{k+1}}\right)}\right|=0.

(142)

In the following proof, we assume that these two cases would not happen, i.e.,

\displaystyle 1\leq\left|\left\{j_{1},\dots,j_{k+1}\right\}\cap\{1,\dots,r_{1}\}\right|\leq k.

(143)

Let
\gamma_{3}
denote the following counterclockwise rectangular contour:

\displaystyle\gamma_{3}=\bigg\{x+y\rm{i}

\displaystyle:x=\frac{\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf max}}+\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}}{2},-\frac{\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf max}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}}{2}\leq y\leq\frac{\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf max}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}}{2},

\displaystyle\quad\text{or }x=\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf min}}+\frac{\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf max}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}}{2},-\frac{\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf max}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}}{2}\leq y\leq\frac{\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf max}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}}{2},

\displaystyle\quad\text{or }y=\pm\frac{\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf max}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}}{2},\frac{\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf max}}+\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}}{2}\leq x\leq\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf min}}+\frac{\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf max}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}}{2}\bigg\},

where we recall that
j_{\sf min}
(resp.
j_{\sf max}
) is the smallest (resp. largest) element in the set
\mathcal{J}
defined in (
133
). Then one can check that

\displaystyle\left|\eta-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\ell}}\right|\geq\frac{\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf max}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}}{2},~\qquad~\forall 1\leq\ell\leq k+1,\eta\in\gamma_{3},

(144)

and the length of
\gamma_{3}
satisfies

\displaystyle L\left(\gamma_{3}\right)=2\left(\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf min}}-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf max}}\right)+4\left(\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf max}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}\right)=2\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf min}}+2\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf max}}-4\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}.

(145)

In addition, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2\pi\rm{i}}\oint_{\gamma_{1}}\frac{\rm{d}\eta}{\left(\eta-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{1}}\right)\cdots\left(\eta-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{k+1}}\right)}=\frac{1}{2\pi\rm{i}}\oint_{\gamma_{3}}\frac{\rm{d}\eta}{\left(\eta-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{1}}\right)\cdots\left(\eta-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{k+1}}\right)}.

(146)

Case 1:
\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf min}}-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf max}}\leq 3(\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf max}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1})
.

In this scenario, one has

\displaystyle\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf min}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}=\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf min}}-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf max}}+\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf max}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}\leq 4\left(\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf max}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}\right),

(147)

which further leads to

\displaystyle L(\gamma_{3})\leq 10\left(\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf max}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}\right).

In view of (
144
), (
146
), (
147
) and the previous inequality, one has

\displaystyle\left|\frac{1}{2\pi\rm{i}}\oint_{\gamma_{1}}\frac{\overline{\lambda}_{j_{k+1}}\rm{d}\eta}{\left(\eta-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{1}}\right)\cdots\left(\eta-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{k+1}}\right)}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2\pi}\overline{\lambda}_{j_{k+1}}\left(\frac{2}{\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf max}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}}\right)^{k+1}L\left(\gamma_{3}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2\pi}\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf min}}\left(\frac{2}{\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf max}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}}\right)^{k+1}\cdot 10\left(\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf max}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{80}{\pi}\frac{\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf min}}}{\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf min}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}}\left(\frac{2}{\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf max}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}}\right)^{k-1}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{80}{\pi}\frac{\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}}}{\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}}\left(\frac{2}{\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}}\right)^{k-1}=\frac{40}{\pi}\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}}\left(\frac{2}{\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}}\right)^{k}.

(148)

The third inequality holds because of (
147
), whereas the last one applies the monotonicity of the function
f(x)=\frac{x}{x-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}}
for
x>\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}
and the inequality
\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf min}}\geq\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf max}}\geq\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}}
.

Case 2:
\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf min}}-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf max}}>3(\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf max}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1})
.

Denote the following two disjoint sets

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{1}=\left\{i:1\leq i\leq k+1,\overline{\lambda}_{j_{i}}\geq\frac{2}{3}\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf min}}+\frac{1}{3}\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf max}}\right\}

(149)

and

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{2}=\left\{i:1\leq i\leq k+1,\overline{\lambda}_{j_{i}}\leq\frac{1}{3}\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf min}}+\frac{2}{3}\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf max}}\right\}.

(150)

By the definition of
j_{\sf min}
and
j_{\sf max}
, we know that
\mathcal{I}_{1}
and
\mathcal{I}_{2}
are nonempty.
We consider the following three scenarios: (1)
\min\{|\mathcal{I}_{1}|,|\mathcal{I}_{2}|\}\geq 2
; (2)
|\mathcal{I}_{1}|=1
and (3)
|\mathcal{I}_{2}|=1
.

Case 2.1:
\min\{|\mathcal{I}_{1}|,|\mathcal{I}_{2}|\}\geq 2
.

When it comes to this case, one can find four different indices
i_{1},i_{2},i_{3},i_{4}
such that
i_{1},i_{3}\in\mathcal{I}_{1}
,
i_{2},i_{4}\in\mathcal{I}_{2}
,
\overline{\lambda}_{j_{i_{1}}}=\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf min}}
and
\overline{\lambda}_{j_{i_{2}}}=\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf max}}
. Then the triangle inequality tells us that

\displaystyle\max\left\{\left|\eta-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{i_{1}}}\right|,\left|\eta-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{i_{2}}}\right|\right\}\geq\frac{1}{2}\left(\overline{\lambda}_{j_{i_{1}}}-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{i_{2}}}\right)=\frac{1}{2}\left(\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf min}}-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf max}}\right)

and

\displaystyle\max\left\{\left|\eta-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{i_{3}}}\right|,\left|\eta-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{i_{4}}}\right|\right\}\geq\frac{1}{2}\left(\overline{\lambda}_{j_{i_{3}}}-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{i_{4}}}\right)\geq\frac{1}{6}\left(\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf min}}-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf max}}\right).

Similar to (
C.3
), one can derive

\displaystyle\left|\frac{1}{\left(\eta-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{i_{1}}}\right)\left(\eta-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{i_{2}}}\right)\left(\eta-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{i_{3}}}\right)\left(\eta-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{i_{4}}}\right)}\right|

\displaystyle\quad=\frac{1}{\left|\eta-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{i_{1}}}\right|\left|\eta-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{i_{2}}}\right|}\frac{1}{\left|\eta-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{i_{3}}}\right|\left|\eta-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{i_{4}}}\right|}

\displaystyle\quad=\min\left\{\frac{4}{\left(\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf min}}-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf max}}\right)\left(\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf max}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}\right)},\frac{4}{\left(\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf max}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}\right)^{2}}\right\}

\displaystyle\qquad\cdot\min\left\{\frac{12}{\left(\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf min}}-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf max}}\right)\left(\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf max}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}\right)},\frac{4}{\left(\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf max}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}\right)^{2}}\right\}

\displaystyle\quad\leq\frac{8}{\left(\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf max}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}\right)\left(\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf min}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}\right)}\cdot\frac{16}{\left(\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf max}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}\right)\left(\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf min}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}\right)}

\displaystyle\quad=\frac{128}{\left(\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf max}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}\right)^{2}\left(\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf min}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}\right)^{2}}.

The penultimate line uses the basic inequality
\min\{a/b,c/d\}\leq(a+c)/(b+d)
for
a,b,c,d>0
. Putting the previous inequality, (
144
), (
145
) and (
123
) together, we reach

\displaystyle\left|\frac{1}{2\pi\rm{i}}\oint_{\gamma_{1}}\frac{\overline{\lambda}_{j_{k+1}}\rm{d}\eta}{\left(\eta-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{1}}\right)\cdots\left(\eta-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{k+1}}\right)}\right|

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\leq\frac{1}{2\pi}\sup_{\eta\in\gamma_{3}}\left|\frac{\overline{\lambda}_{j_{k+1}}}{\left(\eta-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{1}}\right)\cdots\left(\eta-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{k+1}}\right)}\right|\cdot L(\gamma_{3})

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\leq\frac{1}{2\pi}\overline{\lambda}_{j_{k+1}}\frac{128}{\left(\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf max}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}\right)^{2}\left(\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf min}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}\right)^{2}}\left(\frac{2}{\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf max}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}}\right)^{k+1-4}\cdot 4\left(\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf min}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\leq\frac{64}{\pi}\frac{\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf min}}}{\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf min}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}}\left(\frac{2}{\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf max}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}}\right)^{k-1}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\leq\frac{64}{\pi}\frac{\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}}}{\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}}\left(\frac{2}{\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf max}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}}\right)^{k-1}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\leq\frac{32}{\pi}\left(\frac{2}{\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}}\right)^{k}\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}}.

(151)

The fourth line is due to
\overline{\lambda}_{j_{k+1}}\leq\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf min}}
and the fifth line holds since
f(x)=\frac{x}{x-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}}
is a decreasing function on
(\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1},\infty)
.

Case 2.2:
|\mathcal{I}_{1}|=1
.

Let us choose

\displaystyle\ell\in\mathop{\rm arg\max}_{i:1\leq i\leq k+1,i\notin\mathcal{I}_{1}}\overline{\lambda}_{j_{i}}.

(152)

We can see from the definition of
\mathcal{I}_{1}
that

\displaystyle\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf min}}-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\ell}}\geq\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf min}}-\left(\frac{2}{3}\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf min}}+\frac{1}{3}\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf max}}\right)=\frac{1}{3}\left(\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf min}}-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf max}}\right)>\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf max}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}.

The last inequality is valid since
\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf min}}-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf max}}>3(\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf max}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1})
. We let
\gamma_{4}
and
\gamma_{5}
denote the following counterclockwise contours:

\displaystyle\gamma_{4}=\bigg\{x+y\rm{i}

\displaystyle:x=\frac{\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf max}}+\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}}{2},-\frac{\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf max}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}}{2}\leq y\leq\frac{\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf max}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}}{2},

\displaystyle\quad\text{or }x=\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\ell}}+\frac{\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf max}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}}{2},-\frac{\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf max}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}}{2}\leq y\leq\frac{\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf max}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}}{2},

\displaystyle\quad\text{or }y=\pm\frac{\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf max}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}}{2},\frac{\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf max}}+\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}}{2}\leq x\leq\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\ell}}+\frac{\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf max}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}}{2}\bigg\}

and

\displaystyle\gamma_{5}=\bigg\{x+y\rm{i}

\displaystyle:x=\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\ell}}+\frac{\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf max}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}}{2},-\frac{\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf max}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}}{2}\leq y\leq\frac{\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf max}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}}{2},

\displaystyle\quad\text{or }x=\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf min}}+\frac{\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf max}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}}{2},-\frac{\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf max}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}}{2}\leq y\leq\frac{\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf max}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}}{2},

\displaystyle\quad\text{or }y=\pm\frac{\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf max}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}}{2},\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\ell}}+\frac{\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf max}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}}{2}\leq x\leq\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf min}}+\frac{\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf max}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}}{2}\bigg\}.

For any complex number
\eta=x+y\rm{i}
with
x=\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\ell}}+\frac{\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf max}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}}{2}
and
-\frac{\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf max}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}}{2}\leq y\leq\frac{\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf max}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}}{2}
, we know that
g(\eta)=\frac{1}{\left(\eta-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{1}}\right)\cdots\left(\eta-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{k+1}}\right)}\neq 0
, and thus
g(\eta)
is analytic on
\{\eta=x+y{\rm{i}}:x=\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\ell}}+\frac{\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf max}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}}{2},-\frac{\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf max}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}}{2}\leq y\leq\frac{\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf max}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}}{2}\}
. Applying Cauchy’s integral formula yields

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2\pi\rm{i}}\oint_{\gamma_{3}}\frac{\rm{d}\eta}{\left(\eta-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{1}}\right)\cdots\left(\eta-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{k+1}}\right)}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt=\frac{1}{2\pi\rm{i}}\oint_{\gamma_{4}}\frac{\rm{d}\eta}{\left(\eta-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{1}}\right)\cdots\left(\eta-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{k+1}}\right)}+\frac{1}{2\pi\rm{i}}\oint_{\gamma_{5}}\frac{\rm{d}\eta}{\left(\eta-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{1}}\right)\cdots\left(\eta-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{k+1}}\right)}.

(153)

Repeating a similar argument as for (
138
) reveals that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2\pi}\sup_{\eta\in\gamma_{4}}\left|\frac{1}{\prod_{1\leq i\leq k+1,i\notin\mathcal{I}_{1}}\left(\eta-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{i}}\right)}\right|\cdot L\left(\gamma_{4}\right)\leq\frac{8}{\pi}\left(\frac{2}{\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}}\right)^{k-1}.

(154)

In addition, for any
\eta\in\gamma_{4}
, we know that its real part

\displaystyle\text{Re}(\eta)

\displaystyle\leq\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\ell}}+\frac{\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf max}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}}{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2}{3}\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf min}}+\frac{1}{3}\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf max}}+\frac{\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf max}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}}{2}

\displaystyle=\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf min}}-\frac{\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf min}}-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf max}}}{3}+\frac{\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf max}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}}{2}

\displaystyle\leq\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf min}}-\frac{\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf min}}-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf max}}}{8}-\frac{5}{8}\left(\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf max}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}\right)+\frac{\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf max}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}}{2}

\displaystyle=\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf min}}-\frac{\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf min}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}}{8},

which further tells us that

\displaystyle\left|\eta-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf min}}\right|\geq\frac{\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf min}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}}{8},~\qquad~\forall\eta\in\gamma_{4}.

Putting (
154
) and the previous inequality together, one has

\displaystyle\left|\frac{1}{2\pi\rm{i}}\oint_{\gamma_{4}}\frac{\overline{\lambda}_{j_{k+1}}\rm{d}\eta}{\left(\eta-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{1}}\right)\cdots\left(\eta-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{k+1}}\right)}\right|

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\leq\frac{1}{2\pi}\sup_{\eta\in\gamma_{4}}\left|\frac{1}{\prod_{1\leq i\leq k,i\notin\mathcal{I}_{1}}\left(\eta-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{i}}\right)}\right|\cdot\sup_{\eta\in\gamma_{4}}\frac{\overline{\lambda}_{j_{k+1}}}{\left|\eta-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf min}}\right|}\cdot L\left(\gamma_{4}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\leq\frac{8}{\pi}\left(\frac{2}{\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}}\right)^{k-1}\cdot\frac{8\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf min}}}{\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf min}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\leq\frac{32}{\pi}\left(\frac{2}{\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}}\right)^{k}\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}}.

(155)

Here, the second and the third lines also make use of
|\mathcal{I}_{1}|=1
. Note that for all
i\in\{1,\dots,k+1\}\backslash\mathcal{I}_{1}
,
\overline{\lambda}_{j_{i}}
is not in or on
\gamma_{5}
. By virtue of Cauchy’s integral formula, one has

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2\pi\rm{i}}\oint_{\gamma_{5}}\frac{\rm{d}\eta}{\left(\eta-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{1}}\right)\cdots\left(\eta-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{k+1}}\right)}=\frac{1}{2\pi\rm{i}}\oint_{\gamma_{5}}\frac{\prod_{1\leq i\leq k+1,i\notin\mathcal{I}_{1}}\frac{1}{\eta-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{i}}}}{\eta-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf min}}}{\rm{d}\eta}=\prod_{1\leq i\leq k+1,i\notin\mathcal{I}_{1}}\frac{1}{\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf min}}-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{i}}}.

(156)

Moreover, the definition of
\mathcal{I}_{1}
tells us that

\displaystyle\min_{i:1\leq i\leq k+1,i\notin\mathcal{I}_{1}}\left|\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf min}}-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{i}}\right|

\displaystyle\geq\frac{\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf min}}-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf max}}}{3}

\displaystyle=\frac{\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf min}}-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf max}}}{4}+\frac{\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf min}}-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf max}}}{12}

\displaystyle\geq\left(\frac{\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf min}}-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf max}}}{4}+\frac{\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf max}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}}{4}\right)\vee\left(\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf max}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf min}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}}{4}\vee\left(\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf max}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}\right).

Combining (
156
) and the previous inequality, one has

\displaystyle\left|\frac{1}{2\pi\rm{i}}\oint_{\gamma_{5}}\frac{\overline{\lambda}_{j_{k+1}}\rm{d}\eta}{\left(\eta-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{1}}\right)\cdots\left(\eta-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{k+1}}\right)}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\prod_{1\leq i\leq k+1,i\notin\mathcal{I}_{1}}\frac{1}{\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf min}}-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{i}}}\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf min}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\left(\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf max}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}\right)^{k-1}}\frac{4\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf min}}}{\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf min}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\left(\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}\right)^{k-1}}\frac{4\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}}}{\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}}

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{2}{\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}}\right)^{k}\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}}.

(157)

Eqn. (
146
) together with (
C.3
), (
C.3
) and (
C.3
) implies that

\displaystyle\left|\frac{1}{2\pi\rm{i}}\oint_{\gamma_{1}}\frac{\overline{\lambda}_{j_{k+1}}\rm{d}\eta}{\left(\eta-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{1}}\right)\cdots\left(\eta-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{k+1}}\right)}\right|\leq\frac{36}{\pi}\left(\frac{2}{\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}}\right)^{k}\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}}.

(158)

Case 2.3:
|\mathcal{I}_{2}|=1
.

In this case, we define

\displaystyle\ell^{\prime}\in\mathop{\rm arg\min}_{i:1\leq i\leq k+1,i\notin\mathcal{I}_{2}}\overline{\lambda}_{j_{i}}.

Denote by
\gamma_{6}
and
\gamma_{7}
the following counterclockwise contours:

\displaystyle\gamma_{4}=\bigg\{x+y\rm{i}

\displaystyle:x=\frac{\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf max}}+\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}}{2},-\frac{\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf max}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}}{2}\leq y\leq\frac{\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf max}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}}{2},

\displaystyle\quad\text{or }x=\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\ell^{\prime}}}-\frac{\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf max}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}}{2},-\frac{\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf max}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}}{2}\leq y\leq\frac{\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf max}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}}{2},

\displaystyle\quad\text{or }y=\pm\frac{\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf max}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}}{2},\frac{\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf max}}+\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}}{2}\leq x\leq\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\ell^{\prime}}}-\frac{\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf max}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}}{2}\bigg\}

and

\displaystyle\gamma_{5}=\bigg\{x+y\rm{i}

\displaystyle:x=\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\ell^{\prime}}}-\frac{\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf max}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}}{2},-\frac{\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf max}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}}{2}\leq y\leq\frac{\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf max}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}}{2},

\displaystyle\quad\text{or }x=\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf min}}+\frac{\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf max}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}}{2},-\frac{\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf max}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}}{2}\leq y\leq\frac{\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf max}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}}{2},

\displaystyle\quad\text{or }y=\pm\frac{\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf max}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}}{2},\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\ell^{\prime}}}-\frac{\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf max}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}}{2}\leq x\leq\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf min}}+\frac{\overline{\lambda}_{j_{\sf max}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}}{2}\bigg\}.

Similar to (
C.3
), one has

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2\pi\rm{i}}\oint_{\gamma_{3}}\frac{\rm{d}\eta}{\left(\eta-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{1}}\right)\cdots\left(\eta-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{k+1}}\right)}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt=\frac{1}{2\pi\rm{i}}\oint_{\gamma_{6}}\frac{\rm{d}\eta}{\left(\eta-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{1}}\right)\cdots\left(\eta-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{k+1}}\right)}+\frac{1}{2\pi\rm{i}}\oint_{\gamma_{7}}\frac{\rm{d}\eta}{\left(\eta-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{1}}\right)\cdots\left(\eta-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{k+1}}\right)}.

(159)

Repeating similar arguments as in (
C.3
) and (
C.3
) yields

\displaystyle\left|\frac{1}{2\pi\rm{i}}\oint_{\gamma_{6}}\frac{\overline{\lambda}_{j_{k+1}}\rm{d}\eta}{\left(\eta-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{1}}\right)\cdots\left(\eta-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{k+1}}\right)}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{2}{\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}}\right)^{k}\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}},,

(160a)

\displaystyle\left|\frac{1}{2\pi\rm{i}}\oint_{\gamma_{7}}\frac{\overline{\lambda}_{j_{k+1}}\rm{d}\eta}{\left(\eta-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{1}}\right)\cdots\left(\eta-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{k+1}}\right)}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{32}{\pi}\left(\frac{2}{\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}}\right)^{k}\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}}.

(160b)

Putting (
146
), (
C.3
), (
160a
) and (
160b
) together, one has

\displaystyle\left|\frac{1}{2\pi\rm{i}}\oint_{\gamma_{1}}\frac{\overline{\lambda}_{j_{k+1}}\rm{d}\eta}{\left(\eta-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{1}}\right)\cdots\left(\eta-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{k+1}}\right)}\right|\leq\frac{36}{\pi}\left(\frac{2}{\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}}\right)^{k}\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}}.

(161)

In summary, we are guaranteed to have

\displaystyle\left|\frac{1}{2\pi\rm{i}}\oint_{\gamma_{1}}\frac{\overline{\lambda}_{j_{k+1}}\rm{d}\eta}{\left(\eta-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{1}}\right)\cdots\left(\eta-\overline{\lambda}_{j_{k+1}}\right)}\right|\leq\frac{40}{\pi}\left(\frac{2}{\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}}-\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}+1}}\right)^{k}\overline{\lambda}_{r_{1}}.

This together with (
C.3
) finishes the proof of (
87b
).
∎

C.4
Proof of Lemma
5

Throughout the subsection, we assume that
\mathcal{E}
holds.

Proof of (
92a
).

(
92a
) clearly holds for
i=0
due to the definition of
\mu
. Now we consider the case
i\geq 1
. It is easy to verify that for any matrices
\bm{A},\bm{B}\in\mathbb{R}^{m_{1}\times m_{1}}
,

\displaystyle\left(\bm{A}+\bm{B}\right)^{i}=\bm{B}^{i}+\sum_{j=0}^{i-1}\bm{B}^{j}\bm{A}\left(\bm{A}+\bm{B}\right)^{i-j-1}.

(162)

This allows us to decompose
\bm{Z}_{3}^{i}\bm{U}^{\star}
as follows:

\displaystyle\bm{Z}_{3}^{i}\bm{U}^{\star}

\displaystyle=\left[\mathcal{P}_{\sf off\text{-}diag}\left(\bm{E}\bm{E}^{\top}-\bm{E}\bm{V}^{\star}\bm{V}^{\star\top}\bm{E}^{\top}\right)\right]^{i}\bm{U}^{\star}

\displaystyle=-\sum_{j=0}^{i-1}\left[\mathcal{P}_{\sf off\text{-}diag}\left(\bm{E}\bm{E}^{\top}\right)\right]^{j}\mathcal{P}_{\sf off\text{-}diag}\left(\bm{E}\bm{V}^{\star}\bm{V}^{\star\top}\bm{E}^{\top}\right)\left[\mathcal{P}_{\sf off\text{-}diag}\left(\bm{E}\bm{E}^{\top}-\bm{E}\bm{V}^{\star}\bm{V}^{\star\top}\bm{E}^{\top}\right)\right]^{i-j-1}\bm{U}^{\star}

\displaystyle\quad+\left[\mathcal{P}_{\sf off\text{-}diag}\left(\bm{E}\bm{E}^{\top}\right)\right]^{i}\bm{U}^{\star}

\displaystyle=-\sum_{j=0}^{i-1}\left[\mathcal{P}_{\sf off\text{-}diag}\left(\bm{E}\bm{E}^{\top}\right)\right]^{j}\bm{E}\bm{V}^{\star}\bm{V}^{\star\top}\bm{E}^{\top}\left[\mathcal{P}_{\sf off\text{-}diag}\left(\bm{E}\bm{E}^{\top}-\bm{E}\bm{V}^{\star}\bm{V}^{\star\top}\bm{E}^{\top}\right)\right]^{i-j-1}\bm{U}^{\star}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{j=0}^{i-1}\left[\mathcal{P}_{\sf off\text{-}diag}\left(\bm{E}\bm{E}^{\top}\right)\right]^{j}\mathcal{P}_{\sf diag}\left(\bm{E}\bm{V}^{\star}\bm{V}^{\star\top}\bm{E}^{\top}\right)\left[\mathcal{P}_{\sf off\text{-}diag}\left(\bm{E}\bm{E}^{\top}-\bm{E}\bm{V}^{\star}\bm{V}^{\star\top}\bm{E}^{\top}\right)\right]^{i-j-1}\bm{U}^{\star}

\displaystyle\quad+\left[\mathcal{P}_{\sf off\text{-}diag}\left(\bm{E}\bm{E}^{\top}\right)\right]^{i}\bm{U}^{\star}.

In view of (
88
), (
89
), (
90a
) and (
90b
), one can obtain the following upper bound for
\|\bm{Z}^{i}\bm{U}^{\star}\|_{2,\infty}
:

\displaystyle\big\|\bm{Z}_{3}^{i}\bm{U}^{\star}\big\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\quad\leq\sum_{j=0}^{i-1}\left\|\left[\mathcal{P}_{\sf off\text{-}diag}\left(\bm{E}\bm{E}^{\top}\right)\right]^{j}\bm{E}\bm{V}^{\star}\right\|_{2,\infty}\left\|\bm{V}^{\star\top}\bm{E}^{\top}\left[\mathcal{P}_{\sf off\text{-}diag}\left(\bm{E}\bm{E}^{\top}-\bm{E}\bm{V}^{\star}\bm{V}^{\star\top}\bm{E}^{\top}\right)\right]^{i-j-1}\bm{U}^{\star}\right\|

\displaystyle\qquad+\sum_{j=0}^{i-1}\left\|\left[\mathcal{P}_{\sf off\text{-}diag}\left(\bm{E}\bm{E}^{\top}\right)\right]^{j}\mathcal{P}_{\sf diag}\left(\bm{E}\bm{V}^{\star}\bm{V}^{\star\top}\bm{E}^{\top}\right)\left[\mathcal{P}_{\sf off\text{-}diag}\left(\bm{E}\bm{E}^{\top}-\bm{E}\bm{V}^{\star}\bm{V}^{\star\top}\bm{E}^{\top}\right)\right]^{i-j-1}\bm{U}^{\star}\right\|

\displaystyle\qquad+\left\|\left[\mathcal{P}_{\sf off\text{-}diag}\left(\bm{E}\bm{E}^{\top}\right)\right]^{i}\bm{U}^{\star}\right\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\quad\leq\sum_{j=0}^{i-1}\left\|\left[\mathcal{P}_{\sf off\text{-}diag}\left(\bm{E}\bm{E}^{\top}\right)\right]^{j}\bm{E}\bm{V}^{\star}\right\|_{2,\infty}\left\|\bm{E}\bm{V}^{\star}\right\|\left\|\mathcal{P}_{\sf off\text{-}diag}\left(\bm{E}\bm{E}^{\top}-\bm{E}\bm{V}^{\star}\bm{V}^{\star\top}\bm{E}^{\top}\right)\right\|^{i-j-1}

\displaystyle\qquad+\sum_{j=0}^{i-1}\left\|\mathcal{P}_{\sf off\text{-}diag}\left(\bm{E}\bm{E}^{\top}\right)\right\|^{j}\left\|\bm{E}\bm{V}^{\star}\right\|_{2,\infty}^{2}\left\|\mathcal{P}_{\sf off\text{-}diag}\left(\bm{E}\bm{E}^{\top}-\bm{E}\bm{V}^{\star}\bm{V}^{\star\top}\bm{E}^{\top}\right)\right\|^{i-j-1}

\displaystyle\qquad+\left\|\left[\mathcal{P}_{\sf off\text{-}diag}\left(\bm{E}\bm{E}^{\top}\right)\right]^{i}\bm{U}^{\star}\right\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\quad\leq\sum_{j=0}^{i-1}\left(C_{3}\sqrt{\mu r}\left(C_{3}\left(\sqrt{m_{1}m_{2}}+m_{1}\right)\omega_{\sf max}^{2}\log^{2}m\right)^{j}\omega_{\sf max}\log m\right)\cdot C_{5}\sqrt{m_{1}}\omega_{\sf max}\log m

\displaystyle\hskip 42.67912pt\cdot\left(3C_{5}\left(\sqrt{m_{1}m_{2}}+m_{1}\right)\omega_{\sf max}^{2}\log^{2}m\right)^{i-j-1}

\displaystyle\qquad+\sum_{j=0}^{i-1}\left(C_{5}\left(\sqrt{m_{1}m_{2}}+m_{1}\right)\omega_{\sf max}^{2}\log^{2}m\right)^{j}\left(C_{3}\sqrt{\mu r}\omega_{\sf max}\log m\right)^{2}\cdot\left(3C_{5}\left(\sqrt{m_{1}m_{2}}+m_{1}\right)\omega_{\sf max}^{2}\log^{2}m\right)^{i-j-1}

\displaystyle\qquad+C_{3}\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{m_{1}}}\left(C_{3}\left(\sqrt{m_{1}m_{2}}+m_{1}\right)\omega_{\sf max}^{2}\log^{2}m\right)^{i}

\displaystyle\quad\leq 3C_{3}\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{m_{1}}}\left(C_{3}\left(\sqrt{m_{1}m_{2}}+m_{1}\right)\omega_{\sf max}^{2}\log^{2}m\right)^{i},

(163)

provided that
C_{3}\geq 6C_{5}
.

Proof of (
92b
).

When
i=0
, (
92b
) is a direct consequence of Lemma
2
. For
i\geq 1
, similar to (
C.4
), one has

\displaystyle\big\|\bm{Z}_{3}^{i}\bm{E}\bm{V}^{\star}\big\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\quad\leq\sum_{j=0}^{i-1}\left\|\left[\mathcal{P}_{\sf off\text{-}diag}\left(\bm{E}\bm{E}^{\top}\right)\right]^{j}\bm{E}\bm{V}^{\star}\right\|_{2,\infty}\left\|\bm{V}^{\star\top}\bm{E}^{\top}\left[\mathcal{P}_{\sf off\text{-}diag}\left(\bm{E}\bm{E}^{\top}-\bm{E}\bm{V}^{\star}\bm{V}^{\star\top}\bm{E}^{\top}\right)\right]^{i-j-1}\bm{E}\bm{V}^{\star}\right\|

\displaystyle\qquad+\sum_{j=0}^{i-1}\left\|\left[\mathcal{P}_{\sf off\text{-}diag}\left(\bm{E}\bm{E}^{\top}\right)\right]^{j}\mathcal{P}_{\sf diag}\left(\bm{E}\bm{V}^{\star}\bm{V}^{\star\top}\bm{E}^{\top}\right)\left[\mathcal{P}_{\sf off\text{-}diag}\left(\bm{E}\bm{E}^{\top}-\bm{E}\bm{V}^{\star}\bm{V}^{\star\top}\bm{E}^{\top}\right)\right]^{i-j-1}\bm{E}\bm{V}^{\star}\right\|

\displaystyle\qquad+\left\|\left[\mathcal{P}_{\sf off\text{-}diag}\left(\bm{E}\bm{E}^{\top}\right)\right]^{i}\bm{E}\bm{V}^{\star}\right\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\quad\leq\sum_{j=0}^{i-1}\left\|\left[\mathcal{P}_{\sf off\text{-}diag}\left(\bm{E}\bm{E}^{\top}\right)\right]^{j}\bm{E}\bm{V}^{\star}\right\|_{2,\infty}\left\|\bm{E}\bm{V}^{\star}\right\|^{2}\left\|\mathcal{P}_{\sf off\text{-}diag}\left(\bm{E}\bm{E}^{\top}-\bm{E}\bm{V}^{\star}\bm{V}^{\star\top}\bm{E}^{\top}\right)\right\|^{i-j-1}

\displaystyle\qquad+\sum_{j=0}^{i-1}\left\|\mathcal{P}_{\sf off\text{-}diag}\left(\bm{E}\bm{E}^{\top}\right)\right\|^{j}\left\|\bm{E}\bm{V}^{\star}\right\|_{2,\infty}^{2}\left\|\mathcal{P}_{\sf off\text{-}diag}\left(\bm{E}\bm{E}^{\top}-\bm{E}\bm{V}^{\star}\bm{V}^{\star\top}\bm{E}^{\top}\right)\right\|^{i-j-1}\left\|\bm{E}\bm{V}^{\star}\right\|

\displaystyle\qquad+\left\|\left[\mathcal{P}_{\sf off\text{-}diag}\left(\bm{E}\bm{E}^{\top}\right)\right]^{i}\bm{E}\bm{V}^{\star}\right\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\quad\leq\sum_{j=0}^{i-1}\left(C_{3}\sqrt{\mu r}\left(C_{3}\left(\sqrt{m_{1}m_{2}}+m_{1}\right)\omega_{\sf max}^{2}\log^{2}m\right)^{j}\omega_{\sf max}\log m\right)\cdot\left(\sqrt{C_{5}}\sqrt{m_{1}}\omega_{\sf max}\log m\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\hskip 42.67912pt\cdot\left(3C_{5}\left(\sqrt{m_{1}m_{2}}+m_{1}\right)\omega_{\sf max}^{2}\log^{2}m\right)^{i-j-1}

\displaystyle\qquad+\sum_{j=0}^{i-1}\left(C_{5}\left(\sqrt{m_{1}m_{2}}+m_{1}\right)\omega_{\sf max}^{2}\log^{2}m\right)^{j}\left(C_{3}\sqrt{\mu r}\omega_{\sf max}\log m\right)^{2}\cdot\left(3C_{5}\left(\sqrt{m_{1}m_{2}}+m_{1}\right)\omega_{\sf max}^{2}\log^{2}m\right)^{i-j-1}

\displaystyle\hskip 56.9055pt\cdot\sqrt{C_{5}}\sqrt{m_{1}}\omega_{\sf max}\log m

\displaystyle\qquad+C_{3}\sqrt{\mu r}\left(C_{3}\left(\sqrt{m_{1}m_{2}}+m_{1}\right)\omega_{\sf max}^{2}\log^{2}m\right)^{i}\omega_{\sf max}\log m

\displaystyle\quad\leq 3C_{3}\sqrt{\mu r}\left(C_{3}\left(\sqrt{m_{1}m_{2}}+m_{1}\right)\omega_{\sf max}^{2}\log^{2}m\right)^{i}\omega_{\sf max}\log m,

(164)

provided that
C_{3}\geq 6C_{5}
. In the last inequality, we used the assumption that
m_{1}\gg\mu r
.

Proof of (
92c
).

We can directly use the same argument of
Zhou and Chen, 2023
to prove (
92c
). We omit the details here for the sake of brevity.

Proof of (
92d
).

Putting (
78
), (
90a
), (
92a
) and (
92b
) together shows that: for all
0\leq i\leq\log m
, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{Z}_{3}^{i}\bm{Z}_{1}\right\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle=\left\|\bm{Z}_{3}^{i}\left(\bm{U}^{\star(2)}\bm{\Sigma}^{\star(2)}\bm{V}^{\star(2)\top}\bm{E}+\bm{E}\bm{V}^{\star(2)}\bm{\Sigma}^{\star(2)}\bm{U}^{\star(2)\top}+\bm{E}\bm{V}^{\star(2)}\bm{V}^{\star(2)\top}\bm{E}\right)\right\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\bm{Z}_{3}^{i}\bm{U}^{\star(2)}\bm{\Sigma}^{\star(2)}\bm{V}^{\star(2)\top}\bm{E}^{\top}\right\|_{2,\infty}+\left\|\bm{Z}_{3}^{i}\bm{E}\bm{V}^{\star(2)}\bm{\Sigma}^{\star(2)}\bm{U}^{\star(2)\top}\right\|_{2,\infty}+\left\|\bm{Z}_{3}^{i}\bm{E}\bm{V}^{\star(2)}\bm{V}^{\star(2)\top}\bm{E}^{\top}\right\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\big\|\bm{Z}_{3}^{i}\bm{U}^{\star(2)}\big\|_{2,\infty}\big\|\bm{\Sigma}^{\star(2)}\big\|\big\|\bm{E}\bm{V}^{\star(2)}\big\|+\big\|\bm{Z}_{3}^{i}\bm{E}\bm{V}^{\star(2)}\big\|_{2,\infty}\big\|\bm{\Sigma}^{\star(2)}\big\|+\left\|\bm{Z}_{3}^{i}\bm{E}\bm{V}^{\star(2)}\right\|_{2,\infty}\big\|\bm{E}\bm{V}^{\star(2)}\big\|

\displaystyle\leq\sigma_{\overline{r}+1}^{\star}\big\|\bm{Z}_{3}^{i}\bm{U}^{\star}\big\|_{2,\infty}\big\|\bm{E}\bm{V}^{\star}\big\|+\sigma_{\overline{r}+1}^{\star}\big\|\bm{Z}_{3}^{i}\bm{E}\bm{V}^{\star}\big\|_{2,\infty}+\left\|\bm{Z}_{3}^{i}\bm{E}\bm{V}^{\star}\right\|_{2,\infty}\big\|\bm{E}\bm{V}^{\star}\big\|

\displaystyle\leq\sigma_{\overline{r}+1}^{\star}\cdot 3C_{3}\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{m_{1}}}\left(C_{3}\left(\sqrt{m_{1}m_{2}}+m_{1}\right)\omega_{\sf max}^{2}\log^{2}m\right)^{i}\cdot\sqrt{C}_{5}\sqrt{m}_{1}\omega_{\sf max}\log m

\displaystyle\quad+\sigma_{\overline{r}+1}^{\star}\omega_{\sf max}\log m\cdot 3C_{3}\sqrt{\mu r}\left(C_{3}\left(\sqrt{m_{1}m_{2}}+m_{1}\right)\omega_{\sf max}^{2}\log^{2}m\right)^{i}\omega_{\sf max}\log m

\displaystyle\quad+3C_{3}\sqrt{\mu r}\left(C_{3}\left(\sqrt{m_{1}m_{2}}+m_{1}\right)\omega_{\sf max}^{2}\log^{2}m\right)^{i}\omega_{\sf max}\log m\cdot\sqrt{C}_{5}\sqrt{m}_{1}\omega_{\sf max}\log m

\displaystyle\leq C_{2}\sqrt{\mu r}\left(\sigma_{\overline{r}+1}^{\star}+\sqrt{m}_{1}\omega_{\sf max}\log m\right)\left(C_{3}\left(\sqrt{m_{1}m_{2}}+m_{1}\right)\omega_{\sf max}^{2}\log^{2}m\right)^{i}\omega_{\sf max}\log m,

(165)

provided that
C_{2}\geq 9C_{3}\sqrt{C_{5}}
.
The fourth line makes use of the fact that
\|\bm{B}\|\leq\|\bm{A}\|
and
\|\bm{B}\|_{2,\infty}\leq\|\bm{A}\|_{2,\infty}
for any
\bm{A}
and its submatrix
\bm{B}
.

Proof of (
92e
).

By virtue of (
78
), (
92a
), (
92c
) and (
C.2
), we know that for all
0\leq i\leq\log m
,

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{Z}_{3}^{i}\bm{Z}_{2}\right\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\bm{Z}_{3}^{i}\left(\mathcal{P}_{\widetilde{\bm{U}}^{(1)}}\bm{U}^{\star(2)}\big(\bm{\Sigma}^{\star(2)}\big)^{2}\bm{U}^{\star(2)\top}\mathcal{P}_{\big(\widetilde{\bm{U}}^{(1)}\big)_{\perp}}+\bm{U}^{\star(2)}\big(\bm{\Sigma}^{\star(2)}\big)^{2}\bm{U}^{\star(2)\top}\mathcal{P}_{\widetilde{\bm{U}}^{(1)}}\right)\right\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\big\|\bm{Z}_{3}^{i}\widetilde{\bm{U}}^{(1)}\big\|_{2,\infty}\big\|\widetilde{\bm{U}}^{(1)\top}\bm{U}^{\star(2)}\big\|\big\|\bm{\Sigma}^{\star(2)}\big\|^{2}+\big\|\bm{Z}_{3}^{i}\bm{U}^{\star(2)}\big\|_{2,\infty}\big\|\bm{\Sigma}^{\star(2)}\big\|^{2}\big\|\widetilde{\bm{U}}^{(1)\top}\bm{U}^{\star(2)}\big\|

\displaystyle\leq\big\|\bm{Z}_{3}^{i}\widetilde{\bm{U}}^{(1)}\big\|_{2,\infty}\big\|\widetilde{\bm{U}}^{(1)\top}\left(\bm{U}_{1}^{\star}\right)_{\perp}\big\|\sigma_{\overline{r}+1}^{\star 2}+\big\|\bm{Z}_{3}^{i}\bm{U}^{\star(2)}\big\|_{2,\infty}\big\|\widetilde{\bm{U}}^{(1)\top}\left(\bm{U}_{1}^{\star}\right)_{\perp}\big\|\sigma_{\overline{r}+1}^{\star 2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{m_{1}}}\left(C_{3}\left(\sqrt{m_{1}m_{2}}+m_{1}\right)\omega_{\sf max}^{2}\log^{2}m\right)^{i}\cdot\frac{\sqrt{m}_{1}\omega_{\sf max}\log m}{\sigma_{\overline{r}}^{\star}}\sigma_{\overline{r}+1}^{\star 2}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{m_{1}}}\left(C_{3}\left(\sqrt{m_{1}m_{2}}+m_{1}\right)\omega_{\sf max}^{2}\log^{2}m\right)^{i}\cdot\sqrt{m}_{1}\omega_{\sf max}\sigma_{\overline{r}+1}^{\star}\log m

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\mu r}\left(C_{3}\left(\sqrt{m_{1}m_{2}}+m_{1}\right)\omega_{\sf max}^{2}\log^{2}m\right)^{i}\omega_{\sf max}\sigma_{\overline{r}+1}^{\star}\log m.

(166)

The third line holds since
\bm{U}^{\star(1)\top}\bm{U}^{\star(2)}=\bm{0}
,
whereas the fifth line is due to the basic fact that
\sigma_{\overline{r}}^{\star}\geq\sigma_{\overline{r}+1}^{\star}
.
∎

Appendix D
Proof of Theorem
6

D.1
Several notation

First, we introduce some notation that will be useful throughout the proof. We let

\displaystyle\bm{G}_{k+1}^{0}:=\bm{G}_{k},~\qquad~\forall 0\leq k\leq k_{\sf max},

(167)

For any
0\leq t\leq t_{k+1}
and
0\leq k\leq k_{\sf max}
, we define

\displaystyle\bm{U}_{k+1}^{t}\bm{\Lambda}_{k+1}^{t}\bm{U}_{k+1}^{t\top}:=\text{ the leading }r_{k}\text{ eigendecomposition of }\bm{G}_{k+1}^{t},

(168)

and denote

\displaystyle\bm{G}_{k+1}^{t+1}:=\mathcal{P}_{\sf off\text{-}diag}\left(\bm{G}_{k+1}^{t}\right)+\mathcal{P}_{\sf diag}\left(\bm{U}_{k+1}^{t}\bm{\Lambda}_{k+1}^{t}\bm{U}_{k+1}^{t\top}\right).

(169)

Recall that we can decompose
\bm{M}^{\sf oracle}
into four terms:

\displaystyle\bm{M}^{\sf oracle}=\widetilde{\bm{M}}+\bm{Z}_{1}+\bm{Z}_{2}+\bm{Z}_{3}=\widetilde{\bm{M}}+\bm{Z},

(170)

where
\widetilde{\bm{M}},\bm{Z}_{1},\bm{Z}_{2}
and
\bm{Z}_{3}
are defined in (
C
).

For notational convenience, we let

\displaystyle D_{k}^{t}=\left\|\bm{G}_{k}^{t}-\bm{M}^{\sf oracle}\right\|,~\qquad~F_{k}^{t}=\big\|\mathcal{P}_{\sf diag}\big(\bm{G}_{k}^{t}-\widetilde{\bm{M}}\big)\big\|,~\qquad~\text{and}~\qquad~L_{k}^{t}=\big\|\bm{G}_{k}^{t}-\widetilde{\bm{M}}\big\|.

(171)

In addition, we let

\displaystyle\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{k}=\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{:,1:r_{k}},~\qquad~\bm{U}^{\sf oracle}_{k}=\bm{U}^{\sf oracle}_{:,1:r_{k}},~\qquad~\text{and}~\qquad~\bm{U}^{\star}_{k}=\bm{U}^{\star}_{:,1:r_{k}},

(172)

where
\widetilde{\bm{U}}
(resp.
\bm{U}^{\sf oracle}
) is the rank-
r
leading eigenspace of
\widetilde{\bm{M}}
(resp.
\bm{M}^{\sf oracle}
).
We also let
\mathcal{E}
denote the following event:

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}

\displaystyle=\{\eqref{ineq:power_V}\text{ and }\eqref{ineq:power_U}\text{ hold for }0\leq k\leq\log n\}\cap\{\eqref{ineq2a},\eqref{ineq2b},\eqref{ineq2c}\text{ and }\eqref{ineq2d}\text{ hold}\}

\displaystyle\quad\cap\{\eqref{ineq:oracle_tilde}~\text{and}~\eqref{ineq:oracle}~\text{for all }~r^{\prime}\in\mathcal{A}\}.

(173)

We know from Lemmas
2
,
3
,
4
, Theorem
5
and the union bound that

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\mathcal{E}\right)\geq 1-O\left(m^{-10}\right).

(174)

Throughout the rest of the proof, we assume that
\mathcal{E}
occurs.

D.2
Main steps for proving Theorem
6

Step 1: a key property of
r_{1}
selected in Algorithm
1
.

First, we show that

\displaystyle r_{1}\in\mathcal{R}_{1}\cap\mathcal{A},

(175)

where

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}_{1}:=\left\{r^{\prime}\leq r:\frac{\sigma_{1}\left(\bm{G}_{0}\right)}{\sigma_{r^{\prime}}\left(\bm{G}_{0}\right)}\leq 4\quad\text{and}\quad\sigma_{r^{\prime}}\left(\bm{G}_{0}\right)-\sigma_{r^{\prime}+1}\left(\bm{G}_{0}\right)\geq\frac{1}{r}\sigma_{r^{\prime}}\left(\bm{G}_{0}\right)\right\}.

(176)

and
\mathcal{A}
is defined in (
38
). Noting that
\widetilde{\bm{U}}=[\widetilde{\bm{U}}^{(1)}\ \widetilde{\bm{U}}^{(2)}]
and putting (
90d
) and (
C.2
) together, one has

\displaystyle\big\|\widetilde{\bm{U}}\big\|_{2,\infty}=\big\|\big[\widetilde{\bm{U}}^{(1)}\ \widetilde{\bm{U}}^{(2)}\big]\big\|_{2,\infty}\leq\big\|\widetilde{\bm{U}}^{(1)}\big\|_{2,\infty}+\big\|\widetilde{\bm{U}}^{(2)}\big\|_{2,\infty}\leq 4\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{m_{1}}}.

(177)

In view of (
107
), (
177
) and the definition
\bm{G}_{1}^{0}=\bm{G}_{0}=\mathcal{P}_{\sf off\text{-}diag}(\bm{M})=\mathcal{P}_{\sf off\text{-}diag}(\bm{M}^{\sf oracle})=\mathcal{P}_{\sf off\text{-}diag}(\widetilde{\bm{U}}\widetilde{\bm{\Lambda}}\widetilde{\bm{U}}^{\top}+\bm{Z})
, we can derive

\displaystyle L_{1}^{0}

\displaystyle=\big\|\bm{G}_{0}-\widetilde{\bm{M}}\big\|

\displaystyle=\big\|\mathcal{P}_{\sf diag}\big(\widetilde{\bm{U}}\widetilde{\bm{\Lambda}}\widetilde{\bm{U}}^{\top}\big)-\mathcal{P}_{\sf off\text{-}diag}\left(\bm{Z}\right)\big\|

\displaystyle\leq\big\|\mathcal{P}_{\sf diag}\big(\widetilde{\bm{U}}\widetilde{\bm{\Lambda}}\widetilde{\bm{U}}^{\top}\big)\big\|+\left\|\mathcal{P}_{\sf off\text{-}diag}\left(\bm{Z}\right)\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\big\|\widetilde{\bm{U}}\big\|_{2,\infty}^{2}\big\|\widetilde{\bm{\Lambda}}\big\|+2\left\|\bm{Z}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq 16\frac{\mu r}{m_{1}}\widetilde{\sigma}_{1}^{2}+C_{2}\left(\sqrt{m_{1}}\omega_{\sf max}\log m\cdot\sigma_{\overline{r}+1}^{\star}+\left(\sqrt{m_{1}m_{2}}+m_{1}\right)\omega_{\sf max}^{2}\log^{2}m\right).

(178)

This together with Weyl’s inequality shows that, for all
i\in[m_{1}]
,

\displaystyle\big|\sigma_{i}\left(\bm{G}_{0}\right)-\widetilde{\sigma}_{i}^{2}\big|

\displaystyle=\big|\sigma_{i}\left(\bm{G}_{0}\right)-\sigma_{i}\big(\widetilde{\bm{M}}\big)\big|

\displaystyle\leq 16\frac{\mu r}{m_{1}}\widetilde{\sigma}_{1}^{2}+C_{2}\left(\sqrt{m_{1}}\omega_{\sf max}\log m\cdot\sigma_{\overline{r}+1}^{\star}+\left(\sqrt{m_{1}m_{2}}+m_{1}\right)\omega_{\sf max}^{2}\log^{2}m\right).

(179)

Moreover, (
90a
) tells us that

\displaystyle\widetilde{\sigma}_{i}\leq\sigma_{i}^{\star}+\sqrt{C_{5}}\sqrt{m_{1}}\omega_{\sf max}\log m\leq\left(1+\frac{1}{Cr^{2}}\right)\sigma_{i}^{\star},~\qquad~\forall i\in\left[\overline{r}\right]

for some large constant
C>0
, where
\overline{r}=\max\mathcal{A}
and the last inequality holds since
\sigma_{\overline{r}}^{\star}\geq C_{0}r\big[(m_{1}m_{2})^{1/4}+rm_{1}^{1/2}\big]\omega_{\sf max}\log m
. Similarly, one can show that

\displaystyle\left(1-\frac{1}{Cr^{2}}\right)\sigma_{i}^{\star}\leq\widetilde{\sigma}_{i}\leq\left(1+\frac{1}{Cr^{2}}\right)\sigma_{i}^{\star},~\qquad~\forall i\in\left[\overline{r}\right].

(180)

By virtue of (
180
) and the assumption
\sigma_{\overline{r}}^{\star}\geq C_{0}r\big[(m_{1}m_{2})^{1/4}+rm_{1}^{1/2}\big]\omega_{\sf max}\log m
, one has

\displaystyle 16\frac{\mu r}{m_{1}}\widetilde{\sigma}_{1}^{2}+C_{2}\left(\sqrt{m_{1}}\omega_{\sf max}\log m\cdot\sigma_{\overline{r}+1}^{\star}+\left(\sqrt{m_{1}m_{2}}+m_{1}\right)\omega_{\sf max}^{2}\log^{2}m\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\leq 16\frac{\mu r}{m_{1}}\widetilde{\sigma}_{1}^{2}+C_{2}\left(\sqrt{m_{1}}\omega_{\sf max}\log m\cdot\sigma_{\overline{r}+1}^{\star}+\frac{1}{C_{0}^{2}r^{2}}\sigma_{\overline{r}}^{\star 2}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\leq 16\frac{\mu r}{m_{1}}\widetilde{\sigma}_{1}^{2}+C_{2}\left(2\sqrt{m_{1}}\omega_{\sf max}\log m\cdot\widetilde{\sigma}_{\overline{r}+1}+\frac{4}{C_{0}^{2}r^{2}}\widetilde{\sigma}_{\overline{r}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\leq 16\frac{\mu r}{m_{1}}\widetilde{\sigma}_{1}^{2}+C_{2}\left(\frac{2}{C_{0}r^{2}}\widetilde{\sigma}_{\overline{r}}^{2}+\frac{4}{C_{0}^{2}r^{2}}\widetilde{\sigma}_{\overline{r}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\leq\frac{16c_{1}}{r^{2}}\widetilde{\sigma}_{1}^{2}+\frac{1}{2Cr^{2}}\widetilde{\sigma}_{\overline{r}}^{2}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\leq\frac{1}{Cr^{2}}\widetilde{\sigma}_{1}^{2},

(181)

provided that
C_{0}\geq 8C\cdot C_{2}
and
c_{1}\leq\frac{1}{32C}
. Here, the fourth line comes from
\sigma_{\overline{r}}^{\star}\geq C_{0}r\big[(m_{1}m_{2})^{1/4}+rm_{1}^{1/2}\big]\omega_{\sf max}\log m
and (
180
), whereas the penultimate line uses the assumption
\mu\leq c_{0}m_{1}/r^{3}
. Combining (
D.2
) and (
D.2
) gives

\displaystyle\sigma_{1}\left(\bm{G}_{0}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\widetilde{\sigma}_{1}^{2}-\frac{1}{Cr^{2}}\widetilde{\sigma}_{1}^{2}>\max\left\{\frac{1}{2}\widetilde{\sigma}_{1}^{2},\frac{1}{2}\sigma_{1}^{\star 2},\tau\right\},

(182)

where the last inequality holds due to (
180
) and
C_{1}^{2}/2\geq C_{\tau}
.

Equipped with (
D.2
), (
180
), (
D.2
) and (
182
),
we can establish the property
r_{1}\in\mathcal{R}_{1}
using a similar argument as in the proof of
Zhou and Chen, 2023
. Therefore, we only need to prove
r_{1}\in\mathcal{A}
. In view of (
180
), (
182
) and the definition of
\mathcal{R}_{1}
, we know that

\displaystyle\sigma_{r_{1}}\left(\bm{G}_{0}\right)\geq\frac{1}{4}\sigma_{1}\left(\bm{G}_{0}\right)\geq\frac{1}{8}\sigma_{1}^{\star 2},

(183)

and consequently,

\displaystyle\sigma_{r_{1}}^{\star}

\displaystyle\geq\left(1-\frac{C}{r^{2}}\right)\widetilde{\sigma}_{r_{1}}\geq\left(1-\frac{C}{r^{2}}\right)\left[\sigma_{r_{1}}\left(\bm{G}_{0}\right)-\frac{1}{Cr^{2}}\widetilde{\sigma}_{1}^{2}\right]^{1/2}\geq\left(1-\frac{C}{r^{2}}\right)\left(\frac{1}{8}\sigma_{1}^{\star 2}-\frac{4}{Cr^{2}}\sigma_{1}^{\star 2}\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{3}\sigma_{1}^{\star}>2C_{0}r\big[(m_{1}m_{2})^{1/4}+rm_{1}^{1/2}\big]\omega_{\sf max}\log m.

(184)

Furthermore, inequality (
D.2
), (
D.2
) and (
183
) combined imply that

\displaystyle\widetilde{\sigma}_{r_{1}}^{2}\geq\sigma_{r_{1}}\left(\bm{G}_{0}\right)-\frac{1}{Cr^{2}}\widetilde{\sigma}_{1}^{2}\geq\frac{1}{4}\sigma_{1}\left(\bm{G}_{0}\right)-\frac{1}{Cr^{2}}\widetilde{\sigma}_{1}^{2}\geq\frac{1}{4}\left(\widetilde{\sigma}_{1}^{2}-\frac{1}{Cr^{2}}\widetilde{\sigma}_{1}^{2}\right)-\frac{1}{Cr^{2}}\widetilde{\sigma}_{1}^{2}\geq\frac{1}{5}\widetilde{\sigma}_{1}^{2}.

(185)

Inequalities (
D.2
), (
D.2
), (
185
) and the triangle inequality taken together show that

\displaystyle\widetilde{\sigma}_{r_{1}}^{2}-\widetilde{\sigma}_{r_{1}+1}^{2}

\displaystyle\geq\sigma_{r_{1}}\left(\bm{G}_{0}\right)-\sigma_{r_{1}+1}\left(\bm{G}_{0}\right)-\big|\sigma_{r_{1}}\left(\bm{G}_{0}\right)-\widetilde{\sigma}_{r_{1}}^{2}\big|-\big|\sigma_{r_{1}+1}\left(\bm{G}_{0}\right)-\widetilde{\sigma}_{r_{1}+1}^{2}\big|

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{r}\sigma_{r_{1}}\left(\bm{G}_{0}\right)-\frac{2}{Cr^{2}}\widetilde{\sigma}_{1}^{2}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{r}\widetilde{\sigma}_{r_{1}}^{2}-\frac{1}{r}\big|\sigma_{r_{1}}\left(\bm{G}_{0}\right)-\widetilde{\sigma}_{r_{1}}^{2}\big|-\frac{2}{Cr^{2}}\widetilde{\sigma}_{1}^{2}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{r}\widetilde{\sigma}_{r_{1}}^{2}-\frac{3}{Cr^{2}}\widetilde{\sigma}_{1}^{2}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{r}\widetilde{\sigma}_{r_{1}}^{2}-\frac{15}{Cr^{2}}\widetilde{\sigma}_{r_{1}}^{2}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{9}{10r}\widetilde{\sigma}_{r_{1}}^{2}.

(186)

Note that (
78
) together with (
D.2
) reveals that
r_{1}\leq\overline{r}
, where
\overline{r}
is the largest element in
\mathcal{A}
. Putting (
90a
), (
103
), (
180
) and the previous inequality together, we arrive at

\displaystyle\sigma_{r_{1}}^{\star}-\sigma_{r_{1}+1}^{\star}

\displaystyle\geq\widetilde{\sigma}_{r_{1}}-\widetilde{\sigma}_{r_{1}+1}-\left|\widetilde{\sigma}_{r_{1}}-\sigma_{r_{1}}^{\star}\right|-\left|\widetilde{\sigma}_{r_{1}+1}-\sigma_{r_{1}+1}^{\star}\right|

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{\widetilde{\sigma}_{r_{1}}+\widetilde{\sigma}_{r_{1}+1}}\big(\widetilde{\sigma}_{r_{1}}^{2}-\widetilde{\sigma}_{r_{1}+1}^{2}\big)-\sqrt{C_{5}}\sqrt{m_{1}}\omega_{\sf max}\log m-2\sqrt{C_{5}}\sqrt{m_{1}}\omega_{\sf max}\log m

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{2\widetilde{\sigma}_{r_{1}}}\cdot\frac{9}{10r}\widetilde{\sigma}_{r_{1}}^{2}-\frac{1}{20r^{2}}\sigma_{r_{1}}^{\star}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{9}{20r}\left(1-\frac{1}{Cr}\right)\sigma_{r_{1}}^{\star}-\frac{1}{20r^{2}}\sigma_{r_{1}}^{\star}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{4r}\sigma_{r_{1}}^{\star}.

(187)

Inequality (
D.2
) taken together with (
D.2
) validates
r_{1}\in\mathcal{A}
, thus finishing the proof of (
175
).

Step 2: bounding
D_{1}^{t}=\|\bm{G}_{1}^{t}-\bm{M}^{\sf oracle}\|
.

Now, we would like to deal with the quantities
\{D_{1}^{t}\}
. Recognizing that for all
t
and
k
,

\displaystyle\mathcal{P}_{\sf off\text{-}diag}\left(\bm{G}_{k}^{t}\right)=\mathcal{P}_{\sf off\text{-}diag}\left(\bm{M}^{\sf oracle}\right)=\mathcal{P}_{\sf off\text{-}diag}\left(\bm{Y}\bm{Y}^{\top}\right),

we can deduce that

\displaystyle D_{k}^{t}=\left\|\mathcal{P}_{\sf diag}\left(\bm{G}_{k}^{t}-\bm{M}^{\sf oracle}\right)\right\|.

(188)

We would like to prove the following inequalities by induction:

\displaystyle F_{1}^{t}-40\sqrt{\frac{\mu r^{3}}{m_{1}}}\left\|\bm{Z}\right\|-20\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{m_{1}}}\widetilde{\sigma}_{r_{1}+1}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{e^{t}}\left(F_{1}^{0}-40\sqrt{\frac{\mu r^{3}}{m_{1}}}\left\|\bm{Z}\right\|-20\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{m_{1}}}\widetilde{\sigma}_{r_{1}+1}^{2}\right),

(189a)

\displaystyle D_{1}^{t}

\displaystyle\leq F_{1}^{t}+6C_{3}\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{m_{1}}}\sqrt{m}_{1}\omega_{\sf max}\log m\cdot\sigma_{\overline{r}+1}^{\star}+C_{3}^{2}\mu r\omega_{\sf max}^{2}\log^{2}m,

(189b)

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{U}_{1}^{t}\bm{U}_{1}^{t\top}-\bm{U}_{1}^{\sf oracle}\bm{U}_{1}^{\sf oracle\top}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq 2\frac{D_{1}^{t}}{\lambda_{r_{1}}\left(\bm{M}^{\sf oracle}\right)-\lambda_{r_{1}+1}\left(\bm{M}^{\sf oracle}\right)}\leq\frac{1}{8},

(189c)

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{U}_{1}^{t}\right\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\bm{U}_{1}^{t}\bm{U}_{1}^{t\top}-\bm{U}_{1}^{\sf oracle}\bm{U}_{1}^{\sf oracle\top}\right\|+\left\|\bm{U}_{1}^{\sf oracle}\right\|_{2,\infty}\leq\frac{1}{4e}.

(189d)

Step 2.1: the base case (
t=0
) for (
189a
)-(
189d
).

Note that (
189a
) automatically holds when
t=0
. Recalling that
\mathcal{P}_{\sf diag}(\bm{G}_{1}^{0})=0
,
we can invoke
Zhang et al., 2022
together with (
177
) to obtain

\displaystyle F_{1}^{0}=\big\|\mathcal{P}_{\sf diag}\big(\widetilde{\bm{M}}\big)\big\|=\big\|\mathcal{P}_{\sf diag}\big(\widetilde{\bm{U}}\widetilde{\bm{\Lambda}}\widetilde{\bm{U}}^{\top}\big)\big\|\leq\big\|\widetilde{\bm{U}}\big\|_{2,\infty}^{2}\big\|\widetilde{\bm{\Lambda}}\big\|\leq 16\frac{\mu r}{m_{1}}\widetilde{\sigma}_{1}^{2}.

(190)

Furthermore, putting Lemma
2
, (
C
), (
90d
), (
C.2
) and (
190
) together, we have

\displaystyle D_{1}^{0}

\displaystyle=\left\|\mathcal{P}_{\sf diag}\left(\bm{M}^{\sf oracle}\right)\right\|=\big\|\mathcal{P}_{\sf diag}\big(\widetilde{\bm{M}}+\bm{Z}_{1}+\bm{Z}_{2}+\bm{Z}_{3}\big)\big\|

\displaystyle\leq\big\|\mathcal{P}_{\sf diag}\big(\widetilde{\bm{M}}\big)\big\|+\left\|\mathcal{P}_{\sf diag}\left(\bm{Z}_{1}\right)\right\|+\left\|\mathcal{P}_{\sf diag}\left(\bm{Z}_{2}\right)\right\|+\left\|\mathcal{P}_{\sf diag}\left(\bm{Z}_{3}\right)\right\|

\displaystyle\leq F_{1}^{0}+\left\|\mathcal{P}_{\sf off-diag}\left(\bm{U}^{\star(2)}\bm{\Sigma}^{\star(2)}\bm{V}^{\star(2)\top}\bm{E}^{\top}+\bm{E}\bm{V}^{\star(2)}\bm{\Sigma}^{\star(2)}\bm{U}^{\star(2)\top}+\bm{E}\bm{V}^{\star(2)}\bm{V}^{\star(2)\top}\bm{E}^{\top}\right)\right\|

\displaystyle\quad+2\left\|\mathcal{P}_{\sf diag}\left(\mathcal{P}_{\widetilde{\bm{U}}^{(1)}}\bm{U}^{\star(2)}\big(\bm{\Sigma}^{\star(2)}\big)^{2}\bm{U}^{\star(2)\top}\mathcal{P}_{\big(\widetilde{\bm{U}}^{(1)}\big)_{\perp}}\right)\right\|+0

\displaystyle\leq F_{1}^{0}+2\big\|\bm{U}^{\star(2)}\big\|_{2,\infty}\big\|\bm{E}\bm{V}^{\star(2)}\big\|_{2,\infty}\big\|\bm{\Sigma}^{\star(2)}\big\|+\big\|\bm{E}\bm{V}^{\star(2)}\big\|_{2,\infty}^{2}+2\big\|\widetilde{\bm{U}}^{(1)}\big\|_{2,\infty}\big\|\widetilde{\bm{U}}^{(1)\top}\bm{U}^{\star(2)}\big\|\big\|\bm{\Sigma}^{\star(2)}\big\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq F_{1}^{0}+2\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{m_{1}}}\cdot C_{3}\sqrt{\mu r}\omega_{\sf max}\log m\cdot\sigma_{\overline{r}+1}^{\star}+\left(C_{3}\sqrt{\mu r}\omega_{\sf max}\log m\right)^{2}+4\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{m_{1}}}\cdot C_{3}\frac{\sqrt{m}_{1}\omega_{\sf max}\log m}{\sigma_{\overline{r}}^{\star}}\sigma_{\overline{r}+1}^{\star 2}

\displaystyle\leq F_{1}^{0}+6C_{3}\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{m_{1}}}\sqrt{m}_{1}\omega_{\sf max}\log m\cdot\sigma_{\overline{r}+1}^{\star}+C_{3}^{2}\mu r\omega_{\sf max}^{2}\log^{2}m,

(191)

which validates (
189b
) for
t=0
. Here, the last line holds since
\mu\leq c_{0}m_{1}/r^{3}
and
\sigma_{\overline{r}}^{\star}\geq\sigma_{\overline{r}+1}^{\star}
. Combining (
D.2
), (
185
), (
D.2
) and (
D.2
), one has

\displaystyle\max\left\{\frac{\sigma_{1}^{\star}}{\sigma_{r_{1}}^{\star}},\frac{\widetilde{\sigma}_{1}}{\widetilde{\sigma}_{r_{1}}}\right\}\leq 3~\qquad~\text{and}~\qquad~\min\left\{\frac{\widetilde{\sigma}_{r_{1}}^{2}-\widetilde{\sigma}_{r_{1}+1}^{2}}{\widetilde{\sigma}_{r_{1}}^{2}},\frac{\sigma_{r_{1}}^{\star 2}-\sigma_{r_{1}+1}^{\star 2}}{\sigma_{r_{1}}^{\star 2}}\right\}\geq\frac{1}{4r}.

(192)

Moreover, by virtue of (
90a
), (
103
) and the fact
r_{1}\in\mathcal{A}
, we know that

\displaystyle\big|\big(\widetilde{\sigma}_{r_{1}}-\widetilde{\sigma}_{r_{1}+1}\big)-\big(\sigma_{r_{1}}^{\star}-\sigma_{r_{1}+1}^{\star}\big)\big|

\displaystyle\leq\big|\widetilde{\sigma}_{r_{1}}-\sigma_{r_{1}}^{\star}\big|+\big|\widetilde{\sigma}_{r_{1}+1}-\sigma_{r_{1}+1}^{\star}\big|\leq\sqrt{C}_{5}\sqrt{m_{1}}\omega_{\sf max}\log m+2\sqrt{C_{5}}\sqrt{m_{1}}\omega_{\sf max}\log m

\displaystyle\ll\frac{1}{r^{2}}\sigma_{r_{1}}^{\star}\lesssim\frac{1}{r}\left(\sigma_{r_{1}}^{\star}-\sigma_{r_{1}+1}^{\star}\right),

where the last inequality comes from (
D.2
). This implies that

\displaystyle\left(1-\frac{1}{Cr}\right)\big(\sigma_{r_{1}}^{\star}-\sigma_{r_{1}+1}^{\star}\big)\leq\widetilde{\sigma}_{r_{1}}-\widetilde{\sigma}_{r_{1}+1}\leq\left(1+\frac{1}{Cr}\right)\big(\sigma_{r_{1}}^{\star}-\sigma_{r_{1}+1}^{\star}\big).

The previous inequality together with (
180
), (
107
) and (
192
) gives

\displaystyle\widetilde{\sigma}_{r_{1}}^{2}-\widetilde{\sigma}_{r_{1}+1}^{2}\asymp\sigma_{r_{1}}^{\star 2}-\sigma_{r_{1}+1}^{\star 2}\gg\left\|\bm{Z}\right\|.

Recalling that
\bm{M}^{\sf oracle}=\widetilde{\bm{M}}+\bm{Z}
, one can invoke Weyl’s inequality to obtain

\displaystyle\lambda_{r_{1}}\left(\bm{M}^{\sf oracle}\right)-\lambda_{r_{1}+1}\left(\bm{M}^{\sf oracle}\right)\asymp\widetilde{\sigma}_{r_{1}}^{2}-\widetilde{\sigma}_{r_{1}+1}^{2}\asymp\sigma_{r_{1}}^{\star 2}-\sigma_{r_{1}+1}^{\star 2}\gg\left\|\bm{Z}\right\|.

(193)

Note that
\bm{U}_{1}^{0}
(resp.
\bm{U}_{1}^{\sf oracle}
) is the rank-
r
leading eigenspace of
\bm{G}_{0}
(resp.
\bm{M}^{\sf oracle}
). We know from the Davis-Kahan Theorem
(
Chen et al., 2021a
, Theorem 2.7)
, (
D.2
), (
192
) and (
193
) that

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{U}_{1}^{0}\bm{U}_{1}^{0\top}-\bm{U}_{1}^{\sf oracle}\bm{U}_{1}^{\sf oracle\top}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq 2\frac{\left\|\bm{G}_{0}-\bm{M}^{\sf oracle}\right\|}{\lambda_{r_{1}}\left(\bm{M}^{\sf oracle}\right)-\lambda_{r_{1}+1}\left(\bm{M}^{\sf oracle}\right)}=2\frac{D_{1}^{0}}{\lambda_{r_{1}}\left(\bm{M}^{\sf oracle}\right)-\lambda_{r_{1}+1}\left(\bm{M}^{\sf oracle}\right)}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\eqref{ineq125}}}{{\lesssim}}\frac{\frac{\mu r}{m_{1}}\widetilde{\sigma}_{1}^{2}}{\widetilde{\sigma}_{r_{1}}^{2}-\widetilde{\sigma}_{r_{1}+1}^{2}}+\frac{\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{m_{1}}}\sqrt{m}_{1}\omega_{\sf max}\log m\cdot\sigma_{\overline{r}+1}^{\star}}{\sigma_{r_{1}}^{\star 2}-\sigma_{r_{1}+1}^{\star 2}}+\frac{\mu r\omega_{\sf max}^{2}\log^{2}m}{\sigma_{r_{1}}^{\star 2}-\sigma_{r_{1}+1}^{\star 2}}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\eqref{ineq124}}}{{\lesssim}}\frac{\frac{\mu r}{m_{1}}\widetilde{\sigma}_{1}^{2}}{\widetilde{\sigma}_{r_{1}}^{2}/r}+\frac{\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{m_{1}}}\sqrt{m}_{1}\omega_{\sf max}\log m\cdot\sigma_{\overline{r}+1}^{\star}}{\sigma_{r_{1}}^{\star 2}/r}+\frac{\mu r\omega_{\sf max}^{2}\log^{2}m}{\sigma_{r_{1}}^{\star 2}/r}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\eqref{ineq124}}}{{\lesssim}}\frac{\mu r^{2}}{m_{1}}+\frac{\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{m_{1}}}r\sqrt{m}_{1}\omega_{\sf max}\log m}{\sigma_{r_{1}}^{\star}}+\frac{\mu r\omega_{\sf max}^{2}\log^{2}m}{\sigma_{r_{1}}^{\star 2}/r}

\displaystyle\ll\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{m_{1}}}\leq\frac{1}{8},

(194)

which proves (
189c
) for
t=0
. Here, the last inequality is due to
\mu\leq c_{0}m_{1}/r^{3}
and
\sigma_{r_{1}}^{\star}\geq\sigma_{\overline{r}}^{\star}\geq C_{0}r\big[(m_{1}m_{2})^{1/4}+rm_{1}^{1/2}\big]\omega_{\sf max}\log m
. Inequality (
39b
) and the fact
r_{1}\in\mathcal{A}
together imply that

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{U}_{1}^{\sf oracle}\right\|_{2,\infty}=\left\|\bm{U}_{1}^{\sf oracle}\bm{U}_{1}^{\sf oracle\top}\right\|_{2,\infty}\leq\left\|\bm{U}_{1}^{\sf oracle}\bm{U}_{1}^{\sf oracle\top}-\bm{U}_{1}^{\star}\bm{U}_{1}^{\star\top}\right\|_{2,\infty}+\|\bm{U}_{1}^{\star}\|_{2,\infty}\leq 2\sqrt{\frac{\mu r^{3}}{m_{1}}}.

(195)

Combining (
D.2
) and (
195
), one can further obtain that

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{U}_{1}^{0}\right\|_{2,\infty}=\left\|\bm{U}_{1}^{0}\bm{U}_{1}^{0\top}\right\|_{2,\infty}\leq\left\|\bm{U}_{1}^{0}\bm{U}_{1}^{0\top}-\bm{U}_{1}^{\sf oracle}\bm{U}_{1}^{\sf oracle\top}\right\|+\left\|\bm{U}_{1}^{\sf oracle}\right\|_{2,\infty}\leq 3\sqrt{\frac{\mu r^{3}}{m_{1}}}\leq\frac{1}{4e},

(196)

i.e., (
189d
) holds for
t=0
.

Step 2.2: induction step (
t>0
) for (
189a
)-(
189d
).

Suppose that (
189a
)-(
189d
) hold for
t=t^{\prime}
. We aim to show that they continue to hold for
t=t^{\prime}+1
.

Recognizing that
\bm{U}_{1}^{t^{\prime}}
is the top-
r_{1}
singular space of

\displaystyle\bm{G}_{1}^{t^{\prime}}=\mathcal{P}_{\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{1}}\widetilde{\bm{M}}+\left(\bm{G}_{1}^{t^{\prime}}-\mathcal{P}_{\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{1}}\widetilde{\bm{M}}\right),

we have

\displaystyle F_{1}^{t^{\prime}+1}

\displaystyle=\big\|\mathcal{P}_{\sf diag}\big(\bm{G}_{1}^{t^{\prime}+1}-\widetilde{\bm{M}}\big)\big\|

\displaystyle=\left\|\mathcal{P}_{\sf diag}\big(\mathcal{P}_{\bm{U}_{1}^{t^{\prime}}}\bm{G}_{1}^{t^{\prime}}-\widetilde{\bm{M}}\big)\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\mathcal{P}_{\sf diag}\left(\mathcal{P}_{\bm{U}_{1}^{t^{\prime}}}\big(\bm{G}_{1}^{t^{\prime}}-\widetilde{\bm{M}}\big)\right)\right\|+\left\|\mathcal{P}_{\sf diag}\left(\mathcal{P}_{\left(\bm{U}_{1}^{t^{\prime}}\right)_{\perp}}\widetilde{\bm{M}}\mathcal{P}_{\widetilde{\bm{U}}}\right)\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\bm{U}_{1}^{t^{\prime}}\right\|_{2,\infty}\big\|\bm{G}_{1}^{t^{\prime}}-\widetilde{\bm{M}}\big\|+\big\|\widetilde{\bm{U}}\big\|_{2,\infty}\left\|\big(\bm{U}_{1}^{t^{\prime}}\big)_{\perp}\widetilde{\bm{M}}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\bm{U}_{1}^{t^{\prime}}\right\|_{2,\infty}L_{1}^{t^{\prime}}+4\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{m_{1}}}\left(\left\|\big(\bm{U}_{1}^{t^{\prime}}\big)_{\perp}\mathcal{P}_{\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{1}}\widetilde{\bm{M}}\right\|+\left\|\mathcal{P}_{\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{:,r_{1}+1:r}}\widetilde{\bm{M}}\right\|\right)

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\bm{U}_{1}^{t^{\prime}}\right\|_{2,\infty}L_{1}^{t^{\prime}}+4\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{m_{1}}}\left(2\left\|\bm{G}_{1}^{t^{\prime}}-\mathcal{P}_{\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{1}}\widetilde{\bm{M}}\right\|+\left\|\mathcal{P}_{\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{:,r_{1}+1:r}}\widetilde{\bm{M}}\right\|\right)

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\bm{U}_{1}^{t^{\prime}}\right\|_{2,\infty}L_{1}^{t^{\prime}}+4\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{m_{1}}}\left(2\left\|\bm{G}_{1}^{t^{\prime}}-\widetilde{\bm{M}}\right\|+3\left\|\mathcal{P}_{\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{:,r_{1}+1:r}}\widetilde{\bm{M}}\right\|\right)

\displaystyle\leq\left(\left\|\bm{U}_{1}^{t^{\prime}}\right\|_{2,\infty}+8\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{m_{1}}}\right)L_{1}^{t^{\prime}}+12\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{m_{1}}}\widetilde{\sigma}_{r_{1}+1}^{2}.

(197)

Here, the second line holds since
\mathcal{P}_{\sf diag}(\bm{G}_{1}^{t^{\prime}+1})=\mathcal{P}_{\sf diag}(\bm{U}_{1}^{t^{\prime}}\bm{\Lambda}_{1}^{t^{\prime}}\bm{U}_{1}^{t^{\prime}\top})=\mathcal{P}_{\sf diag}(\mathcal{P}_{\bm{U}_{1}^{t^{\prime}}}\bm{G}_{1}^{t^{\prime}})
; the fourth line comes from
Zhang et al., 2022
; the fifth line makes use of (
177
); the sixth line applies
Zhou and Chen, 2023
; and the penultimate line invokes the triangle inequality. Note that

\displaystyle L_{1}^{t^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\leq F_{1}^{t^{\prime}}+\big\|\mathcal{P}_{\sf off\text{-}diag}\big(\bm{G}_{1}^{t^{\prime}+1}-\widetilde{\bm{M}}\big)\big\|=F_{1}^{t^{\prime}}+\big\|\mathcal{P}_{\sf off\text{-}diag}\big(\bm{M}^{\sf oracle}-\widetilde{\bm{M}}\big)\big\|

\displaystyle\leq F_{1}^{t^{\prime}}+\left\|\mathcal{P}_{\sf off\text{-}diag}\left(\bm{Z}\right)\right\|\leq F_{1}^{t^{\prime}}+\left\|\bm{Z}\right\|+\left\|\mathcal{P}_{\sf diag}\left(\bm{Z}\right)\right\|\leq F_{1}^{t^{\prime}}+2\left\|\bm{Z}\right\|.

Inequality (
D.2
) taken together with the previous inequality leads us to

\displaystyle F_{1}^{t^{\prime}+1}

\displaystyle\leq\left(\left\|\bm{U}_{1}^{t^{\prime}}\right\|_{2,\infty}+8\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{m_{1}}}\right)F_{1}^{t^{\prime}}+2\left(\left\|\bm{U}_{1}^{t^{\prime}}\right\|_{2,\infty}+8\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{m_{1}}}\right)\left\|\bm{Z}\right\|+12\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{m_{1}}}\widetilde{\sigma}_{r_{1}+1}^{2}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\eqref{ineq:induction_4}}}{{\leq}}\left(\frac{1}{4e}+\frac{1}{4e}\right)F_{1}^{t^{\prime}}+2\left(\left\|\bm{U}_{1}^{t}\bm{U}_{1}^{t\top}-\bm{U}_{1}^{\sf oracle}\bm{U}_{1}^{\sf oracle\top}\right\|+\left\|\bm{U}_{1}^{\sf oracle}\right\|_{2,\infty}+8\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{m_{1}}}\right)\left\|\bm{Z}\right\|+12\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{m_{1}}}\widetilde{\sigma}_{r_{1}+1}^{2}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\eqref{ineq:induction_3}~\text{and}~\eqref{ineq129}}}{{\leq}}\frac{1}{2e}F_{1}^{t^{\prime}}+2\left(2\frac{D_{1}^{t^{\prime}}}{\lambda_{r_{1}}\left(\bm{M}^{\sf oracle}\right)-\lambda_{r_{1}+1}\left(\bm{M}^{\sf oracle}\right)}+2\sqrt{\frac{\mu r^{3}}{m_{1}}}+8\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{m_{1}}}\right)\left\|\bm{Z}\right\|+12\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{m_{1}}}\widetilde{\sigma}_{r_{1}+1}^{2}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\eqref{ineq:induction_2}}}{{\leq}}\frac{1}{2e}F_{1}^{t^{\prime}}+2\left(2\frac{F_{1}^{t^{\prime}}}{\lambda_{r_{1}}\left(\bm{M}^{\sf oracle}\right)-\lambda_{r_{1}+1}\left(\bm{M}^{\sf oracle}\right)}+10\sqrt{\frac{\mu r^{3}}{m_{1}}}\right)\left\|\bm{Z}\right\|+12\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{m_{1}}}\widetilde{\sigma}_{r_{1}+1}^{2}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+4\frac{6C_{3}\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{m_{1}}}\sqrt{m}_{1}\omega_{\sf max}\log m\cdot\sigma_{\overline{r}+1}^{\star}+C_{3}^{2}\mu r\omega_{\sf max}^{2}\log^{2}m}{\lambda_{r_{1}}\left(\bm{M}^{\sf oracle}\right)-\lambda_{r_{1}+1}\left(\bm{M}^{\sf oracle}\right)}\left\|\bm{Z}\right\|

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\eqref{ineq124}~\text{and}~\eqref{ineq125}}}{{\leq}}\frac{1}{e}F_{1}^{t^{\prime}}+20\sqrt{\frac{\mu r^{3}}{m_{1}}}\left\|\bm{Z}\right\|+12\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{m_{1}}}\widetilde{\sigma}_{r_{1}+1}^{2}+C_{3}^{3}\frac{\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{m_{1}}}\sqrt{m}_{1}\omega_{\sf max}\log m\cdot\sigma_{\overline{r}+1}^{\star}+\mu r\omega_{\sf max}^{2}\log^{2}m}{\sigma_{r_{1}}^{\star 2}/r}\left\|\bm{Z}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{e}F_{1}^{t^{\prime}}+21\sqrt{\frac{\mu r^{3}}{m_{1}}}\left\|\bm{Z}\right\|+12\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{m_{1}}}\widetilde{\sigma}_{r_{1}+1}^{2},

(198)

where the last inequality is a consequence of
\sigma_{r_{1}}^{\star}\geq\sigma_{\overline{r}}^{\star}\geq C_{0}r\big[(m_{1}m_{2})^{1/4}+rm_{1}^{1/2}\big]\omega_{\sf max}\log m
. Then one immediately has

\displaystyle F_{1}^{t^{\prime}+1}-40\sqrt{\frac{\mu r^{3}}{m_{1}}}\left\|\bm{Z}\right\|-20\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{m_{1}}}\widetilde{\sigma}_{r_{1}+1}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{e}\left(F_{1}^{t^{\prime}}-40\sqrt{\frac{\mu r^{3}}{m_{1}}}\left\|\bm{Z}\right\|-20\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{m_{1}}}\widetilde{\sigma}_{r_{1}+1}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{e^{t^{\prime}+1}}\left(F_{1}^{0}-40\sqrt{\frac{\mu r^{3}}{m_{1}}}\left\|\bm{Z}\right\|-20\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{m_{1}}}\widetilde{\sigma}_{r_{1}+1}^{2}\right),

which confirms that (
189a
) holds for
t=t^{\prime}+1
.

In addition, we can prove (
189b
) for
t=t^{\prime}+1
by using the same argument as in (
D.2
). Combining (
189a
), (
189b
) for
t=t^{\prime}+1
and Weyl’s inequality, we further have

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{U}_{1}^{t^{\prime}+1}\bm{U}_{1}^{t^{\prime}+1\top}-\bm{U}_{1}^{\sf oracle}\bm{U}_{1}^{\sf oracle\top}\right\|

\displaystyle\quad\leq 2\frac{D_{1}^{t^{\prime}+1}}{\lambda_{r_{1}}\left(\bm{M}^{\sf oracle}\right)-\lambda_{r_{1}+1}\left(\bm{M}^{\sf oracle}\right)}

\displaystyle\quad\leq 2\frac{F_{1}^{t^{\prime}+1}}{\lambda_{r_{1}}\left(\bm{M}^{\sf oracle}\right)-\lambda_{r_{1}+1}\left(\bm{M}^{\sf oracle}\right)}+2\frac{6C_{3}\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{m_{1}}}\sqrt{m}_{1}\omega_{\sf max}\log m\cdot\sigma_{\overline{r}+1}^{\star}+C_{3}^{2}\mu r\omega_{\sf max}^{2}\log^{2}m}{\lambda_{r_{1}}\left(\bm{M}^{\sf oracle}\right)-\lambda_{r_{1}+1}\left(\bm{M}^{\sf oracle}\right)}

\displaystyle\quad=2\frac{F_{1}^{t^{\prime}+1}-40\sqrt{\frac{\mu r^{3}}{m_{1}}}\left\|\bm{Z}\right\|-20\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{m_{1}}}\widetilde{\sigma}_{r_{1}+1}^{2}}{\lambda_{r_{1}}\left(\bm{M}^{\sf oracle}\right)-\lambda_{r_{1}+1}\left(\bm{M}^{\sf oracle}\right)}+2\frac{6C_{3}\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{m_{1}}}\sqrt{m}_{1}\omega_{\sf max}\log m\cdot\sigma_{\overline{r}+1}^{\star}+C_{3}^{2}\mu r\omega_{\sf max}^{2}\log^{2}m}{\lambda_{r_{1}}\left(\bm{M}^{\sf oracle}\right)-\lambda_{r_{1}+1}\left(\bm{M}^{\sf oracle}\right)}

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{80\sqrt{\frac{\mu r^{3}}{m_{1}}}\left\|\bm{Z}\right\|+40\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{m_{1}}}\widetilde{\sigma}_{r_{1}+1}^{2}}{\lambda_{r_{1}}\left(\bm{M}^{\sf oracle}\right)-\lambda_{r_{1}+1}\left(\bm{M}^{\sf oracle}\right)}

\displaystyle\quad\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\eqref{ineq125}}}{{\lesssim}}\frac{1}{e^{t^{\prime}+1}}\frac{F_{1}^{0}}{\lambda_{r_{1}}\left(\bm{M}^{\sf oracle}\right)-\lambda_{r_{1}+1}\left(\bm{M}^{\sf oracle}\right)}+\frac{\sqrt{\frac{\mu r^{3}}{m_{1}}}\left\|\bm{Z}\right\|+\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{m_{1}}}\widetilde{\sigma}_{r_{1}+1}^{2}}{\widetilde{\sigma}_{r_{1}}^{2}-\widetilde{\sigma}_{r_{1}+1}^{2}}

\displaystyle\qquad 2\frac{6C_{3}\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{m_{1}}}\sqrt{m}_{1}\omega_{\sf max}\log m\cdot\sigma_{\overline{r}+1}^{\star}+C_{3}^{2}\mu r\omega_{\sf max}^{2}\log^{2}m}{\sigma_{r_{1}}^{\star 2}-\sigma_{r_{1}+1}^{\star 2}}

\displaystyle\quad\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\eqref{ineq:F_1^0},\eqref{ineq124}~\text{and}~\eqref{ineq125}}}{{\ll}}\sqrt{\frac{\mu r^{3}}{m_{1}}}\ll\frac{1}{8},

which validates (
189c
) for
t=t^{\prime}+1
.

Putting the previous inequality and (
195
) together, one can prove that (
189d
) also holds for
t=t^{\prime}+1
:

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{U}_{1}^{t^{\prime}+1}\right\|_{2,\infty}=\left\|\bm{U}_{1}^{t^{\prime}+1}\bm{U}_{1}^{t^{\prime}+1\top}\right\|_{2,\infty}\leq\left\|\bm{U}_{1}^{t^{\prime}+1}\bm{U}_{1}^{t^{\prime}+1\top}-\bm{U}_{1}^{\sf oracle}\bm{U}_{1}^{\sf oracle\top}\right\|+\left\|\bm{U}_{1}^{\sf oracle}\right\|_{2,\infty}\leq 3\sqrt{\frac{\mu r^{3}}{m_{1}}}\leq\frac{1}{4e}.

Therefore, we have completed the proof of the induction step for (
189a
) - (
189d
).

Step 3: bounding
D_{k}^{t}
for
k>1

After establishing upper bounds on
\{D_{1}^{t}\}
, we now turn attention to the quantities
\{D_{k}^{t}\}_{k>1}
. By setting

t_{1}\geq\log\left(C\frac{\sigma_{1}^{\star 2}}{\sigma_{r_{1}+1}^{\star 2}+\omega_{\sf max}^{2}}\right),

we can show that

\displaystyle F_{2}^{0}=F_{1}^{t_{1}}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\eqref{ineq:induction_1}}}{{\leq}}40\sqrt{\frac{\mu r^{3}}{m_{1}}}\left\|\bm{Z}\right\|+20\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{m_{1}}}\widetilde{\sigma}_{r_{1}+1}^{2}+\frac{1}{e^{t_{1}}}F_{1}^{0}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\eqref{ineq:D_1^0}}}{{\leq}}40\sqrt{\frac{\mu r^{3}}{m_{1}}}\left\|\bm{Z}\right\|+20\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{m_{1}}}\widetilde{\sigma}_{r_{1}+1}^{2}+\frac{\sigma_{r_{1}+1}^{\star 2}+\omega_{\sf max}^{2}}{C\sigma_{1}^{\star 2}}\cdot 16\frac{\mu r}{m_{1}}\widetilde{\sigma}_{1}^{2}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\eqref{ineq:spectral_Z}~\text{and}~\eqref{ineq124}}}{{\leq}}40C_{2}\sqrt{\frac{\mu r^{3}}{m_{1}}}\left(\sqrt{m_{1}}\omega_{\sf max}\log m\cdot\sigma_{\overline{r}+1}^{\star}+\left(\sqrt{m_{1}m_{2}}+m_{1}\right)\omega_{\sf max}^{2}\log^{2}m\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 56.9055pt+20\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{m_{1}}}\widetilde{\sigma}_{r_{1}+1}^{2}+\frac{1}{C}\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{m_{1}}}\left(\omega_{\sf max}^{2}+\sigma_{r_{1}+1}^{\star 2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq 41C_{2}\sqrt{\frac{\mu r^{3}}{m_{1}}}\left(\sqrt{m_{1}}\omega_{\sf max}\log m\cdot\sigma_{\overline{r}+1}^{\star}+\left(\sqrt{m_{1}m_{2}}+m_{1}\right)\omega_{\sf max}^{2}\log^{2}m\right)+21\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{m_{1}}}\widetilde{\sigma}_{r_{1}+1}^{2}.

(199)

Here, the last line makes use of the following inequality:

\displaystyle\sigma_{r_{1}+1}^{\star 2}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\eqref{ineq2a}~\text{and}~\eqref{ineq128}}}{{\leq}}\left(\widetilde{\sigma}_{r_{1}+1}+2\sqrt{C_{5}}\sqrt{m_{1}}\omega_{\sf max}\log m\right)^{2}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{Cauchy-Schwarz}}}{{\leq}}2\widetilde{\sigma}_{r_{1}+1}^{2}+8C_{5}m_{1}\omega_{\sf max}^{2}\log^{2}m.

We define

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}_{k}

\displaystyle:=\left\{r^{\prime}:\frac{\sigma_{r_{k-1}+1}\left(\bm{G}_{0}\right)}{\sigma_{r^{\prime}}\left(\bm{G}_{0}\right)}\leq 4\quad\text{and}\quad\sigma_{r^{\prime}}\left(\bm{G}_{0}\right)-\sigma_{r^{\prime}+1}\left(\bm{G}_{0}\right)\geq\frac{1}{r}\sigma_{r^{\prime}}\left(\bm{G}_{0}\right)\right\},

(200)

Choosing the numbers of iterations
\{t_{i}\}
as in (
41a
) and (
41b
) and repeating similar arguments as in (
175
), (
189a
) - (
189d
), (
192
), (
193
) and (
D.2
), we know that for all
1\leq k\leq k_{\sf max}
and
1\leq t\leq t_{k}
,

\displaystyle r_{k}

\displaystyle\in\mathcal{R}_{k}\cap\mathcal{A},

(201a)

\displaystyle\max\left\{\frac{\sigma_{r_{k-1}+1}^{\star}}{\sigma_{r_{k}}^{\star}},\frac{\widetilde{\sigma}_{r_{k-1}+1}}{\widetilde{\sigma}_{r_{k}}}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq 3~\quad~\text{and}~\quad~\min\left\{\frac{\widetilde{\sigma}_{r_{k}}^{2}-\widetilde{\sigma}_{r_{k}+1}^{2}}{\widetilde{\sigma}_{r_{k}}^{2}},\frac{\sigma_{r_{k}}^{\star 2}-\sigma_{r_{k}+1}^{\star 2}}{\sigma_{r_{k}}^{\star 2}}\right\}\geq\frac{1}{4r},

(201b)

\displaystyle\lambda_{r_{k}}\left(\bm{M}^{\sf oracle}\right)-\lambda_{r_{k}+1}\left(\bm{M}^{\sf oracle}\right)

\displaystyle\asymp\widetilde{\sigma}_{r_{k}}^{2}-\widetilde{\sigma}_{r_{k}+1}^{2}\asymp\sigma_{r_{k}}^{\star 2}-\sigma_{r_{k}+1}^{\star 2}\gg\left\|\bm{Z}\right\|,

(201c)

\displaystyle F_{k}^{t}-40\sqrt{\frac{\mu r^{3}}{m_{1}}}\left\|\bm{Z}\right\|-20\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{m_{1}}}\widetilde{\sigma}_{r_{k}+1}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{e^{t}}\left(F_{k}^{0}-40\sqrt{\frac{\mu r^{3}}{m_{1}}}\left\|\bm{Z}\right\|-20\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{m_{1}}}\widetilde{\sigma}_{r_{k}+1}^{2}\right),

(201d)

\displaystyle D_{k}^{t}

\displaystyle\leq F_{k}^{t}+6C_{3}\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{m_{1}}}\sqrt{m}_{1}\omega_{\sf max}\log m\cdot\sigma_{\overline{r}+1}^{\star}+C_{3}^{2}\mu r\omega_{\sf max}^{2}\log^{2}m,

(201e)

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{U}_{k}^{t}\bm{U}_{k}^{t\top}-\bm{U}_{k}^{\sf oracle}\bm{U}_{k}^{\sf oracle\top}\right\|
