\displaystyle\leq 2\frac{D_{k}^{t}}{\lambda_{r_{k}}\left(\bm{M}^{\sf oracle}\right)-\lambda_{r_{k}+1}\left(\bm{M}^{\sf oracle}\right)}\leq\frac{1}{8},

(201f)

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{U}_{k}^{t}\right\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\bm{U}_{k}^{t}\bm{U}_{k}^{t\top}-\bm{U}_{k}^{\sf oracle}\bm{U}_{k}^{\sf oracle\top}\right\|+\left\|\bm{U}_{k}^{\sf oracle}\right\|_{2,\infty}\leq\frac{1}{4e}.

(201g)

\displaystyle F_{k+1}^{0}=F_{k}^{t_{k}}\leq 41C_{2}\sqrt{\frac{\mu r^{3}}{m_{1}}}\big(\sqrt{m_{1}}\omega_{\sf max}

\displaystyle\log m\cdot\sigma_{\overline{r}+1}^{\star}+\left(\sqrt{m_{1}m_{2}}+m_{1}\right)\omega_{\sf max}^{2}\log^{2}m\big)+21\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{m_{1}}}\widetilde{\sigma}_{r_{k}+1}^{2}.

(201h)

Taking
k=k_{\sf max}
in (
201h
) yields that

\displaystyle F_{k_{\sf max}}^{t_{k_{\sf max}}}

\displaystyle\leq 41C_{2}\sqrt{\frac{\mu r^{3}}{m_{1}}}\big(\sqrt{m_{1}}\omega_{\sf max}\log m\cdot\sigma_{\overline{r}+1}^{\star}+\left(\sqrt{m_{1}m_{2}}+m_{1}\right)\omega_{\sf max}^{2}\log^{2}m\big)+21\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{m_{1}}}\widetilde{\sigma}_{r_{k_{\sf max}}+1}^{2}.

(202)

This together with (
201e
) implies that

\displaystyle D_{k_{\sf max}}^{t_{k_{\sf max}}}\leq 42C_{2}\sqrt{\frac{\mu r^{3}}{m_{1}}}\big(\sqrt{m_{1}}\omega_{\sf max}\log m\cdot\sigma_{\overline{r}+1}^{\star}+\left(\sqrt{m_{1}m_{2}}+m_{1}\right)\omega_{\sf max}^{2}\log^{2}m\big)+21\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{m_{1}}}\widetilde{\sigma}_{r_{k_{\sf max}}+1}^{2}.

(203)

Recall that
r_{k_{\sf max}}
satisfies
r_{k_{\sf max}}=r
or
\sigma_{r_{k_{\sf max}}+1}(\bm{G}_{k_{\sf max}})=\sigma_{r_{k_{\sf max}}+1}(\bm{G}_{k_{\sf max}}^{t_{k_{\sf max}}})\leq\tau
.

1.

If
r_{k_{\sf max}}=r
, then it follows that

\widetilde{\sigma}_{r_{k_{\sf max}}+1}^{2}=\widetilde{\sigma}_{r+1}^{2}=0.

2.

If
\sigma_{r_{k_{\sf max}}+1}(\bm{G}_{k_{\sf max}})\leq\tau
, then Weyl’s inequality and (
202
) together show that

\displaystyle\widetilde{\sigma}_{r_{k_{\sf max}}+1}^{2}

\displaystyle=\sigma_{r_{k_{\sf max}}+1}\big(\widetilde{\bm{M}}\big)\leq\sigma_{r_{k_{\sf max}}+1}\left(\bm{G}_{k_{\sf max}}^{t_{k_{\sf max}}}\right)+\left\|\widetilde{\bm{M}}-\bm{G}_{k_{\sf max}}^{t_{k_{\sf max}}}\right\|

\displaystyle=\sigma_{r_{k_{\sf max}}+1}\left(\bm{G}_{k_{\sf max}}^{t_{k_{\sf max}}}\right)+F_{k_{\sf max}}^{t_{k_{\sf max}}}

\displaystyle\leq\tau+41C_{2}\sqrt{\frac{\mu r^{3}}{m_{1}}}\big(\sqrt{m_{1}}\omega_{\sf max}\log m\cdot\sigma_{\overline{r}+1}^{\star}+\left(\sqrt{m_{1}m_{2}}+m_{1}\right)\omega_{\sf max}^{2}\log^{2}m\big)+21\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{m_{1}}}\widetilde{\sigma}_{r_{k_{\sf max}}+1}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\tau+41C_{2}\sqrt{\frac{\mu r^{3}}{m_{1}}}\big(\sqrt{m_{1}}\omega_{\sf max}\log m\cdot\sigma_{\overline{r}+1}^{\star}+\left(\sqrt{m_{1}m_{2}}+m_{1}\right)\omega_{\sf max}^{2}\log^{2}m\big)+\frac{1}{2}\widetilde{\sigma}_{r_{k_{\sf max}}+1}^{2},

which further gives

\displaystyle\widetilde{\sigma}_{r_{k_{\sf max}}+1}^{2}\leq 2\tau+82C_{2}\sqrt{\frac{\mu r^{3}}{m_{1}}}\big(\sqrt{m_{1}}\omega_{\sf max}\log m\cdot\sigma_{\overline{r}+1}^{\star}+\left(\sqrt{m_{1}m_{2}}+m_{1}\right)\omega_{\sf max}^{2}\log^{2}m\big)\leq 3\tau.

(204)

Therefore, inequality (
204
) is guaranteed to hold.

Step 4: proving (
42a
).

We know from (
201a
) that
r_{k_{\sf max}}\in\mathcal{A}
. Also, (
39b
) tell us that

\displaystyle\big\|\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}^{\sf oracle}\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}^{\sf oracle\top}-\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}^{\star\top}\big\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{\mu r^{3}}{m_{1}}}\left(\frac{r^{2}\sqrt{m_{1}}\omega_{\sf max}\log m}{\sigma_{r_{k_{\sf max}}}^{\star}}+\frac{r^{2}\sqrt{m_{1}m_{2}}\omega_{\sf max}^{2}\log^{2}m}{\sigma_{r_{k_{\sf max}}}^{\star 2}}\right)\leq\sqrt{\frac{\mu r^{3}}{m_{1}}}.

In view of (
203
), (
201b
), (
201c
) and (
201g
), we can demonstrate that

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}^{\top}-\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}^{\sf oracle}\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}^{{\sf oracle}\top}\right\|

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim\frac{D_{k_{\sf max}}^{t_{k_{\sf max}}}}{\lambda_{r_{k_{\sf max}}}\left(\bm{M}^{\sf oracle}\right)-\lambda_{r_{k_{\sf max}}+1}\left(\bm{M}^{\sf oracle}\right)}

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim\frac{\sqrt{\frac{\mu r^{3}}{m_{1}}}\big(\sqrt{m_{1}}\omega_{\sf max}\log m\cdot\sigma_{\overline{r}+1}^{\star}+\left(\sqrt{m_{1}m_{2}}+m_{1}\right)\omega_{\sf max}^{2}\log^{2}m\big)}{\sigma_{r_{k_{\sf max}}}^{\star 2}-\sigma_{r_{k_{\sf max}}+1}^{\star 2}}+\frac{\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{m_{1}}}\widetilde{\sigma}_{r_{k_{\sf max}}+1}^{2}}{\widetilde{\sigma}_{r_{k_{\sf max}}}^{2}-\widetilde{\sigma}_{r_{k_{\sf max}}+1}^{2}}

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim\frac{\sqrt{\frac{\mu r^{3}}{m_{1}}}r\big(\sqrt{m_{1}}\omega_{\sf max}\log m\cdot\sigma_{\overline{r}+1}^{\star}+\left(\sqrt{m_{1}m_{2}}+m_{1}\right)\omega_{\sf max}^{2}\log^{2}m\big)}{\sigma_{r_{k_{\sf max}}}^{\star 2}}+\frac{\sqrt{\frac{\mu r^{3}}{m_{1}}}\widetilde{\sigma}_{r_{k_{\sf max}}+1}^{2}}{\widetilde{\sigma}_{r_{k_{\sf max}}}^{2}}

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{\mu r^{3}}{m_{1}}}.

(205)

Here, the last inequality holds since
\widetilde{\sigma}_{r_{k_{\sf max}}}\geq\widetilde{\sigma}_{r_{k_{\sf max}}+1}
and
\sigma_{r_{k_{\sf max}}}^{\star}\geq\sigma_{\overline{r}}^{\star}\geq C_{0}r\big[(m_{1}m_{2})^{1/4}+rm_{1}^{1/2}\big]\omega_{\sf max}\log m
.
Combining the previous two inequalities yields

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}^{\top}-\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}^{\star\top}\right\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\big\|\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}^{\sf oracle}\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}^{\sf oracle\top}-\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}^{\star\top}\big\|_{2,\infty}+\left\|\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}^{\top}-\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}^{\sf oracle}\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}^{{\sf oracle}\top}\right\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{\mu r^{3}}{m_{1}}},

(206)

which validates (
42a
).

Step 5: proving (
42b
) and (
42c
).

Note that

\displaystyle\big\|\big(\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}^{\top}-\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}^{\star\top}\big)\bm{X}^{\star}\big\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\quad=\big\|\big(\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}^{\top}-\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}^{\star\top}\big)\big(\bm{U}^{\star(1)}\bm{\Sigma}^{\star(1)}\bm{V}^{\star(1)\top}+\bm{U}^{\star(2)}\bm{\Sigma}^{\star(2)}\bm{V}^{\star(2)\top}\big)\big\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\quad=\big\|\big(\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}^{\top}-\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}^{\star\top}\big)\bm{U}^{\star(1)}\bm{\Sigma}^{\star(1)}\bm{V}^{\star(1)\top}\big\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\qquad+\big\|\big(\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}^{\top}-\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}^{\star\top}\big)\bm{U}^{\star(2)}\bm{\Sigma}^{\star(2)}\bm{V}^{\star(2)\top}\big\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\quad\leq\underbrace{\big\|\big(\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}^{\top}-\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{k_{\sf max}}\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{k_{\sf max}}^{\top}\big)\bm{U}^{\star(1)}\bm{\Sigma}^{\star(1)}\bm{V}^{\star(1)\top}\big\|_{2,\infty}}_{=:\alpha_{1}}

\displaystyle\qquad+\underbrace{\big\|\big(\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{k_{\sf max}}\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{k_{\sf max}}^{\top}-\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}^{\star\top}\big)\bm{U}^{\star(1)}\bm{\Sigma}^{\star(1)}\bm{V}^{\star(1)\top}\big\|_{2,\infty}}_{=:\alpha_{2}}

\displaystyle\qquad+\underbrace{\big\|\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}^{\top}-\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}^{\star\top}\big\|_{2,\infty}\sigma_{\overline{r}+1}^{\star}}_{=:\alpha_{3}},

(207)

where the last line holds due to
\|\bm{U}^{\star(2)}\bm{\Sigma}^{\star(2)}\bm{V}^{\star(2)\top}\|=\|\bm{\Sigma}^{\star(2)}\|=\sigma_{\overline{r}+1}^{\star}
. To control
\big\|\big(\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}^{\top}-\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}^{\star\top}\big)\bm{X}^{\star}\big\|_{2,\infty}
, one only needs to bound
\alpha_{1}
,
\alpha_{2}
and
\alpha_{3}
, respectively.

Step 5.1: bounding
\alpha_{1}
.

We first bound
\alpha_{1}=\|(\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}^{\top}-\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{k_{\sf max}}\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{k_{\sf max}}^{\top})\bm{U}^{\star(1)}\bm{\Sigma}^{\star(1)}\bm{V}^{\star(1)\top}\|_{2,\infty}
.

Step 5.1.1: bounding
\|(\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}^{\top}-\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}^{\sf oracle}\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}^{{\sf oracle}\top})\widetilde{\bm{M}}\|_{2,\infty}
.

By virtue of Weyl’s inequality and (
107
), one has

\displaystyle\lambda_{r_{k_{\sf max}}}\left(\bm{M}^{\sf oracle}\right)\leq\widetilde{\sigma}_{r_{k_{\sf max}}}^{2}+\left\|\bm{Z}\right\|\asymp\widetilde{\sigma}_{r_{k_{\sf max}}}^{2}\asymp\sigma_{r_{k_{\sf max}}}^{\star 2}.

(208)

Recognizing that
\bm{G}_{k_{\sf max}}^{t_{k_{\sf max}}}=\bm{M}^{\sf oracle}+(\bm{G}_{k_{\sf max}}^{t_{k_{\sf max}}}-\bm{M}^{\sf oracle})
and
\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}
(resp.
\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}^{\sf oracle}
) is the rank-
r_{k_{\sf max}}
leading singular subspace of
\bm{G}_{k_{\sf max}}^{t_{k_{\sf max}}}
(resp.
\bm{M}^{\sf oracle}
), we invoke Lemma
6
to yield

\displaystyle\left\|\left(\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}^{\top}-\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}^{\sf oracle}\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}^{{\sf oracle}\top}\right)\bm{M}^{\sf oracle}\right\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\quad\leq\left\|\left(\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}^{\top}-\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}^{\sf oracle}\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}^{{\sf oracle}\top}\right)\bm{M}^{\sf oracle}\right\|

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim\frac{\lambda_{r_{k_{\sf max}}}\left(\bm{M}^{\sf oracle}\right)}{\lambda_{r_{k_{\sf max}}}\left(\bm{M}^{\sf oracle}\right)-\lambda_{r_{k_{\sf max}}+1}\left(\bm{M}^{\sf oracle}\right)}\big\|\bm{G}_{k_{\sf max}}^{t_{k_{\sf max}}}-\bm{M}^{\sf oracle}\big\|

\displaystyle\quad\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\eqref{ineq:induction_general_3}~\text{and}~\eqref{ineq145}}}{{\lesssim}}\frac{\sigma_{r_{k_{\sf max}}}^{\star 2}}{\sigma_{r_{k_{\sf max}}}^{\star 2}-\sigma_{r_{k_{\sf max}}+1}^{\star 2}}D_{k_{\sf max}}^{t_{k_{\sf max}}}

\displaystyle\quad\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\eqref{ineq:induction_general_2}~\text{and}~\eqref{ineq136}}}{{\lesssim}}r\left[\sqrt{\frac{\mu r^{3}}{m_{1}}}\big(\sqrt{m_{1}}\omega_{\sf max}\log m\cdot\sigma_{\overline{r}+1}^{\star}+\left(\sqrt{m_{1}m_{2}}+m_{1}\right)\omega_{\sf max}^{2}\log^{2}m\big)+\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{m_{1}}}\widetilde{\sigma}_{r_{k_{\sf max}}+1}^{2}\right].

(209)

Combining (
107
), (
D.2
) and (
D.2
) leads to

\displaystyle\left\|\left(\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}^{\top}-\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}^{\sf oracle}\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}^{{\sf oracle}\top}\right)\widetilde{\bm{M}}\right\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\quad\leq\left\|\left(\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}^{\top}-\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}^{\sf oracle}\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}^{{\sf oracle}\top}\right)\bm{M}^{\sf oracle}\right\|_{2,\infty}+\left\|\left(\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}^{\top}-\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}^{\sf oracle}\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}^{{\sf oracle}\top}\right)\bm{Z}\right\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\quad\leq\left\|\left(\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}^{\top}-\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}^{\sf oracle}\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}^{{\sf oracle}\top}\right)\bm{M}^{\sf oracle}\right\|_{2,\infty}+\left\|\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}^{\top}-\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}^{\sf oracle}\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}^{{\sf oracle}\top}\right\|_{2,\infty}\left\|\bm{Z}\right\|

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim r\left[\sqrt{\frac{\mu r^{3}}{m_{1}}}\left(\sqrt{m_{1}}\omega_{\sf max}\log m\cdot\sigma_{\overline{r}+1}^{\star}+\left(\sqrt{m_{1}m_{2}}+m_{1}\right)\omega_{\sf max}^{2}\log^{2}m\right)+\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{m_{1}}}\widetilde{\sigma}_{r_{k_{\sf max}}+1}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad+\sqrt{\frac{\mu r^{3}}{m_{1}}}\left(\sqrt{m_{1}}\omega_{\sf max}\log m\cdot\sigma_{\overline{r}+1}^{\star}+\left(\sqrt{m_{1}m_{2}}+m_{1}\right)\omega_{\sf max}^{2}\log^{2}m\right)

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{\mu r^{5}}{m_{1}}}\left(\sqrt{m_{1}}\omega_{\sf max}\log m\cdot\sigma_{\overline{r}+1}^{\star}+\left(\sqrt{m_{1}m_{2}}+m_{1}\right)\omega_{\sf max}^{2}\log^{2}m\right)+\sqrt{\frac{\mu r^{3}}{m_{1}}}\widetilde{\sigma}_{r_{k_{\sf max}}+1}^{2}.

(210)

Step 5.1.2: bounding
\|(\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}^{\top}-\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{k_{\sf max}}\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{k_{\sf max}}^{\top})\widetilde{\bm{M}}\|_{2,\infty}
.

Recalling that (
107
) holds, we can invoke Lemma
1
to obtain

\displaystyle\big\|\big(\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}^{\sf oracle}\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}^{{\sf oracle}\top}-\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{k_{\sf max}}\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{k_{\sf max}}^{\top}\big)\widetilde{\bm{M}}\big\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\quad\leq\frac{40}{\pi}\widetilde{\sigma}_{r_{k_{\sf max}}}^{2}\sum_{k\geq 1}\frac{2^{k}}{\big(\widetilde{\sigma}_{r_{k_{\sf max}}}^{2}-\widetilde{\sigma}_{r_{k_{\sf max}}+1}^{2}\big)^{k}}\sum_{0\leq j_{1},\dots,j_{k+1}\leq r\atop\left(j_{1},...,j_{k+1}\right)^{\top}\neq\bm{0}_{k+1}}\big\|\widetilde{\bm{P}}_{j_{1}}\bm{Z}\widetilde{\bm{P}}_{j_{2}}\bm{Z}\cdots\bm{Z}\widetilde{\bm{P}}_{j_{k+1}}\big\|_{2,\infty}.

(211)

Here, we recall that
\widetilde{\bm{P}}_{j}=\widetilde{\bm{u}}_{j}\widetilde{\bm{u}}_{j}^{\top}
for
1\leq j\leq r
and
\widetilde{\bm{P}}_{0}=\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{\perp}\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{\perp}^{\top}
. Repeat similar arguments as in (
C.2
) to deduce that

\displaystyle\big\|\big(\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}^{\sf oracle}\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}^{{\sf oracle}\top}-\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{k_{\sf max}}\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{k_{\sf max}}^{\top}\big)\widetilde{\bm{M}}\big\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{\mu r^{3}}{m_{1}}}\frac{r\left(\sqrt{m_{1}}\omega_{\sf max}\log m\cdot\sigma_{\overline{r}+1}^{\star}+\left(\sqrt{m_{1}m_{2}}+m_{1}\right)\omega_{\sf max}^{2}\log^{2}m\right)}{\sigma_{r_{k_{\sf max}}}^{\star 2}-\sigma_{r_{k_{\sf max}}+1}^{\star 2}}\cdot\widetilde{\sigma}_{r_{k_{\sf max}}}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\eqref{ineq:induction_general_2}~\text{and}~\eqref{ineq115}}}{{\lesssim}}\sqrt{\frac{\mu r^{3}}{m_{1}}}\frac{r\left(\sqrt{m_{1}}\omega_{\sf max}\log m\cdot\sigma_{\overline{r}+1}^{\star}+\left(\sqrt{m_{1}m_{2}}+m_{1}\right)\omega_{\sf max}^{2}\log^{2}m\right)}{\sigma_{r_{k_{\sf max}}}^{\star 2}/r}\cdot\sigma_{r_{k_{\sf max}}}^{\star 2}

\displaystyle\quad=\sqrt{\frac{\mu r^{3}}{m_{1}}}r^{2}\left(\sqrt{m_{1}}\omega_{\sf max}\log m\cdot\sigma_{\overline{r}+1}^{\star}+\left(\sqrt{m_{1}m_{2}}+m_{1}\right)\omega_{\sf max}^{2}\log^{2}m\right).

(212)

Inequality (
D.2
) taken together with (
D.2
) and the triangle inequality shows that

\displaystyle\big\|\big(\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}^{\top}-\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{k_{\sf max}}\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{k_{\sf max}}^{\top}\big)\widetilde{\bm{M}}\big\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{\mu r^{3}}{m_{1}}}r^{2}\left(\sqrt{m_{1}}\omega_{\sf max}\log m\cdot\sigma_{\overline{r}+1}^{\star}+\left(\sqrt{m_{1}m_{2}}+m_{1}\right)\omega_{\sf max}^{2}\log^{2}m\right)+\sqrt{\frac{\mu r^{3}}{m_{1}}}\widetilde{\sigma}_{r_{k_{\sf max}}+1}^{2}.

(213)

Step 5.1.3: bounding
\alpha_{1}
.

Equipped with (
D.2
), we are now ready to bound
\alpha_{1}
. Recall that

\displaystyle\widetilde{\bm{M}}=\widetilde{\bm{U}}^{(1)}\big(\widetilde{\bm{\Sigma}}^{(1)}\big)^{2}\widetilde{\bm{U}}^{(1)\top}+\widetilde{\bm{U}}^{(2)}\big(\widetilde{\bm{\Sigma}}^{(2)}\big)^{2}\widetilde{\bm{U}}^{(2)\top},

where
\widetilde{\bm{U}}^{(1)}
and
\widetilde{\bm{\Sigma}}^{(1)}
(resp.
\widetilde{\bm{U}}^{(2)}
and
\widetilde{\bm{\Sigma}}^{(2)}
) are defined in (
82
) (resp. (
95
)).
In view of (
D.2
) and (
D.2
), one can obtain

\displaystyle\big\|\big(\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}^{\top}-\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{k_{\sf max}}\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{k_{\sf max}}^{\top}\big)\widetilde{\bm{U}}^{(1)}\widetilde{\bm{\Sigma}}^{(1)}\big\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\quad\leq\left\|\big(\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}^{\top}-\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{k_{\sf max}}\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{k_{\sf max}}^{\top}\big)\widetilde{\bm{U}}^{(1)}\big(\widetilde{\bm{\Sigma}}^{(1)}\big)^{2}\widetilde{\bm{U}}^{(1)\top}\right\|_{2,\infty}\left\|\big(\widetilde{\bm{\Sigma}}^{(1)}\big)^{-1}\right\|

\displaystyle\quad\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(i)}}{{\lesssim}}\left(\big\|\big(\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}^{\top}-\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{k_{\sf max}}\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{k_{\sf max}}^{\top}\big)\widetilde{\bm{M}}\big\|_{2,\infty}+\left\|\big(\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}^{\top}-\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{k_{\sf max}}\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{k_{\sf max}}^{\top}\big)\widetilde{\bm{U}}^{(2)}\big(\widetilde{\bm{\Sigma}}^{(2)}\big)^{2}\widetilde{\bm{U}}^{(2)\top}\right\|_{2,\infty}\right)\frac{1}{\sigma_{\overline{r}}^{\star}}

\displaystyle\quad\leq\left(\big\|\big(\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}^{\top}-\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{k_{\sf max}}\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{k_{\sf max}}^{\top}\big)\widetilde{\bm{M}}\big\|_{2,\infty}+\left\|\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}^{\top}-\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{k_{\sf max}}\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{k_{\sf max}}^{\top}\right\|_{2,\infty}\big\|\widetilde{\bm{\Sigma}}^{(2)}\big\|^{2}\right)\frac{1}{\sigma_{\overline{r}}^{\star}}

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim\Bigg(\sqrt{\frac{\mu r^{3}}{m_{1}}}r^{2}\left(\sqrt{m_{1}}\omega_{\sf max}\log m\cdot\sigma_{\overline{r}+1}^{\star}+\left(\sqrt{m_{1}m_{2}}+m_{1}\right)\omega_{\sf max}^{2}\log^{2}m\right)+\sqrt{\frac{\mu r^{3}}{m_{1}}}\widetilde{\sigma}_{r_{k_{\sf max}}+1}^{2}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+\sqrt{\frac{\mu r^{3}}{m_{1}}}\left(\frac{r^{2}\sqrt{m_{1}}\omega_{\sf max}\log m}{\sigma_{r_{k_{\sf max}}}^{\star}}+\frac{r^{2}\sqrt{m_{1}m_{2}}\omega_{\sf max}^{2}\log^{2}m}{\sigma_{r_{k_{\sf max}}}^{\star 2}}\right)\widetilde{\sigma}_{\overline{r}+1}^{2}\Bigg)\frac{1}{\sigma_{\overline{r}}^{\star}}

\displaystyle\quad\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(ii)}}{{\lesssim}}\Bigg(\sqrt{\frac{\mu r^{3}}{m_{1}}}r^{2}\left(\sqrt{m_{1}}\omega_{\sf max}\log m\cdot\sigma_{\overline{r}+1}^{\star}+\left(\sqrt{m_{1}m_{2}}+m_{1}\right)\omega_{\sf max}^{2}\log^{2}m\right)+\sqrt{\frac{\mu r^{3}}{m_{1}}}\widetilde{\sigma}_{r_{k_{\sf max}}+1}^{2}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+\sqrt{\frac{\mu r^{3}}{m_{1}}}\left(\frac{r^{2}\sqrt{m_{1}}\omega_{\sf max}\log m}{\sigma_{r_{k_{\sf max}}}^{\star}}+\frac{r^{2}\sqrt{m_{1}m_{2}}\omega_{\sf max}^{2}\log^{2}m}{\sigma_{r_{k_{\sf max}}}^{\star 2}}\right)\sigma_{\overline{r}}^{\star 2}\Bigg)\frac{1}{\sigma_{\overline{r}}^{\star}}

\displaystyle\quad\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(iii)}}{{\lesssim}}\sqrt{\frac{\mu r^{3}}{m_{1}}}r^{2}\left(\sqrt{m_{1}}\omega_{\sf max}\log m+\frac{\left(\sqrt{m_{1}m_{2}}+m_{1}\right)\omega_{\sf max}^{2}\log^{2}m}{\sigma_{\overline{r}}^{\star}}\right)+\sqrt{\frac{\mu r^{3}}{m_{1}}}\frac{\widetilde{\sigma}_{r_{k_{\sf max}}+1}^{2}}{\sigma_{\overline{r}}^{\star}}

\displaystyle\quad\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(iv)}}{{\lesssim}}\sqrt{\frac{\mu r^{3}}{m_{1}}}\left(r^{2}\sqrt{m_{1}}\omega_{\sf max}\log m+r(m_{1}m_{2})^{1/4}\omega_{\sf max}\log m\right)+\sqrt{\frac{\mu r^{3}}{m_{1}}}\frac{\tau}{\sigma_{\overline{r}}^{\star}}

\displaystyle\quad\asymp\sqrt{\frac{\mu r^{3}}{m_{1}}}\left(r^{2}\sqrt{m_{1}}\omega_{\sf max}\log m+r(m_{1}m_{2})^{1/4}\omega_{\sf max}\log m\right),

(214)

where
(i)
,
(ii)
and
(iii)
are consequences of
\widetilde{\sigma}_{r_{k_{\sf max}}}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\eqref{ineq115}}}{{\asymp}}\sigma_{r_{k_{\sf max}}}^{\star}\geq\sigma_{\overline{r}}^{\star}\asymp\widetilde{\sigma}_{\overline{r}}\gtrsim\max\{\sigma_{\overline{r}+1}^{\star},\widetilde{\sigma}_{\overline{r}+1}\}
, and
(iv)
makes use of the fact
\sigma_{\overline{r}}^{\star}\geq C_{0}r[(m_{1}m_{2})^{1/4}+rm_{1}^{1/2}]\omega_{\sf max}\log m
and (
204
). Note that
\widetilde{\bm{U}}^{(1)}\widetilde{\bm{\Sigma}}^{(1)}\widetilde{\bm{W}}^{(1)\top}
is the SVD of
\bm{U}^{\star(1)}\bm{\Sigma}^{\star(1)}+\bm{E}\bm{V}^{\star(1)}
. By virtue of the previous inequality, (
90a
), Theorem
5
and the triangle inequality, we arrive at

\displaystyle\alpha_{1}

\displaystyle=\big\|\big(\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}^{\top}-\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{k_{\sf max}}\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{k_{\sf max}}^{\top}\big)\bm{U}^{\star(1)}\bm{\Sigma}^{\star(1)}\bm{V}^{\star(1)\top}\big\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle=\big\|\big(\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}^{\top}-\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{k_{\sf max}}\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{k_{\sf max}}^{\top}\big)\bm{U}^{\star(1)}\bm{\Sigma}^{\star(1)}\big\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\big\|\big(\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}^{\top}-\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{k_{\sf max}}\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{k_{\sf max}}^{\top}\big)\widetilde{\bm{U}}^{(1)}\widetilde{\bm{\Sigma}}^{(1)}\widetilde{\bm{W}}^{\top}\big\|_{2,\infty}+\big\|\big(\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}^{\top}-\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{k_{\sf max}}\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{k_{\sf max}}^{\top}\big)\bm{E}\bm{V}^{\star(1)}\big\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\big\|\big(\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}^{\top}-\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{k_{\sf max}}\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{k_{\sf max}}^{\top}\big)\widetilde{\bm{U}}^{(1)}\widetilde{\bm{\Sigma}}^{(1)}\big\|_{2,\infty}+\big\|\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}^{\top}-\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{k_{\sf max}}\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{k_{\sf max}}^{\top}\big\|_{2,\infty}\big\|\bm{E}\bm{V}^{\star(1)}\big\|

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\frac{\mu r^{3}}{m_{1}}}\left(r^{2}\sqrt{m_{1}}\omega_{\sf max}\log m+r(m_{1}m_{2})^{1/4}\omega_{\sf max}\log m\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\sqrt{\frac{\mu r^{3}}{m_{1}}}\left(\frac{r^{2}\sqrt{m_{1}}\omega_{\sf max}\log m}{\sigma_{r_{k_{\sf max}}}^{\star}}+\frac{r^{2}\sqrt{m_{1}m_{2}}\omega_{\sf max}^{2}\log^{2}m}{\sigma_{r_{k_{\sf max}}}^{\star 2}}\right)\cdot\sqrt{m_{1}}\omega_{\sf max}\log m

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\frac{\mu r^{3}}{m_{1}}}\left(r^{2}\sqrt{m_{1}}\omega_{\sf max}\log m+r(m_{1}m_{2})^{1/4}\omega_{\sf max}\log m\right)+\sqrt{\frac{\mu r^{3}}{m_{1}}}\sqrt{m_{1}}\omega_{\sf max}\log m

\displaystyle\asymp\sqrt{\frac{\mu r^{3}}{m_{1}}}\left(r^{2}\sqrt{m_{1}}\omega_{\sf max}\log m+r(m_{1}m_{2})^{1/4}\omega_{\sf max}\log m\right),

(215)

where the penultimate line follows since
\sigma_{\overline{r}}^{\star}\geq C_{0}r[(m_{1}m_{2})^{1/4}+rm_{1}^{1/2}]\omega_{\sf max}\log m
.

Step 5.2: bounding
\alpha_{2}
.

In view of (
C.2
), we have

\displaystyle\mathcal{P}_{\left(\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{k_{\sf max}}\right)_{\perp}}\mathcal{P}_{\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}^{\star}}\bm{U}^{\star(1)}\bm{\Sigma}^{\star(1)}\bm{V}^{\star(1)\top}

\displaystyle\quad=\left(\mathcal{P}_{\left(\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{k_{\sf max}}\right)_{\perp}}\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}^{\star}\right)\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}^{\star\top}\bm{U}^{\star(1)}\bm{\Sigma}^{\star(1)}\bm{V}^{\star(1)\top}

\displaystyle\quad=\Big[\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{:,r_{k_{\sf max}}+1:\overline{r}}^{(1)}\widetilde{\bm{\Sigma}}_{r_{k_{\sf max}}+1:\overline{r},r_{k_{\sf max}}+1:\overline{r}}^{(1)}\widetilde{\bm{W}}_{:,r_{k_{\sf max}}+1:\overline{r}}^{(1)\top}\left(\bm{I}_{r_{k_{\sf max}}}\ \bm{0}\right)^{\top}\left(\bm{\Sigma}^{\star}_{1:r_{k_{\sf max}},1:r_{k_{\sf max}}}\right)^{-1}

\displaystyle\qquad-\mathcal{P}_{\left(\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{:,1:r_{k_{\sf max}}}\right)_{\perp}}\bm{E}\bm{V}^{\star(1)}\left(\bm{I}_{r_{k_{\sf max}}}\ \bm{0}\right)^{\top}\left(\bm{\Sigma}^{\star}_{1:r_{k_{\sf max}},1:r_{k_{\sf max}}}\right)^{-1}\Big]

\displaystyle\qquad\cdot\bm{\Sigma}^{\star}_{1:r_{k_{\sf max}},1:r_{k_{\sf max}}}\bm{V}_{:,1:r_{k_{\sf max}}}^{\star\top}

\displaystyle\quad=\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{:,r_{k_{\sf max}}+1:\overline{r}}^{(1)}\widetilde{\bm{\Sigma}}_{r_{k_{\sf max}}+1:\overline{r},r_{k_{\sf max}}+1:\overline{r}}^{(1)}\widetilde{\bm{W}}_{:,r_{k_{\sf max}}+1:\overline{r}}^{(1)\top}\left(\bm{I}_{r_{k_{\sf max}}}\ \bm{0}\right)^{\top}\bm{V}_{:,1:r_{k_{\sf max}}}^{\star\top}

\displaystyle\qquad-\mathcal{P}_{\left(\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{:,1:r_{k_{\sf max}}}\right)_{\perp}}\bm{E}\bm{V}^{\star(1)}\left(\bm{I}_{r_{k_{\sf max}}}\ \bm{0}\right)^{\top}\bm{V}_{:,1:r_{k_{\sf max}}}^{\star\top}.

(216)

Here, the third line holds since
r_{k_{\sf max}}\leq\overline{r}
and

\displaystyle\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}^{\star\top}\bm{U}^{\star(1)}\bm{\Sigma}^{\star(1)}\bm{V}^{\star(1)\top}

\displaystyle=\bm{U}_{:,1:r_{k_{\sf max}}}^{\star\top}\bm{U}_{:,1:\overline{r}}^{\star}\bm{\Sigma}_{1:\overline{r},1:\overline{r}}^{\star}\bm{V}_{:,1:\overline{r}}^{\star\top}

\displaystyle=\bm{U}_{:,1:r_{k_{\sf max}}}^{\star\top}\left(\bm{U}_{:,1:r_{k_{\sf max}}}^{\star}\bm{\Sigma}_{1:r_{k_{\sf max}},1:r_{k_{\sf max}}}^{\star}\bm{V}_{:,1:r_{k_{\sf max}}}^{\star\top}+\bm{U}_{:,r_{k_{\sf max}}+1:\overline{r}}^{\star}\bm{\Sigma}_{r_{k_{\sf max}}+1:\overline{r},r_{k_{\sf max}}+1:\overline{r}}^{\star}\bm{V}_{:,r_{k_{\sf max}}+1:\overline{r}}^{\star\top}\right)

\displaystyle=\bm{\Sigma}_{1:r_{k_{\sf max}},1:r_{k_{\sf max}}}^{\star}\bm{V}_{:,1:r_{k_{\sf max}}}^{\star\top}.

(217)

Repeating similar arguments as in (
C.2
) and (
C.2
), one has

\displaystyle\left\|\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{:,r_{k_{\sf max}}+1:\overline{r}}^{(1)}\widetilde{\bm{\Sigma}}_{r_{k_{\sf max}}+1:\overline{r},r_{k_{\sf max}}+1:\overline{r}}^{(1)}\widetilde{\bm{W}}_{:,r_{k_{\sf max}}+1:\overline{r}}^{(1)\top}\left(\bm{I}_{r_{k_{\sf max}}}\ \bm{0}\right)^{\top}\bm{V}_{:,1:r_{k_{\sf max}}}^{\star\top}\right\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{m_{1}}}r\sqrt{m}_{1}\omega_{\sf max}\log m,

(218a)

\displaystyle\left\|\mathcal{P}_{\left(\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{:,1:r_{k_{\sf max}}}\right)_{\perp}}\bm{E}\bm{V}^{\star(1)}\left(\bm{I}_{r_{k_{\sf max}}}\ \bm{0}\right)^{\top}\bm{V}_{:,1:r_{k_{\sf max}}}^{\star\top}\right\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{m_{1}}}\sqrt{m}_{1}\omega_{\sf max}\log m.

(218b)

Combining (
D.2
), (
218a
), (
218b
) and the triangle inequality yields

\displaystyle\left\|\mathcal{P}_{\left(\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{k_{\sf max}}\right)_{\perp}}\mathcal{P}_{\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}^{\star}}\bm{U}^{\star(1)}\bm{\Sigma}^{\star(1)}\bm{V}^{\star(1)\top}\right\|_{2,\infty}\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{\mu r^{3}}{m_{1}}}\sqrt{m}_{1}\omega_{\sf max}\log m.

(219)

Then we can bound
\alpha_{2}
as follows:

\displaystyle\alpha_{2}

\displaystyle=\left\|\big(\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{k_{\sf max}}\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{k_{\sf max}}^{\top}-\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}^{\star\top}\big)\bm{U}^{\star(1)}\bm{\Sigma}^{\star(1)}\bm{V}^{\star(1)\top}\right\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\big(\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{k_{\sf max}}\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{k_{\sf max}}^{\top}-\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}^{\star\top}\big)\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}^{\star\top}\bm{U}^{\star(1)}\bm{\Sigma}^{\star(1)}\bm{V}^{\star(1)\top}\right\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\quad+\left\|\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{k_{\sf max}}\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{k_{\sf max}}^{\top}\left(\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}^{\star}\right)_{\perp}\left(\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}^{\star}\right)_{\perp}^{\star\top}\bm{U}^{\star(1)}\bm{\Sigma}^{\star(1)}\bm{V}^{\star(1)\top}\right\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle=\left\|\mathcal{P}_{\left(\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{k_{\sf max}}\right)_{\perp}}\mathcal{P}_{\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}^{\star}}\bm{U}^{\star(1)}\bm{\Sigma}^{\star(1)}\bm{V}^{\star(1)\top}\right\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\quad+\left\|\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{k_{\sf max}}\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{k_{\sf max}}^{\top}\left(\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}^{\star}\right)_{\perp}\left(\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}^{\star}\right)_{\perp}^{\star\top}\bm{U}_{:,r_{k_{\sf max}}+1:\overline{r}}^{\star}\bm{\Sigma}_{r_{k_{\sf max}}+1:\overline{r},r_{k_{\sf max}}+1:\overline{r}}^{\star}\bm{V}_{:,r_{k_{\sf max}}+1:\overline{r}}^{\star\top}\right\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\eqref{ineq151}}}{{\lesssim}}\sqrt{\frac{\mu r^{3}}{m_{1}}}\sqrt{m}_{1}\omega_{\sf max}\log m+\big\|\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{k_{\sf max}}\big\|_{2,\infty}\big\|\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{k_{\sf max}}^{\top}\left(\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}^{\star}\right)_{\perp}\big\|\big\|\bm{\Sigma}_{r_{k_{\sf max}}+1:\overline{r},r_{k_{\sf max}}+1:\overline{r}}^{\star}\big\|

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\eqref{ineq2d}~\text{and}~\eqref{ineq109b}}}{{\lesssim}}\sqrt{\frac{\mu r^{3}}{m_{1}}}\sqrt{m}_{1}\omega_{\sf max}\log m+\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{m_{1}}}\frac{r\sqrt{m}_{1}\omega_{\sf max}\log m}{\sigma_{r_{k_{\sf max}}}^{\star}}\sigma_{r_{k_{\sf max}}+1}^{\star}

\displaystyle\asymp\sqrt{\frac{\mu r^{3}}{m_{1}}}\sqrt{m}_{1}\omega_{\sf max}\log m.

(220)

Step 5.3: bounding
\alpha_{3}
.

By virtue of (
78
) and (
D.2
), one has

\displaystyle\alpha_{3}

\displaystyle=\big\|\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}^{\top}-\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}^{\star\top}\big\|_{2,\infty}\sigma_{\overline{r}+1}^{\star}\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{\mu r^{3}}{m_{1}}}\left(r\left[(m_{1}m_{2})^{1/4}+rm_{1}^{1/2}\right]\omega_{\sf max}\log m\right).

(221)

Step 5.4: bounding
\|(\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}^{\top}-\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}^{\star\top})\bm{X}^{\star}\|_{2,\infty}
and
\|(\bm{I}_{n_{1}}-\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}^{\top})\bm{X}^{\star}\|_{2,\infty}
.

Putting (
D.2
), (
D.2
), (
D.2
) and (
221
) together, we arrive at

\displaystyle\big\|\big(\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}^{\top}-\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}^{\star\top}\big)\bm{X}^{\star}\big\|_{2,\infty}\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{\mu r^{3}}{m_{1}}}\left(r^{2}\sqrt{m_{1}}\omega_{\sf max}\log m+r(m_{1}m_{2})^{1/4}\omega_{\sf max}\log m\right).

(222)

Furthermore, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\left(\bm{I}_{m_{1}}-\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}^{\star\top}\right)\bm{X}^{\star}\right\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle=\left\|\bm{U}^{\star}\bm{\Sigma}^{\star}\bm{V}^{\star\top}-\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}^{\star}\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}^{\star\top}\bm{U}^{\star}\bm{\Sigma}^{\star}\bm{V}^{\star\top}\right\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle=\left\|\bm{U}^{\star}\bm{\Sigma}^{\star}\bm{V}^{\star\top}-\bm{U}_{:,1:r_{k_{\sf max}}}^{\star}\bm{\Sigma}_{1:r_{k_{\sf max}},1:r_{k_{\sf max}}}^{\star}\bm{V}_{:,1:r_{k_{\sf max}}}^{\star\top}\right\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle=\left\|\bm{U}_{:,r_{k_{\sf max}}+1:r}^{\star}\bm{\Sigma}_{r_{k_{\sf max}}+1:r,r_{k_{\sf max}}+1:r}^{\star}\bm{V}_{:,r_{k_{\sf max}}+1:r}^{\star\top}\right\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\bm{U}^{\star}\right\|_{2,\infty}\left\|\bm{\Sigma}_{r_{k_{\sf max}}+1:r,r_{k_{\sf max}}+1:r}^{\star}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{m_{1}}}\sigma_{r_{k_{\sf max}}+1}^{\star}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\eqref{ineq2a}~\text{and}~\eqref{ineq128}}}{{\leq}}\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{m_{1}}}\big(\widetilde{\sigma}_{r_{k_{\sf max}}+1}+2\sqrt{C_{5}}\sqrt{m}_{1}\omega_{\sf max}\log m\big)

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\eqref{ineq135}}}{{\lesssim}}\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{m_{1}}}\left(\sqrt{\tau}+\sqrt{m}_{1}\omega_{\sf max}\log m\right)

\displaystyle\asymp\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{m_{1}}}\left(r^{2}\sqrt{m_{1}}\omega_{\sf max}\log m+r(m_{1}m_{2})^{1/4}\omega_{\sf max}\log m\right).

This together with (
222
) gives

\displaystyle\|(\bm{I}_{n_{1}}-\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}\bm{U}_{k_{\sf max}}^{\top})\bm{X}^{\star}\|_{2,\infty}\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{\mu r^{3}}{m_{1}}}\left(r^{2}\sqrt{m_{1}}\omega_{\sf max}\log m+r(m_{1}m_{2})^{1/4}\omega_{\sf max}\log m\right).

(223)

Appendix E
Proof of Theorem
2

For notational convenience, we let
\bm{U}_{i}^{\star}=\bm{U}_{\bm{X}_{i}^{\star}}\in\mathcal{O}^{n_{i},k_{i}}
denote the left singular subspace of
\bm{X}_{i}^{\star}
for all
i\in[3]
. Then we know from (
45
) that

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{U}_{i}^{\star}\right\|_{2,\infty}\leq\sqrt{\frac{k_{i}}{\beta n_{i}}},~\quad~\forall i\in[3].

(224)

In view of
Zhou and Chen, 2023
, with probability exceeding
1-O(n^{-10})
,

\displaystyle\left\|\mathcal{P}_{\sf off\text{-}diag}\left(\bm{E}_{1}\bm{E}_{1}^{\top}\right)\right\|\lesssim B^{2}\log^{2}n+\sqrt{n_{1}n_{2}n_{3}}\omega_{\sf max}^{2}\log n\leq\sqrt{n_{1}n_{2}n_{3}}\omega_{\sf max}^{2}\log n\ll n_{2}n_{3}\omega_{\sf max}^{2}.

(225)

For any
i\in[n_{1}]
, we know that

\displaystyle\left(\bm{E}_{1}\bm{E}_{1}^{\top}\right)_{i,i}=\sum_{j=1}^{n_{2}}\sum_{\ell=1}^{n_{3}}E_{i,j,\ell}^{2}=\sum_{j=1}^{n_{2}}\sum_{\ell=1}^{n_{3}}\omega_{i,j,\ell}^{2}+\sum_{j=1}^{n_{2}}\sum_{\ell=1}^{n_{3}}\left(E_{i,j,\ell}^{2}-\omega_{i,j,\ell}^{2}\right).

(226)

If the noise is bounded, i.e.,
E_{i,j,k}\leq B
for all
(i,j,k)\in[n_{1}]\times[n_{2}]\times[n_{3}]
, then
\{E_{i,j,\ell}^{2}-\omega_{i,j,\ell}^{2}\}_{i,j,\ell}
are zero-mean, and

\displaystyle\left|E_{i,j,\ell}^{2}-\omega_{i,j,\ell}^{2}\right|

\displaystyle\leq 2B^{2},

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left(E_{i,j,\ell}^{2}-\omega_{i,j,\ell}^{2}\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[E_{i,j,\ell}^{4}\right]-\omega_{i,j,\ell}^{4}\leq B^{2}\mathbb{E}\left[E_{i,j,\ell}^{2}\right]-\omega_{i,j,\ell}^{4}\leq B^{2}\omega_{\sf max}^{2}.

In view of Bernstein’s inequality and the union bound, one has, with probability exceeding
1-O(n^{-10})
, for all
i\in[n_{1}]
,

\displaystyle\left|\sum_{j=1}^{n_{2}}\sum_{\ell=1}^{n_{3}}\left(E_{i,j,\ell}^{2}-\omega_{i,j,\ell}^{2}\right)\right|\lesssim\sqrt{n_{2}n_{3}}B\omega_{\sf max}\sqrt{\log n}+B^{2}\log n\ll n_{2}n_{3}\omega_{\sf max}^{2}.

(228)

For the general case where the noise satisfies Assumption
1
, using the truncation trick as in
Zhou and Chen, 2023
, one can show that (
228
) also holds with probability exceeding
1-O(n^{-10})
. Putting (
225
), (
226
) and (
228
) together, we know that with probability exceeding
1-O(n^{-10})
,

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{E}_{1}\bm{E}_{1}^{\top}-{\sf diag}\left(\Bigg[\sum_{j=1}^{n_{2}}\sum_{\ell=1}^{n_{3}}\omega_{i,j,\ell}^{2}\Bigg]_{1\leq i\leq n_{1}}\right)\right\|\ll n_{2}n_{3}\omega_{\sf max}^{2}.

(229)

For all
i\in[3]
, let
\bm{U}_{i}^{\star}\in\mathcal{O}^{n_{i},k_{i}}
denote the left singular subspace of
\bm{X}_{i}^{\star}
. The min-max principle for singular values reveals that

\displaystyle\sigma_{k_{1}+1}\left(\bm{Y}_{1}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\sigma_{k_{1}+1}\left(\bm{Y}_{1}\left(\mathcal{P}_{\bm{U}_{3\perp}^{\star}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\bm{U}_{2\perp}^{\star}}\right)\right)

\displaystyle=\sigma_{k_{1}+1}\left(\bm{X}_{1}^{\star}\left(\mathcal{P}_{\bm{U}_{3\perp}^{\star}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\bm{U}_{2\perp}^{\star}}\right)+\bm{E}_{1}\left(\mathcal{P}_{\bm{U}_{3\perp}^{\star}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\bm{U}_{2\perp}^{\star}}\right)\right)

\displaystyle=\sigma_{k_{1}+1}\left(\bm{E}_{1}\left(\mathcal{P}_{\bm{U}_{3\perp}^{\star}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\bm{U}_{2\perp}^{\star}}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\geq\sigma_{k_{1}+1}\left(\bm{E}_{1}\right)-\left\|\bm{E}_{1}\left(\mathcal{P}_{\bm{U}_{3}^{\star}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\bm{U}_{2}^{\star}}\right)\right\|-\left\|\bm{E}_{1}\left(\mathcal{P}_{\bm{U}_{3\perp}^{\star}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\bm{U}_{2}^{\star}}\right)\right\|-\left\|\bm{E}_{1}\left(\mathcal{P}_{\bm{U}_{3}^{\star}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\bm{U}_{2\perp}^{\star}}\right)\right\|

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\sigma_{k_{1}+1}\left(\bm{E}_{1}\bm{E}_{1}^{\top}\right)}-\left\|\bm{E}_{1}\left(\bm{U}_{3}^{\star}\otimes\bm{U}_{2}^{\star}\right)\right\|-\left\|\bm{E}_{1}\left(\bm{U}_{3\perp}^{\star}\otimes\bm{U}_{2}^{\star}\right)\right\|-\left\|\bm{E}_{1}\left(\bm{U}_{3}^{\star}\otimes\bm{U}_{2\perp}^{\star}\right)\right\|,

(230)

where the fourth line makes use of Weyl’s inequality. We know from (
224
) that

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{U}_{3}^{\star}\otimes\bm{U}_{2}^{\star}\right\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\frac{k_{2}k_{3}}{\beta^{2}n_{2}n_{3}}},

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{U}_{3\perp}^{\star}\otimes\bm{U}_{2}^{\star}\right\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\|\bm{U}_{2}^{\star}\|_{2,\infty}\leq\sqrt{\frac{k_{2}}{\beta n_{2}}},

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{U}_{3}^{\star}\otimes\bm{U}_{2\perp}^{\star}\right\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\|\bm{U}_{3}^{\star}\|_{2,\infty}\leq\sqrt{\frac{k_{3}}{\beta n_{3}}}.

Applying
Zhou and Chen, 2023
yields that with probability at least
1-O(n^{-10})
,

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{E}_{1}\left(\bm{U}_{3}^{\star}\otimes\bm{U}_{2}^{\star}\right)\right\|\lesssim B\sqrt{\frac{k_{2}k_{3}}{\beta^{2}n_{2}n_{3}}}\log n+\sqrt{n_{1}}\omega_{\sf max}\sqrt{\log n}\ll\sqrt{n_{2}n_{3}}\omega_{\sf max},

(231a)

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{E}_{1}\left(\bm{U}_{3\perp}^{\star}\otimes\bm{U}_{2}^{\star}\right)\right\|\lesssim B\sqrt{\frac{k_{2}}{\beta n_{2}}}\log n+\sqrt{n_{1}+k_{2}n_{3}}\omega_{\sf max}\sqrt{\log n}\ll\sqrt{n_{2}n_{3}}\omega_{\sf max},

(231b)

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{E}_{1}\left(\bm{U}_{3}^{\star}\otimes\bm{U}_{2\perp}^{\star}\right)\right\|\lesssim B\sqrt{\frac{k_{3}}{\beta n_{3}}}\log n+\sqrt{n_{1}+k_{3}n_{2}}\omega_{\sf max}\sqrt{\log n}\ll\sqrt{n_{2}n_{3}}\omega_{\sf max}.

(231c)

Combining (
229
), (
231a
) - (
231c
) and Weyl’s inequality, we obtain that with probability exceeding
1-O(n^{-10})
,

\displaystyle\sigma_{k_{1}+1}\left(\bm{Y}_{1}\right)\leq\sigma_{k_{1}+1}\left(\bm{X}_{1}^{\star}\right)+\left\|\bm{E}_{1}\right\|=\left\|\bm{E}_{1}\right\|=\left(\left\|\bm{E}_{1}\bm{E}_{1}^{\top}\right\|\right)^{1/2}\leq 2\sqrt{n_{2}n_{3}}\omega_{\sf max}.

(232)

1.

If (
24
) holds, then (
229
), (
E
) and (
231a
) - (
231c
) together show that with probability exceeding
1-O(n^{-10})
,

\displaystyle\sigma_{k_{1}+1}\left(\bm{Y}_{1}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\sqrt{cn_{2}n_{3}\omega_{\sf max}^{2}}-\frac{\sqrt{c}}{2}\sqrt{n_{2}n_{3}}\omega_{\sf max}\geq\frac{\sqrt{c}}{2}\sqrt{n_{2}n_{3}}\omega_{\sf max}.

(233)

The previous inequality together with (
232
) shows that

\displaystyle\sigma_{k_{1}+1}\left(\bm{Y}_{1}\right)\asymp\sqrt{n_{2}n_{3}}\omega_{\sf max}

with probability at least
1-O(n^{-10})
. As a consequence, there exist two large enough constants
C_{\tau}>c_{\tau}>0
such that with probability at least
1-O(n^{-10})
,

\displaystyle c_{\tau}\left(n_{1}n_{2}n_{3}\right)^{1/2}\log^{2}n\leq\tau/\omega_{\sf max}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C_{\tau}\left(n_{1}n_{2}n_{3}\right)^{1/2}\log^{2}n.

(234)

2.

If Assumption 2 in Theorem
2
holds, we choose
(\ell_{1},\ell_{2},\ell_{3})\in[k_{1}]\times[k_{2}]\times[k_{3}]
such that

\displaystyle(\ell_{1},\ell_{2},\ell_{3})\in\mathop{\rm arg\max}_{i_{1}\in[k_{1}],i_{2}\in[k_{2}],i_{3}\in[k_{3}]}S^{\star}_{i_{1},i_{2},i_{3}}\left(1-S^{\star}_{i_{1},i_{2},i_{3}}\right)=\omega_{\sf max}^{2}.

Then for any
(j_{1},j_{2},j_{3})\in[n_{1}]\times[n_{2}]\times[n_{3}]
with
z_{i,j_{i}}^{\star}=\ell_{i}
,
i\in[3]
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[E_{j_{1},j_{2},j_{3}}^{2}\right]=\omega_{\sf max}^{2}.

For any
i\in\{j\in[n_{1}]:z_{1,j}^{\star}=\ell_{1}\}
,

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{n_{2}}\sum_{\ell=1}^{n_{3}}\omega_{i,j,\ell}^{2}\geq\omega_{\sf max}^{2}\left|j\in[n_{2}]:z_{2,j}^{\star}=\ell_{2}\right|\cdot\left|j\in[n_{3}]:z_{3,j}^{\star}=\ell_{3}\right|\geq\frac{\beta n_{2}}{k_{2}}\frac{\beta n_{3}}{k_{3}}\omega_{\sf max}^{2}\asymp n_{2}n_{3}\omega_{\sf max}^{2}.

Since
|\{j\in[n_{1}]:z_{1,j}^{\star}=\ell_{1}\}|\geq\beta n_{1}/k_{1}>k_{1}+1
, we can still show that (
233
) holds with probability exceeding
1-O(n^{-10})
. Repeating similar arguments as in (
234
) shows that Theorem
2
also holds.

Appendix F
Technical lemmas

Lemma 6
.

Suppose that

\displaystyle\bm{M}=\bm{M}^{\star}+\bm{E}\in\mathbb{R}^{n_{1}\times n_{2}}

(235)

and the SVDs of
\bm{M}^{\star}
and
\bm{M}
are given by

\displaystyle\bm{M}^{\star}=\sum_{i=1}^{n_{1}}\sigma_{i}^{\star}\bm{u}_{i}^{\star}\bm{v}_{i}^{\star\top},~\quad~\text{and}~\quad~\bm{M}^{\star}=\sum_{i=1}^{n_{1}}\sigma_{i}\bm{u}_{i}\bm{v}_{i}^{\top}.

Here,
\sigma_{1}^{\star}\geq\dots\geq\sigma_{n_{1}}^{\star}\geq 0
(resp.
\sigma_{1}\geq\dots\geq\sigma_{n_{1}}\geq 0
) represent the singular values of
\bm{M}^{\star}
(resp.
\bm{M}
),
u_{i}^{\star}
(resp.
u_{i}
) denotes the left singular vector associated with the singular value
\sigma_{i}^{\star}
(resp.
\sigma_{i}
), and
v_{i}^{\star}
(resp.
v_{i}
) denotes the left singular vector associated with
\sigma_{i}^{\star}
(resp.
\sigma_{i}
). We let
\bm{U}^{\star}=[u_{1}^{\star},\dots,u_{r}^{\star}]\in\mathbb{R}^{n_{1}\times r}
(resp.
\bm{U}=[u_{1},\dots,u_{r}]\in\mathbb{R}^{n_{1}\times r}
) denote the rank-
r
leading singular subspace of
\bm{M}^{\star}
(resp.
\bm{M}
).
If

\sigma_{r}^{\star}-\sigma_{r+1}^{\star}>2\|\bm{E}\|,

then we have

\displaystyle\left\|\left(\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}-\bm{U}^{\star}\bm{U}^{\star\top}\right)\bm{M}^{\star}\right\|\leq\frac{4\sigma_{r}^{\star}\left\|\bm{E}\right\|}{\sigma_{r}^{\star}-\sigma_{r+1}^{\star}}.

Proof.

We define

\displaystyle\bm{\Sigma}

\displaystyle:={\sf diag}\left(\sigma_{1},\dots,\sigma_{r}\right),~\qquad\qquad~\bm{\Sigma}_{\perp}:={\sf diag}\left(\sigma_{r+1},\dots,\sigma_{n_{1}}\right),

\displaystyle\bm{V}

\displaystyle:=\left[\bm{v}_{1},\dots,\bm{v}_{r}\right]\in\mathbb{R}^{n_{2}\times r},~\qquad~\bm{V}_{\perp}:=\left[\bm{v}_{r+1},\dots,\bm{v}_{n_{2}}\right]\in\mathbb{R}^{n_{2}\times(n_{2}-r)},

and define
\bm{\Sigma}^{\star},\bm{\Sigma}_{\perp}^{\star},\bm{V}^{\star},\bm{V}_{\perp}^{\star}
similarly.
In view of
Chen et al., 2021a
, we have

\displaystyle\mathcal{P}_{\bm{U}_{\perp}}\mathcal{P}_{\bm{U}^{\star}}\bm{M}^{\star}

\displaystyle=\bm{U}_{\perp}\left(\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}^{\star}\right)\left(\bm{U}^{\star\top}\bm{M}^{\star}\right)

\displaystyle=\bm{U}_{\perp}\left(\bm{\Sigma}_{\perp}\bm{V}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{V}^{\star}\bm{\Sigma}^{\star-1}-\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{E}\bm{V}^{\star}\bm{\Sigma}^{\star-1}\right)\bm{\Sigma}^{\star}\bm{V}^{\star\top}

\displaystyle=\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{\Sigma}_{\perp}\bm{V}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{V}^{\star}\bm{V}^{\star\top}-\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{E}\bm{V}^{\star}\bm{V}^{\star\top}.

Recognizing that

\displaystyle\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}-\bm{U}^{\star}\bm{U}^{\star\top}

\displaystyle=\left(\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{U}^{\star}-\bm{U}^{\star}\right)\bm{U}^{\star\top}+\left(\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}-\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{U}^{\star}\bm{U}^{\star\top}\right)

\displaystyle=-\mathcal{P}_{\bm{U}_{\perp}}\mathcal{P}_{\bm{U}^{\star}}+\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\star}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\star\top},

we have

\displaystyle\left\|\left(\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}-\bm{U}^{\star}\bm{U}^{\star\top}\right)\bm{M}^{\star}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\mathcal{P}_{\bm{U}_{\perp}}\mathcal{P}_{\bm{U}^{\star}}\bm{M}^{\star}\right\|+\left\|\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\star}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\star\top}\bm{M}^{\star}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{\Sigma}_{\perp}\bm{V}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{V}^{\star}\bm{V}^{\star\top}\right\|+\left\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{E}\bm{V}^{\star}\bm{V}^{\star\top}\right\|+\left\|\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\star}\bm{\Sigma}_{\perp}^{\star}\bm{V}_{\perp}^{\star\top}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\bm{\Sigma}_{\perp}\right\|\left\|\bm{V}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{V}^{\star}\right\|+\left\|\bm{E}\right\|+\left\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\star\top}\bm{U}\right\|\left\|\bm{\Sigma}_{\perp}^{\star}\right\|

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(a)}}{{\leq}}\sigma_{r+1}\frac{2\left\|\bm{E}\right\|}{\sigma_{r}^{\star}-\sigma_{r+1}^{\star}}+\left\|\bm{E}\right\|+\sigma_{r+1}^{\star}\frac{2\left\|\bm{E}\right\|}{\sigma_{r}^{\star}-\sigma_{r+1}^{\star}}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(b)}}{{\leq}}\left(\sigma_{r+1}^{\star}+\left\|\bm{E}\right\|\right)\frac{2\left\|\bm{E}\right\|}{\sigma_{r}^{\star}-\sigma_{r+1}^{\star}}+\left\|\bm{E}\right\|+\sigma_{r+1}^{\star}\frac{2\left\|\bm{E}\right\|}{\sigma_{r}^{\star}-\sigma_{r+1}^{\star}}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(c)}}{{\leq}}\frac{4\sigma_{r}^{\star}\left\|\bm{E}\right\|}{\sigma_{r}^{\star}-\sigma_{r+1}^{\star}}.

(236)

Here, (a) comes from
(
Chen et al., 2021a
, Eqn. (2.26a))
, (b) makes use of Weyl’s inequality, and (c) holds due to the assumption
\sigma_{r}^{\star}-\sigma_{r+1}^{\star}>2\|\bm{E}\|
.
∎

Lemma 7
.

Suppose that Assumption
1
holds. We let
\mathcal{A}
denote the following set:

\displaystyle\mathcal{U}=\left\{\left(\bm{U}_{1},\bm{U}_{2},\bm{U}_{3}\right):\bm{U}_{i}\in\mathbb{R}^{n_{i}\times r_{i}},\left\|\bm{U}_{i}\right\|\leq 1,\left\|\bm{U}_{i}\right\|_{2,\infty}\leq\sqrt{\frac{\mu_{i}r_{i}}{n_{i}}},i\in[3]\right\}

(237)

and we define

\displaystyle n=\max_{1\leq i\leq 3}n_{i},~\quad~\text{and}~\quad~r=\max_{1\leq i\leq 3}r_{i}.

If
n_{1}n_{2}n_{3}\geq r^{4}n^{2}
, then
with probability exceeding
1-O(n^{-10})
, the following inequality holds:

\displaystyle\sup_{\left(\bm{U}_{1},\bm{U}_{2},\bm{U}_{3}\right)\in\mathcal{U}}\left\|\bm{U}_{1}^{\top}\mathcal{M}_{1}\left(\bm{\mathcal{E}}\right)\left(\bm{U}_{3}\otimes\bm{U}_{2}\right)\right\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\omega_{\sf max}\sqrt{n\mu_{1}\mu_{2}\mu_{3}r^{3}\log n},

(238)

If, furthermore, the
E_{i,j,k}
’s are
\omega_{\sf max}
-sub-Gaussian, then with probability exceeding
1-e^{-Cn}
, one has

\displaystyle\sup_{\left(\bm{U}_{1},\bm{U}_{2},\bm{U}_{3}\right)\in\mathcal{U}}\left\|\bm{U}_{1}^{\top}\mathcal{M}_{1}\left(\bm{\mathcal{E}}\right)\left(\bm{U}_{3}\otimes\bm{U}_{2}\right)\right\|\leq\sqrt{nr}\omega_{\sf max}.

(239)

Proof of Lemma
7
.

(
239
) can be proved by simply combining
Zhou et al., 2022
(or Lemma 8.2 presented in its arxiv version) and the standard epsilon-net technique used in the proof of
Zhang and Xia, 2018
and we omit the details here for the sake of brevity.

Proving (
238
): the bounded noise case.

To prove (
238
), we first consider the bounded noise case, i.e.,
|E_{i,j,k}|\leq B
holds for all
(i,j,k)\in[n_{1}]\times[n_{2}]\times[n_{3}]
.
For any fixed
\left(\overline{\bm{U}}_{1},\overline{\bm{U}}_{2},\overline{\bm{U}}_{3}\right)\in\mathcal{U}
, note that

\displaystyle\overline{\bm{U}}_{1}^{\top}\mathcal{M}_{1}\left(\bm{\mathcal{E}}\right)\left(\overline{\bm{U}}_{3}\otimes\overline{\bm{U}}_{2}\right)=\sum_{i\in[n_{1}],j\in[n_{2}n_{3}]}\left(\mathcal{M}_{1}\left(\bm{\mathcal{E}}\right)\right)_{i,j}\left(\overline{\bm{U}}_{1}\right)_{i,:}^{\top}\left(\overline{\bm{U}}_{3}\otimes\overline{\bm{U}}_{2}\right)_{j,:}

is a sum of independent zero-mean matrices. In addition, we have

\displaystyle L

\displaystyle:=\max_{i\in[n_{1}],j\in[n_{2}n_{3}]}\left\|\left(\mathcal{M}_{1}\left(\bm{\mathcal{E}}\right)\right)_{i,j}\left(\overline{\bm{U}}_{1}\right)_{i,:}^{\top}\left(\overline{\bm{U}}_{3}\otimes\overline{\bm{U}}_{2}\right)_{j,:}\right\|\leq B\prod_{i=1}^{3}\left\|\overline{\bm{U}}_{i}\right\|_{2,\infty}\leq B\sqrt{\frac{\mu_{1}\mu_{2}\mu_{3}r_{1}r_{2}r_{3}}{n_{1}n_{2}n_{3}}},

\displaystyle V

\displaystyle:=\max\bigg\{\bigg\|\sum_{i\in[n_{1}],j\in[n_{2}n_{3}]}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\mathcal{M}_{1}\left(\bm{\mathcal{E}}\right)\right)_{i,j}^{2}\right]\left\|\left(\overline{\bm{U}}_{3}\otimes\overline{\bm{U}}_{2}\right)_{j,:}\right\|_{2}^{2}\left(\overline{\bm{U}}_{1}\right)_{i,:}^{\top}\left(\overline{\bm{U}}_{1}\right)_{i,:}\bigg\|,

\displaystyle\hskip 45.52458pt\bigg\|\sum_{i\in[n_{1}],j\in[n_{2}n_{3}]}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\mathcal{M}_{1}\left(\bm{\mathcal{E}}\right)\right)_{i,j}^{2}\right]\left\|\left(\overline{\bm{U}}_{1}\right)_{i,:}\right\|_{2}^{2}\left(\overline{\bm{U}}_{3}\otimes\overline{\bm{U}}_{2}\right)_{j,:}^{\top}\left(\overline{\bm{U}}_{3}\otimes\overline{\bm{U}}_{2}\right)_{j,:}\bigg\|\bigg\}

\displaystyle\leq\max\left\{\omega_{\sf max}^{2}\left\|\left(\overline{\bm{U}}_{3}\otimes\overline{\bm{U}}_{2}\right)\right\|_{\rm F}^{2}\left\|\overline{\bm{U}}_{1}\overline{\bm{U}}_{1}^{\top}\right\|,\omega_{\sf max}^{2}\left\|\overline{\bm{U}}_{1}\right\|_{\rm F}^{2}\left\|\left(\overline{\bm{U}}_{3}\otimes\overline{\bm{U}}_{2}\right)\left(\overline{\bm{U}}_{3}\otimes\overline{\bm{U}}_{2}\right)^{\top}\right\|\right\}

\displaystyle\lesssim\omega_{\sf max}^{2}\max\left\{r_{2}r_{3},r_{1}\right\}\leq\omega_{\sf max}^{2}r^{2}.

In view of the matrix Bernstein inequality, with probability exceeding
1-e^{-Cnr\log n}
,

\displaystyle\left\|\overline{\bm{U}}_{1}^{\top}\mathcal{M}_{1}\left(\bm{\mathcal{E}}\right)\left(\overline{\bm{U}}_{3}\otimes\overline{\bm{U}}_{2}\right)\right\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{Vnr\log n}+Lnr\log n

\displaystyle\lesssim\omega_{\sf max}\sqrt{nr^{3}\log n}+\omega_{\sf max}\frac{\left(n_{1}n_{2}n_{3}\right)^{1/4}}{\log n}\sqrt{\frac{\mu_{1}\mu_{2}\mu_{3}r_{1}r_{2}r_{3}}{n_{1}n_{2}n_{3}}}nr\log n

\displaystyle\leq\omega_{\sf max}\sqrt{n\mu_{1}\mu_{2}\mu_{3}r^{3}\log n}.

(240)

Here, the last line makes use of
n_{1}n_{2}n_{3}\geq r^{4}n^{2}
.

Repeating a similar argument as in
Vershynin, 2010
yields that: there exists a set
\mathcal{B}_{i}\subset\mathcal{D}_{i}=\{\bm{x}:\bm{x}\in\mathbb{R}^{r_{i}},\left\|\bm{x}\right\|_{2}\leq\sqrt{\mu_{i}r_{i}/n_{i}}\}
with cardinality at most
(1+8\sqrt{\mu_{i}r_{i}})^{r_{i}}
such that for any
\bm{x}\in\mathcal{D}_{i}
, one can find
\bm{x}^{\prime}\in\mathcal{B}_{i}
such that

\displaystyle\|\bm{x}-\bm{x}^{\prime}\|_{2}\leq\frac{1}{4}\sqrt{\frac{1}{n_{i}}}.

\|\bm{x}-\bm{x}^{\prime}\|_{2}\leq c\sqrt{1/n_{i}}
. As a direct consequence, for any
\bm{U}_{i}\in\mathcal{U}_{i}:=\left\{\bm{U}\in\mathbb{R}^{n_{i}\times r_{i}}:\left\|\bm{U}\right\|\leq 1,\left\|\bm{U}\right\|_{2,\infty}\leq\sqrt{\frac{\mu_{i}r_{i}}{n_{i}}}\right\}
, one can find
\bm{U}_{i}^{\prime}\in\mathcal{F}_{i}:=\{\bm{U}\in\mathbb{R}^{n_{i}\times r_{i}}:\bm{U}_{j,:}^{\top}\in\mathcal{B}_{i},\forall j\in[n_{i}]\}
such that

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{U}_{i}-\bm{U}_{i}^{\prime}\right\|_{2,\infty}\leq\frac{1}{4}\sqrt{\frac{1}{n_{i}}}.

Let
\mathcal{U}_{i}^{\prime}
denote the following set:

\displaystyle\mathcal{U}_{i}^{\prime}:=\left\{\bm{U}^{\prime}\in\mathbb{R}^{n_{i}\times r_{i}}:\bm{U}^{\prime}\in\mathcal{F}_{i},\inf_{\bm{U}\in\mathcal{U}_{i}}\left\|\bm{U}-\bm{U}^{\prime}\right\|_{2,\infty}\leq\frac{1}{4}\sqrt{\frac{1}{n_{i}}}\right\}.

(241)

Then we can verify the following three properties:

\displaystyle\left|\mathcal{U}_{i}^{\prime}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\left|\mathcal{F}_{i}\right|=\left|\mathcal{B}_{i}\right|^{n_{i}}\leq(1+8\sqrt{\mu_{i}r_{i}})^{n_{i}r_{i}}\leq n_{i}^{n_{i}r_{i}}\leq e^{n_{i}r_{i}\log n},

(242a)

\displaystyle\forall\bm{U}_{i}

\displaystyle\in\mathcal{U}_{i},~\quad~\exists\bm{U}_{i}^{\prime}\in\mathcal{U}_{i}^{\prime}~\quad~\text{s.t.}~\quad~\left\|\bm{U}_{i}-\bm{U}_{i}^{\prime}\right\|_{2,\infty}\leq\frac{1}{4}\sqrt{\frac{1}{n_{i}}}~\quad~\text{and}~\quad~\left\|\bm{U}_{i}-\bm{U}_{i}^{\prime}\right\|\leq\frac{1}{4},

(242b)

\displaystyle\forall\bm{U}_{i}^{\prime}

\displaystyle\in\mathcal{U}_{i}^{\prime},~\quad~\exists\bm{U}_{i}\in\mathcal{U}_{i}~\quad~\text{s.t.}~\quad~\left\|\bm{U}_{i}-\bm{U}_{i}^{\prime}\right\|_{2,\infty}\leq\frac{1}{4}\sqrt{\frac{1}{n_{i}}}~\quad~\text{and}~\quad~\left\|\bm{U}_{i}-\bm{U}_{i}^{\prime}\right\|\leq\frac{1}{4}.

(242c)

Here, the last two inequalities make use of
\|\bm{U}_{i}-\bm{U}_{i}^{\prime}\|\leq\sqrt{n_{i}}\|\bm{U}_{i}-\bm{U}_{i}^{\prime}\|_{2,\infty}
.

We define

A=\sup_{\left(\bm{U}_{1},\bm{U}_{2},\bm{U}_{3}\right)\in\mathcal{U}}\left\|\bm{U}_{1}^{\top}\mathcal{M}_{1}\left(\bm{\mathcal{E}}\right)\left(\bm{U}_{3}\otimes\bm{U}_{2}\right)\right\|~\quad~\text{and}~\quad~B=\sup_{\left(\bm{U}_{1}^{\prime},\bm{U}_{2}^{\prime},\bm{U}_{3}^{\prime}\right)\in\mathcal{U}_{1}^{\prime}\times\mathcal{U}_{2}^{\prime}\times\mathcal{U}_{3}^{\prime}}\left\|\bm{U}_{1}^{{}^{\prime}\top}\mathcal{M}_{1}\left(\bm{\mathcal{E}}\right)\left(\bm{U}_{3}^{\prime}\otimes\bm{U}_{2}^{\prime}\right)\right\|.

For any
(\bm{U}_{1},\bm{U}_{2},\bm{U}_{3})\in\mathcal{U}=\mathcal{U}_{1}\times\mathcal{U}_{2}\times\mathcal{U}_{3}
, we know from (
242b
) and (
242c
) that there exist
(\bm{U}_{1}^{\prime},\bm{U}_{2}^{\prime},\bm{U}_{3}^{\prime})\in\mathcal{U}_{1}^{\prime}\times\mathcal{U}_{2}^{\prime}\times\mathcal{U}_{3}^{\prime}
such that

\displaystyle\bm{U}_{i}-\bm{U}_{i}^{\prime}

\displaystyle\in\frac{1}{4}\mathcal{U}_{i},~\quad~i\in[3],

(243a)

\displaystyle\bm{U}_{i}^{\prime}

\displaystyle\in\frac{5}{4}\mathcal{U}_{i},~\quad~i\in[3].

(243b)

The triangle inequality, (
243a
) and (
243b
) together show that

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{U}_{1}^{\top}\mathcal{M}_{1}\left(\bm{\mathcal{E}}\right)\left(\bm{U}_{3}\otimes\bm{U}_{2}\right)\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\left(\bm{U}_{1}-\bm{U}_{1}^{\prime}\right)^{\top}\mathcal{M}_{1}\left(\bm{\mathcal{E}}\right)\left(\bm{U}_{3}\otimes\bm{U}_{2}\right)\right\|+\left\|\bm{U}_{1}^{{}^{\prime}\top}\mathcal{M}_{1}\left(\bm{\mathcal{E}}\right)\left(\bm{U}_{3}\otimes\left(\bm{U}_{2}-\bm{U}_{2}^{\prime}\right)\right)\right\|

\displaystyle\quad+\left\|\bm{U}_{1}^{{}^{\prime}\top}\mathcal{M}_{1}\left(\bm{\mathcal{E}}\right)\left(\left(\bm{U}_{3}-\bm{U}_{3}^{\prime}\right)\otimes\bm{U}_{2}^{\prime}\right)\right\|+\left\|\bm{U}_{1}^{{}^{\prime}\top}\mathcal{M}_{1}\left(\bm{\mathcal{E}}\right)\left(\bm{U}_{3}^{\prime}\otimes\bm{U}_{2}^{\prime}\right)\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{4}A+\frac{1}{4}\cdot\frac{5}{4}A+\frac{1}{4}\cdot\frac{5}{4}\cdot\frac{5}{4}A+B

\displaystyle\leq\frac{61}{64}A+B,

which implies

\displaystyle A\leq\frac{64}{3}B.

(244)

Furthermore, (
F
), (
242a
), (
243b
) and the union bound together imply that with probability exceeding
1-e^{-Cnr\log n}\cdot\prod_{i}e^{n_{i}r_{i}\log n}\geq 1-e^{-C^{\prime}nr\log n}
,

\displaystyle B\lesssim\left(\frac{5}{4}\right)^{3}\omega_{\sf max}\sqrt{n\mu_{1}\mu_{2}\mu_{3}r^{3}\log n}\asymp\omega_{\sf max}\sqrt{n\mu_{1}\mu_{2}\mu_{3}r^{3}\log n}.

(245)

Putting (
244
) and (
245
) together, we arrive at

\displaystyle\sup_{\left(\bm{U}_{1},\bm{U}_{2},\bm{U}_{3}\right)\in\mathcal{U}}\left\|\bm{U}_{1}^{\top}\mathcal{M}_{1}\left(\bm{\mathcal{E}}\right)\left(\bm{U}_{3}\otimes\bm{U}_{2}\right)\right\|=A\lesssim\omega_{\sf max}\sqrt{n\mu_{1}\mu_{2}\mu_{3}r^{3}\log n}

with probability exceeding
1-e^{-C^{\prime}nr\log n}
.

Proving (
238
): the general case.

For the general case where the noise matrix
\bm{E}
satisfies Assumption
2
, we can prove (
238
) by repeating a similar argument as in
Zhou and Chen, 2023
.
∎
